# Targum Jonathan on Leviticus

תרגום יונתן על ויקרא

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus)

תרגום מרכזי על ספרי הנביאים שנקרא בציבור בבית הכנסת בתקופת התלמוד, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות של יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the books of the Prophets, read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Targum Jonathan/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Targum Jonathan on Leviticus (Segments: 859, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): The Targum of Jonathan ben Uzziel, trans. J. W. Etheridge, London, 1862 (Segments: 858)


## Chapter 1

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:1)


ויקרא וַהֲוָה כֵּיוַן דְאַשְׁלִים משֶׁה לְמִיקְמָא יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא חָשִׁיב משֶׁה וְאַיְידִין בְּלִבֵּיהּ וַאֲמַר טַוְורָא דְסִינָי דַהֲוָה רִיבּוּיֵיהּ רִבּוּי דְשַׁעְתָּא וְקִדוּשֵׁיהּ קִדוּשׁ דִתְלַת יוֹמִין לָא הֲוָה אֶפְשַׁר לִי דְאִיסוּק לְוָתֵיהּ עַד זְמַן דַהֲוָה דִבּוּרָא מִתְמַלֵיל עִמִי בְּרַם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא הָדֵין דְרִבּוּיֵיהּ רִבּוּי לְעָלַם וְקִדוּשֵׁיהּ קִידוּשׁ לְעָלַם מִן דִינָא הֲוָה דְלָא אֵיעוּל לְגַוֵּיהּ עַד זְמַן דִי יִתְמַלֵיל עִמִי מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּבְכֵן קָרָא דִבּוּרָא דַייָ לְמשֶׁה וּמַלֵיל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ עִמֵיהּ מִן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמֵימָר


And it was when Mosheh had completed to erect the tabernacle that Mosheh reasoned and judged in his heart, and said: To Mount Sinai, whose excellency is the excellence only of an hour and its holiness the holiness but of three days, I could not ascend till the time that the word was spoken to me; but the excellence of this the tabernacle of ordinance is an eternal excellency, and its holiness an everlasting holiness; therefore is it right that I should not enter within it until the time that I am spoken with from before the Lord. Then did the word of the Lord call unto Mosheh and the Word of the Lord spake with him from the tabernacle of ordinance saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֵינַשׁ דִי יִקְרַב מִנְכוֹן וְלָא מִן מוּמְרַיָא פַּלְחֵי אֱלִילְיָא קָרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִן בְּעִירָא דַכְיָא מִן תּוֹרֵי וּמִן עָנָא וְלָא מִן חַיְתָא תְקַרְבוּן יַת קָרְבָּנֵיכוֹן


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: If a man of you,-but not of the rebellious worshippers of idols,-bring an oblation before the Lord, (it must be) from the clean cattle, from the oxen or from the sheep; but not from the wild beasts may you offer your oblations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:3)


אִין עֲלָתָא הוּא קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ מִן תּוֹרֵי דְּכַר שְׁלִים יְקַרְבִינֵיהּ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא יַקְרִיב יָתֵיהּ לְרַעֲוָא עֲלוֹי קֳדָם יְיָ


If his oblation be a burnt offering of oxen, he shall bring a male unblemished to the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, and offer him to be accepted for himself before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:4)


וְיִסְמוֹךְ בְּתוּקְפָא יַד יְמִינֵיהּ עַל רֵישׁ עֲלָתָא מְטוּל דְיִתְרְעֵי לֵיהּ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹי


And he shall lay his right hand with firmness upon the head of the sacrifice, that it may be acceptable from him to propitiate on his behalf.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:5)


וְיִכּוֹס טַבְּחָא בְּבֵית מַטְבְּחַיָא יַת בַּר תּוֹרֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִקְרְבוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא יַת אַדְמָא בְּמָנַיָא וְיִדְרְקוּן יַת אַדְמָא בְּמִזְרְקַיָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר דְבִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And the slayer shall kill the ox at the place of slaughter before the Lord, and the sons of Aharon the priest shall bring the blood in vessels, and sprinkle the blood which is in the basins round about the altar that is at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:6)


וְיַשְׁלַח יַת מַשְׁכָא מִן עֲלָתָא וְיִפְסַג יָתָהּ לְפִסְגָהָא


And he shall take away the skin from the sacrifice, and divide him according to his members.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:7)


וִינָתְנוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן אֵשָׁתָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא וִיסַדְרוּן קֵיסִין עַל אֵישָׁתָא


And the sons of Aharon the priest shall put fire upon the altar, and lay wood in order upon the fire;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:8)


וִיסַדְרוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן כַּהֲנַיָא יַת פַּסְגַיָא יַת רֵישָׁא וְיַת פְּרִיסוּתָא דְתַרְבָּא עַל קֵיסִין דְעַל אֵישָׁתָא דְעַל מַדְבְּחָא


and the priests the sons of Aharon shall lay the members in order and the heart and the covering of the fat upon the wood that is on the fire upon the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:9)


וּכְרִיסָא וְרִיגְלוֹי יְחַלֵיל בְּמַיָא וְיִסַק כַּהֲנָא יַת כּוֹלָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא הוּא קָרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּיל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And he shall wash the inwards and his legs with water; and the priest shall offer the whole upon the altar of burnt offering an oblation to be accepted with grace before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:10)


וְאִין מִן בְּנֵי עָנָא קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ מִן אִמְרַיָא אוֹ מִן בְּנֵי עִיזַיָא לַעֲלָתָא דְכַר שְׁלִים יְקַרְבִינֵיהּ


And if his oblation be of the flock, whether of the lambs or of the young goats, he shall bring a male unblemished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:11)


וְיִכּוֹס יָתֵיהּ טַבְּחָא עַל שִׁיפּוּלֵי מַדְבְּחָא בִּסְטַר צִיפּוּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִדְרְקוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן כַּהֲנַיָא יַת אַדְמֵיהּ בְּמִזְרְקַיָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And the slayer shall kill it at the foot of the altar on the north side, before the Lord, and the priests the sons of Aharon shall sprinkle the blood that is in the basins upon the altar round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:12)


וּפַיְיסִיג יָתֵיהּ לְפַסְגוֹי וְיַת רֵישֵׁיהּ וְיַת גוּפֵיהּ וִיסַדֵר כַּהֲנָא יַתְהוֹן עַל קֵיסִין דְעַל אֵישָׁתָא דְעַל מַדְבְּחָא


And he shall divide it by its members, its head and its body, and the priest shall set them in order on the wood which is upon the fire on the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:13)


וּכְרֵיסָא וְרִיגְלוֹי יְחַלֵיל בְּמַיָא וִיקָרֵיב כַּהֲנָא יַת כּוֹלָא וְיַסֵיק לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא הוּא קוּרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And the inwards and his legs he shall wash with water, and the priest shall offer the whole and burn it at the altar of burnt sacrifice; it is an offering to be received with grace before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:14)


וְאִם מִן עוֹפָא עֲלָתָא קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וִיקָרֵב מִן שַׁפְנִינַיָא אוֹ מִן בְּנֵי יוֹנַיָא יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ בְּרַם שַׁפְנִינַיָא יַקְרִיב מִן רַבְרְבִין וּבְנֵי יוֹנַיָא מִן גוֹזְלִין


And if his oblation before the Lord be of birds he shall bring his oblation from the turtle doves or the young Of pigeons; but of the turtle doves he shall bring the largest, and of the pigeons the young ones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:15)


וִיקַרְבִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא לְמַדְבְּחָא וְיַחֲזוֹם יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ וְיַסֵיק לְמַדְבְּחָא וְיִתְמְצֵי אַדְמֵיהּ עַל כּוֹתֶל מַדְבְּחָא


And the priest shall offer it upon the altar, and shall wring off its head, and burn upon the altar, and press out its blood at the side of the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:16)


וְיַעֲדֵי יַת זְרוֹקְפֵיהּ בִּלְקָטֵיהּ וְיִטְלַק יָתָהּ לִסְטַר מַדְבְּחָא קִידוּמָא בַּאֲתַר דְמוֹקְדִין קִיטְמָא


And he shall remove its gullet and the contents thereof, and throw it by the eastern side of the altar in the place where they burn the cinders.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/1:17)


וְיִתְלַע יָתֵיהּ בְּגַדְפוֹי לָא יַפְרִישׁ גַדְפוֹי מִנֵיהּ וְיַסִיק יָתֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עַל קֵיסִין דְעַל אֵישָׁתָא עֲלָתָא הוּא קָרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֲדָם יְיָ


And he shall cut it between its wings, but not to sever the wings from it; and the priest shall burn it at the altar upon the wood which is on the fire: it is a sacrifice, an oblation to be received with favour before the Lord.



## Chapter 2

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:1)


וּבַר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יִקְרַב קָרְבַּן מִנְחָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ קִמְחָא סְמִידָא יְהֵי קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ וְיָרִיק עֲלָהּ מִשְׁחָא וְיִתֵּן עֲלָהּ לְבוֹנְתָּא


But when a man will offer the oblation of mincha before the Lord, his oblation shall be of flour, and he shall pour oil upon it, and put incense thereon,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:2)


וְיַיְתִינָהּ לְוַת בְּנֵי אַהֲרן כַּהֲנַיָא וְיִקְמוֹץ מִתַּמָן מְלֵי קוּמְצֵיהּ מִן קִמְחָא סְמִידָא וּמִן טוּב מִשְׁחָא עַל כָּל לְבוֹנְתָּא וְיַסִיק כַּהֲנָא יַת שְׁפַר אַדְכַּרְתָּא לְמַדְבְּחָא קָרְבָּנָא דְמִתְקַבֵּיל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


and bring it to the priests the sons of Aharon; and he shall take from thence his hand full of the meal and of the best of the oil, with all the frankincense; and the priest shall burn the goodly memorial at the altar, an oblation to be accepted with grace before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:3)


וּמַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר מִן מִנְחָתָא יְהֵא לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין מִקוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ


And what remaineth of the mincha shall be Aharon's and his sons, most holy among the oblations of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:4)


וַאֲרוּם תַּקְרִיב קָרְבַּן מִנְחָתָא דְמִתְאַפֵי בְּתַנוּרָא סְמִידָא גְרִיצִין פַּטִירִין פְּתִיכַן בִּמְשָׁח וּסְפוּגִין פַּטִירִין דִמְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשָׁח


And when thou wilt offer the oblation of a mincha of that which is baked in the oven, it shall be cakes of flour, unleavened and mixed with oil, and wafers unleavened, which are anointed with oil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:5)

And if thy oblation of a mincha be from the pan, it shall be of flour mingled with oil, unleavened shall it be.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:6)

He shall break it in pieces, and pour oil thereupon. It is a mincha.

And if thy oblation be a mincha from the gridiron, it shall be made of flour broiled with oil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:8)


וּתְהַנְעֵל יַת מִנְחָתָא דְתִתְעֲבֵד מִן סְמִידָא וּמִשְׁחָא הָאִלֵין לְקָדָם יְיָ וִיקַרְבִינֵהּ גַבְרָא דְמַיְיתִי יָתָהּ לְוַת כַּהֲנָא וְכַהֲנָא יַמְטִינֵיהּ לְמַדְבְּחָא


And the mincha which hath been made with the flour and the oil thou shalt bring in before the Lord, and the man who bringeth it shall present it to the priest, and the priest shall take it to the altar.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:9)


וְיַפְרֵישׁ כַּהֲנָא מִן מִנְחָתָא יַת שְׁבַח אַדְכַּרְתָּהּ וְיַסֵיק לְמַדְבְּחָא קוּרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And the priest shall separate from the mincha a memorial of praise, and burn it at the altar, an oblation to be accepted with grace before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:10)


וּמַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר מִן מִנְחָתָא יְהֵי לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין מִקָרְבְּנַיָא דַיְיָ


And what remaineth of the mincha shall be for Aharon and his sons, it is most holy among the oblations of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:11)


כָּל מִנְחָתָא דִי תְקַרְבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ לָא תִתְעֲבֵיד חָמִיעַ אֲרוּם כָּל חֲמִיר וְכָל דְבַשׁ לָא תְקַרְבוּן מִינֵיהּ קָרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ


But no mincha which thou offerest to the Lord shalt thou make with leaven; for neither leaven nor honey mayest thou offer as an oblation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:12)


קָרְבַּן שֵׁירוּייָא תְּקַרְבוּן יַתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ דִלְחֵים בִּיכּוּרַיָא חֲמִיר מִתְקָרֵיב וְתַמְרֵי בִּזְמַן בִּכּוּרֵי אִבָּא עִם דוּבְשֵׁיהּ מִתְקָרֵיב וְיֵיכְלוּן יַתְהוֹן כַּהֲנַיָא בְּרַם לְמַדְבְּחָא לָא יִתַּסְקוּן לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא


When thou offerest an oblation of first fruits before the Lord, the bread of the first fruits thou mayest bring leavened, and the dates in the season of first fruits, and the fruit with its honey thou mayest bring, and the priest may eat them; but they shall not burn them at the altar as an oblation to be received with favour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:13)


וְכָל קוּרְבַּן מִנְחָתָךְ בְּמִלְחָא תִמְלָח וְלָא תִבַטֵל מְלַח קְיַים אֳלָהָךְ מֵעִילַוֵי מִנְחָתָךְ מְטוֹל דְעֶשְרִין וְאַרְבְּעָא מוֹהֲבָתָא דְכַהֲנַיָא אִתְגְזָרוּ בִּקְיַים מִלְחָא בְּגִין כֵּן עַל כָּל קָרְבָּנָךְ תַּקְרִיב מִלְחָא


And every oblation of thy mincha thou shalt salt with salt; thou shalt not withhold the salt of the covenant of thy God from thy mincha, because the twenty and four gifts of the priests are appointed with a covenant of salt; therefore salt shalt thou offer with all thy oblations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:14)


וְאִין תְּקָרֵב מִנְחַת בִּכּוּרִין קֳדָם יְיָ מְהַבְהַב קָלִי בְּנוּרָא קֶמַח קָלִי וּפֵירוּכִין תִּקְרַב יַת מִנְחַת בִּיכּוּרַיִךְ


And if thou wilt present a mincha of first fruits before the Lord, (ears of wheat) roasted by fire, roasted flour and meal of barley shalt thou offer as a mincha of thy first fruits.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:15)

And thou shalt put olive oil upon it, and lay frankincense thereon; it is a mincha.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/2:16)

And the two kidneys and the fat which is upon them (and) on the foldings, and the caul which is over the liver, along with the kidneys, he shall take away.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/3:16)


וְיַסְקִינוּן כַּהֲנָא לְמַדְבְּחָא לְחֵם קוּרְבָּנָא לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא כָּל תְּרֵיב קֳדָם יְיָ


And the priest shall sacrifice them at the altar, the meat of an oblation to be received with favour. All the fat (shall be offered) before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/3:17)


קְיַם עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן בְּכָל מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן כָּל תְּרֵיב וְכָל אֲדַם לָא תֵיכְלוּן עַל גַבֵּי מַדְבְּחָא יִתְקְרֵיב לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ


It is an everlasting statute unto all your generations, that neither the fat nor the blood shall be eaten in any of your dwellings, but upon the back of the altar it shall be sacrificed unto the Name of the Lord.



## Chapter 4

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:1)

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימַר בַּר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יֵיחוֹב בְּשָׁלוּ מִכָּל פִּקוּדַיָא דַיְיָ דְלָא כַשְׁרִין לְאִתְעֲבָדָא וְיַעֲבֵד מִן חַד מִנְהוֹן


Speak with the sons of Israel, saying: When a man hath sinned inadvertently against any of the commandments of the Lord (in doing) what ought not to be done, and he hath done it against any one of them:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:3)


אִין כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא דְמִתְרַבֵּי בְּמִשְׁחָא יֵיחוּב בְּמִקְרְבֵיהּ קָרְבַּן חוֹבַת עַמָא דְלָא כְּהִילְכְתֵיהּ וְיִקְרַב בְּגִין חוֹבְתֵיהּ תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי שְׁלִים קֳדָם יְיָ לְחַטָאתָא


if the high priest who is consecrated with oil hath sinned,-as when he hath offered a sin offering for the people not according to the rite, he shall bring for his sin a young bullock unblemished before the Lord for a sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:4)


וְיֵיעוֹל יַת תּוֹרָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא לִקֳדָם יְיָ וְיִסְמוֹךְ יַד יְמִינֵיהּ עַל רֵישׁ תּוֹרָא וְיִכּוֹס טַבְּחָא יַת תּוֹרָא קֳדָם יְיָ


He shall bring in the bullock to the gate of the tabernacle of ordinance, to the presence of the Lord, and lay his right hand upon the head of the bullock, and the slayer shall kill the bullock before the Lord.

and the priest shall dip his fingers in the blood, and sprinkle the blood seven times in the presence of the Lord before the veil of the sanctuary.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/4:7)


וְיִתֵּן כַּהֲנָא מִן אַדְמָא עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא דִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא דִקְדָם יְיָ דִבְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיַת כָּל אַדְמָא דְתוֹרָא יֵישׁוֹד לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא דְעָלָתָא דְבִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא


And the priest shall put some of the blood upon the horns of the altar of sweet incense that is before the Lord in the tabernacle of ordinance, and all the rest of the blood of the bullock he shall pour out at the foundation of the altar of burnt sacrifice which is at the gate of the tabernacle of ordinance.

## Chapter 5

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:1)


וּבַר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יֵיחוֹב וְיִשְׁמַע קַל אוֹמָאָה דִלְוָוט וְאִיהוּ סָהִיד אוֹ חָמָא חַד מֵעָלְמָא דְעָבַר עַל פִּתְגָמָא מוֹמָתָא אוֹ יְדַע בְּחַבְרֵיהּ דְבָטִיל שְׁבוּעָה וּלְוַוט אִין לָא יְחַוֵי יְקַבֵּיל חוֹבֵיהּ


When a man shall have sinned, and heard the voice of the oath of execration, or have been himself a witness, or shall have seen that cue of the world hath transgressed against the words of an oath, or shall have known that his companion hath sworn or imprecated vainly, if he show it not, he shall bear his sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:2)


אוֹ בַר נַשׁ דִי יִקְרַב בְּכָל מִדַעַם דִמְסָאֵיב אוֹ בְּנִיבְלַת חֵיוָא מְסָאַבְתָּא אוֹ בְּנִיבְלַת בְּעִירָא מְסָאַבְתָּא אוֹ בְּנִיבְלַת רְחֵישׁ מְסָאַב וְיִתְכַסֵי מִנֵּיהּ וְהוּא מְסָאֵב וְיִקְרַב בְּכָל קוּדְשַׁיָא וְיִתְחַיֵיב


Or if a man touch anything, unclean, whether the carcase of an unclean beast, or a carcase of unclean cattle, or the carcase of an unclean reptile, and it be hidden from him, and he, being unclean, shall touch any consecrated thing, he is guilty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:3)


אוֹ אֲרוּם יִקְרַב בְּסוֹאֲבִית אֵינָשָׁא לְכָל סוֹבְתֵיהּ דְמִסְתָּאֵב בָּהּ וְיִתְכַסֵי מִנֵּיהּ וְיִקְרַב בְּכָל קוּדְשַׁיָא וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדֵין אִתְגְלֵי לֵיהּ וְאִיהוּ יָדַע דִמְסָאַב וְלָא אִידְכֵּי וְיִתְחַיֵיב


Or if he touch the uncleanness of a man, even whatever uncleanness that defileth him, and it be hidden from him, and he touch anything consecrated, after that it is discovered by him, and he knoweth that he is defiled and not clean, he shall be guilty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:4)


אוֹ בַר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יוֹמֵי לְפָרָשָׁא בְּשִׁיפְוָון לְאַבְאָשָׁא אוֹ לְאוֹטָבָא לְכָל גִוַון דִיפָרֵשׁ אֵינָשָׁא לִדְהָוֵוה וְלִדְעָתִיד לְמֵיתֵי בְּאוֹמָתָא וּמְשַׁקֵיר בֵּיהּ וְיִתְכַסֵי מִינֵיהּ וּמִבָּתַר דְעָבַד אִיתְגְלֵי לֵיהּ וְאִיהוּ יָדַע דְשַׁקָר וְלָא תַב וְאִתְחַיֵיב לַחֲדָא מִן אִילֵין


Or if a man shall swear to make declaration with his lips to do evil or good upon any matter upon which a man may affirm, whether of the present or the future, that he can make declaration by oath, and he falsify therein, and it be hidden from him, but afterward it be discovered to him and he know that he hath falsified, and he repent not; though he hath become guilty in any one of these,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:5)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יֵיחוֹב בְּחָדָא מֵאַרְבַּעֲתֵּי אִלֵין וּבָתַר כֵּן תָּהֵא וְיוֹדִי חוֹבָתָא דְחַב עֲלָהּ


if he shall have (thus) sinned in any one of these four things, but afterwards repent, he shall make confession of the sin by which he hath sinned.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:6)


וְיַיְתִי יַת קָרְבַּן אַשְׁמֵיהּ לִקְדָם יְיָ מְטוֹל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דְחַב נוּקְבָא מִן עָנָא אִימַרְתָּא אוֹ צְפִירְתָּא דְעִיזֵי לְחַטָאתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי כַּהֲנָא מִן חוֹבָתֵיהּ


And he shall bring the oblation of his trespass offering to the Presence of the Lord for the sin that he hath sinned, a female lamb of the flock, or a kid of the goats, for a sin offering; and the priest shall atone for him (that he may be absolved) from his sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:7)


וְאִין לָא תֶאֱרַע יְדֵיהּ כְּמֵיסַת לְמַיְיתַיָא אִימְרָא וְיַיְתֵי יַת קָרְבַּן אַשְׁמֵיהּ דְחַב תְּרֵין שַׁפְנִינִין רַבְרְבִין אוֹ תְּרֵין גוֹזְלִין בְּנֵי יוֹנַיָא קֳדָם יְיָ חַד לְחַטָאתָא וְחַד לַעֲלָתָא


But if his hand find not sufficiency to bring a lamb, let him bring, as an offering for the trespass that he hath committed, two large turtle doves or two young pigeons before the Lord; one for a sin offering, and one for a burnt sacrifice.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:8)


וְיַיְתֵי יַתְהוֹן לְוַת כַּהֲנָא וְיִקְרַב יַת מַאן דְאִתְבְּחַר לְחַטָאתָא בְּשֵׁרוּייָא וְיַחֲזוֹם יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ לִקְבֵיל קוּדְלֵיהּ וְלָא יַפְרֵישׁ רֵישֵׁיהּ מִן קְדָלֵיהּ


And he shall bring them to the priest, who shall offer that which he may choose for the sin offering first: and lie shall wring its head near to the spine, but not separate its head from the neck;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:9)


וְיַדֵי מִן אֲדַם חַטָאתָא עַל כּוֹתַל מַדְבְּחָא וּדְאִשְׁתַּיֵיר בְּאַדְמָא יִתְמְצֵי לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא חַטָאתָא הוּא


and he shall sprinkle some of the blood upon the side of the altar, and pour out the remainder of the blood at the foot of the altar: it is a sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:10)


וְיַת עוֹף תִּנְיָין יַעֲבֵיד עֲלָתָא הֵי כְּהִילְכַת עוֹפָא דְאִתְבְּחַר בְּשֵׁירוּיָא לְחַטָאתָא וְלָא כְּהִלְכַת חַטָאתָא דְתוֹרָא דְאִימַר בַּר עִיזֵי וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי כַּהֲנָא מֵחוֹבְתֵיהּ דְחַב וְיִשְׁתְּבֵיק לֵיהּ


And of the second bird he shall make a burnt sacrifice, according to the rite with the bird which he had chosen for the sin offering, and not according to the rite for the bullock, or the lamb, or the young goat. And the priest shall expiate him from the sin that he hath sinned, and it shall be forgiven him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:11)


וְאִין לָא תֶאֱרַע יְדֵיהּ כְּמֵיסַת לְמַיְיתָיָא תְּרֵין שַׁפְנִינִין רַבְרְבִין אוֹ תְּרֵין גוֹזָלִין בְּנֵי יוֹנַיָא וְיַיְתֵי יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ דְחַב חַד מִן עַשְרָא בִּתְלַת סְאִין סְמִידָא לְחַטָאתָא לָא יִשְׁוֵי עֲלָהּ מִשְׁחָא וְלָא יִתֵּן עֲלָהּ לְבוֹנְתָּא אֲרוּם חַטָאתָא הוּא


But if his hand find not sufficiency to bring two large turtle doves or two young pigeons, let him bring as an oblation for sin a tenth part of three sein of flour for a sin offering; but let him not put oil thereon nor frankincense, for it is a sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:12)


וְיַיְתִינָהּ לְוַת כַּהֲנָא וְיִקְמוֹץ כַּהֲנָא מִינָהּ מְלֵי קוֹמְצֵיהּ יַת שְׁבַח אַדְכַּרְתָּא וְיִסַק לְמַדְבְּחָא עַל קוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ חַטָאתָא הוּא


And he shall bring it to the priest, and the priest shall take a handful for a commendable memorial thereof, and burn it at the altar with the oblations of the Lord: it is a sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:13)


וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי כַּהֲנָא עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דְחַב מֵחַד מֵאִלֵין וְיִשְׁתְּבֵיק לֵיהּ וּתְהֵי לְכַהֲנָא כְּמִנְחָתָא


And the priest shall atone for his sin that he hath sinned, and it shall be forgiven him. And the remainder shall be a mincha to the priest.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:14)

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:15)


בַּר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יְשַׁקֵר שְׁקַר וְיֵיחוֹב בְּשָׁלוּ וְיִתְהֲנִי מִן קוּדְשַׁיָא דַיְיָ וְיַיְתֵי יַת קוּרְבַּן אַשְׁמֵיהּ לִקְדָם יְיָ דְכַר שְׁלִים מִן עֲנָא בְּעִילוּיָיהּ כְּסַף הֵי כִּדְמֵי הַיְנִית קוּדְשָׁא דְאִתְהֲנֵי סִילְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא לְקָרְבַּן אֲשָׁמָא


When a man falsifieth with falsity and sinneth, though with inadvertence, in making misuse of the holy things of the Lord, he shall bring the oblation for his trespass to the presence of the Lord, an unblemished ram from the flock, with an estimation in silver according to the value of the holy thing which hath been misappropriated, in shekels, after the shekels of the sanctuary, for a trespass offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:16)


וְיַת הַנְיַית קוּדְשָׁא דְחַב מִן קוּדְשָׁא יַשְׁלִים וְיַת חוּמְשֵׁיהּ דְדָמוֹי יוֹסִיף עֲלוֹי וְיִתֵּן יָתֵיהּ לְכַהֲנָא וּבְרַם כַּהֲנָא יְכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי בְּדִיכְרָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיִשְׁתְּבֵיק לֵיהּ


And the misuse of the holy thing by which he sinned, (the perversion of what was) sanctified, he shall make good, and shall add the fifth of its value unto it, and bring it to the priest who shall atone for him with the ram of the trespass offering, and it shall be forgiven him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:17)


וְאִין בַּר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יֵיחוּב וְיַעֲבֵד חֲדָא מִכָּל פִּקוּדַיָיא דַיְיָ דְלָא כַשְׁרִין לְאִתְעוֹבָדָא וְלָא יְדַע וְאִתְחַיֵיב וִיקַבֵּל חוֹבֵיהּ


If a man sin, and do against any one of all the commandments of the Lord that which is not right to do, though he knew it not, he hath sinned, and shall bear his guilt;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:18)


וְיַיְתֵי דְכַר שְׁלִים מִן עָנָא בְּעִילוֹיֵי לְאַשְׁמָא לְוַת כַּהֲנָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי כַּהֲנָא עַל שָׁלוּתֵיהּ דְאִישְׁתְּלֵי וְהוּא לָא יְדַע וְחַב וְיִשְׁתְּבֵיק לֵיהּ


but (when he hath discovered it), let him bring a ram unblemished from the flock according to his estimation for a trespass offering unto the priest; and the priest shall atone for him for the ignorance with which he erred ignorantly and sinned, and it shall be forgiven him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:19)


קוּרְבַּן אַשְׁמָא הוּא כָּל דְאִתְחַיֵיב לְקָרְבַּן אַשְׁמָא יַיְתֵי קוּרְבַּן לְאַשְׁמָא לִשְׁמֵיהּ דַיְיָ עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דְחָב


It is an oblation for trespass. Whosoever hath become guilty, a trespass oblation let him bring, an oblation for trespass unto the Name of the Lord, for the sin that he hath sinned.

בַּר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יֵיחוֹב וְיִשְׁקַר שִׁיקְרִין לְשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְיִכְפּוֹר בְּחַבְרֵיהּ בְּפִקְדוֹנָא דְאַפְקֵיד גַבֵּיהּ אוֹ בְּשׁוּתָּפוּת יְדָא אוֹ בִגְזוֹלָא אוֹ דְיִטְלוֹם יַת חַבְרֵיהּ


When a man sinneth and falsifieth with falsehoods unto the Name of the Word of the Lord, or denieth to his neighbour the deposit which hath been deposited with him, whether in partnership of hands, or by rapine, or reckless dealing with his neighbour;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:22)


אוֹ אַשְׁכַּח אֲבִדְתָּא וְיִכְפּוֹר בָּהּ וְאִשְׁתְּבַע עַל שִׁיקְרָא עַל חַדָא מִכָּל דְיַעֲבֵיד אֵינָשָׁא לְאִתְחַיָיבָא בְּהוֹן


or if he find a thing that hath been lost and denieth it, and sweareth falsely about any one of all these by which a mail in doing them shall become guilty,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:23)


וִיהֵי אִין יֵיחְטֵי וְיִתְחַיֵיב וְיוֹמֵי וִיתִיב יַת גְזוֹלָא דְגָזַל אוֹ יַת עֵצְיָא דִי עֲצָא אוֹ יַת פִּקְדוֹנָא דִי אִתְפַּקֵיד גַבֵּיהּ אוֹ יַת אַבֵדְתָּא דְאַשְׁכַּח


he who shall thus transgress, and sin, and swear, shall restore what he hath robbed with robbery or injured by injury, or the deposit that was deposited with him, or the lost thing which he had found,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:24)


אוֹ מִכָּל מִדַעַם דְאִשְׁתְּבַע עֲלוֹי לְשִׁיקְרָא וְיַשְׁלִים יָתֵיהּ בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וְחוֹמֶשׁ דְמוֹי יוֹסֵף עֲלוֹי לְמָרֵיהּ וְהוּא דִילֵיהּ יִתְּנִינָהּ בְּיוֹמָא דִתְהָא עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ


or whatsoever about which he had sworn with falsehood, he shall make good in the capital, and shall add a fifth of its value thereto, and deliver it to its owner on the day that he maketh penance for his sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:25)


וְיַת קוּרְבַּן אֲשָׁמֵיהּ יַיְתֵי לִקְדָם יְיָ דְכַר שְׁלִים מִן עָנָא בְּעִילוּיָיהּ לְאַשְׁמָא לְוַת כַּהֲנָא


And he shall bring an oblation for his trespass to the presence of the Lord; a male unblemished from the flock, according to its estimation for the trespass, (shall he bring) unto the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/5:26)

And the priest shall atone for him before the Lord, and it shall be forgiven him concerning any one of all these which he may have done and become guilty.



## Chapter 6

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:2)


פַּקֵיד יַת אַהֲרן וְיַת בְּנוֹי לְמֵימָר דָא אוֹרַיְיתָא דְעָלָתָא דְאַתְיָא לְמִכַפְּרָא עַל הִרְהוֹרֵי לִבָּא הִיא עֲלָתָא דְמִתְעַבְּדָא בְּעָלָתָא בְּטַוְורָא דְסִינַי וְקַיְמָא עַל אֲתַר בֵּית יְקִידְתָּא עַל מַדְבַּח כָּל לֵילְיָא עַד צַפְרָא בְּרַם אֵישָׁתָא דְמַדְבְּחָא תְּהֵי יוֹקְדָא בֵיהּ


Instruct Aharon and his sons, saying, This is the law of the burnt offering which is brought to make atonement for the thoughts (errors) of the heart: it is a burnt offering, which is made in (the manner of) the burnt offering at Mount Sinai, and abideth upon the place of burning on the altar all the night until the morning: for the fire of the altar shall be burning in it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:3)


וְיִלְבּוֹשׁ כַּהֲנָא לְבוּשִׁין דְבוּץ וְאַוַורְקְטִין דְבוּץ יִלְבּוֹשׁ עַל בִּישְרֵיהּ וְיִפְרוֹשׁ יַת קִיטְמָא דְתֵיכוּל אֵישָׁתָא יַת עֲלָתָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא וִישַׁוִינֵיהּ בִּצְטַר מַדְבְּחָא


And the priest shall dress himself in vestments of linen, and put drawers of linen upon his flesh; and shall separate the ashes which the fire (maketh) in consuming the burnt offering upon the altar, and shall place them at the side of the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:4)


וִישַׁלַח יַת לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיִלְבַּשׁ לְבוּשִׁין חוֹרָנִין וִיהַנְפֵּק יַת קִיטְמָא לְמִיבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא לַאֲתַר דְכֵי


And he shall take off his vestments and put on other garments, and carry forth the ashes without the camp into a clean place.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:5)


וְאֵישָׁתָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא תְהֵי יוֹקְדָא בֵיהּ לָא תִטְפֵי וְיַלְפֵי עֲלָהּ כַּהֲנָא אָעִין בִּצְפַר בִּצְפַר עַד אַרְבַּע שְׁעִין דְיוֹמָא וִיסַדֵר עֲלָהּ עֲלָתָא וְיַסֵיק עֲלָהּ תַרְבֵּי נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא


But the fire upon the altar shall burn upon it unextinguished, and the priest shall lay wood upon it from morning to morning, at four hours of the day, and shall set in order the burnt offering upon it, and burn upon it the fat of the sanctified oblations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:6)


אֵישָׁתָא תְּדִירָא תְּהֵי יוֹקְדָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא לָא תִיטְפֵי


The fire shall be ever burning upon the altar; it shall never be extinguished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:7)


וְדָא אוֹרַיְיתָא דְמִנְחָתָא דְיִקְרְבוּן יָתָהּ בְּנֵי אַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא קֳדָם יְיָ לִקְדָם מַדְבְּחָא


And this is the law of the Mincha, which the priests, the sons of Aharon, shall offer in the presence of the Lord before the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:8)


וְיַפְרֵישׁ מִנֵיהּ בְּקוּמְצֵיהּ מִן סְמִידָא דְמִנְחָתָא וּמִן טוֹבָא עַל כָּל לְבוֹנְתָּא דְעַל מִנְחָתָא וְיַסֵיק לְמַדְבְּחָא לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא שְׁבַח אִדְכַּרְתָּהּ קֳדָם יְיָ


And he shall separate his handful of the flour of the mincha, of the best thereof, with all the frankincense which is upon the mincha, and burn it at the altar to be received with favour, as a memorial of praise before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/6:9)

And that which remaineth of it shall Aharon and his sons eat; unleavened shall they eat it in the holy place, in the court of the tabernacle of ordinance shall they eat it.

This is the oblation of Aharon and of his sons, which they are to offer before the Lord on the day that they anoint him, that he may possess the inheritance of the high priesthood. A tenth of three seahs of fine flour for a mincha, one half in the morning and a half at eventide.

whosoever toucheth the flesh of it must be sanctified. And if any one let some of its blood fall upon a garment, (the garment so) bedropped shall be washed in the holy place.

But no sin offering whose blood is carried into the tabernacle of ordinance to make atonement in the sanctuary may be eaten; it must be burned with fire.

And this is the law of the Trespass Offering; it is most holy.

And he shall offer all the fat thereof, and the tail, and the fat which covereth the inwards;

and the two kidneys, and the fat which is upon them, and upon the inwards. And the caul that is upon the liver upon the kidneys shall he take away;

and the priest shall burn them at the altar, an oblation before the Lord: it is a trespass offering.

Every man of the priests may eat of it, in the holy place shall it be eaten it is most sacred.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:7)


כְּהִלְכַת חַטָאתָא הֵיכְדֵין הִלְכַת אֲשָׁמָא אוֹרַיְיתָא חֲדָא לְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא דִי יְכַפֵּר בֵּיהּ דִילֵיהּ יְהֵי


As the rite of the sin offering, so is the rite of the trespass; there is one law for them: the priest who maketh atonement with its blood shall have it.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:8)


וְכַהֲנָא דִמְקַרִיב יַת עוֹלַת גְבַר אוֹחֲרָן מְשַׁךְ עֲלָתָא דִיקְרֵיב לְכַהֲנָא דִילֵיהּ יְהֵי


And when the priest offereth another man's burnt sacrifice, the skin of the burnt sacrifice which he offereth shall be the priest's.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:9)


וְכָל מִנְחָתָא דְתִיתְאֲפֵי בְּתַנוּרָא וְכָל דְתִתְעֲבֵד בְּמַרְתְּחָא וְעַל מַסְרִיתָא לְכַהֲנָא דְמִקְרַב יָתָהּ דִילֵיהּ יְהֵי


And every mincha which is baked in the oven, and every one that is made in a pot, or in a frying pan, or upon a dish, the priest who offereth it shall have it for his own.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:10)


וְכָל מִנְחָתָא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשָׁח וּמְנַגְבָא לְכָל בְּנֵי אַהֲרן תְּהֵי גְבַר כְּאָחוֹי


And every mincha mixed with oil, or which is dry, shall be for any of the sons of Aharon, a man as his brother.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:11)


וְדָא אוֹרַיְיתָא דְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא דִי יִקְרַב קֳדָם יְיָ


And this is the law of the Sanctified Victims which they may offer before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:12)


אִין עַל תּוֹדָתָא יְקַרְבִינֵיהּ וְיִקְרַב עַל נִכְסַת תּוֹדָתָא גְרִיצִין פַּטִירַן פְּתִיכַן בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא וְעַרוּכִין פְּטִירִין מְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא וְקִמְחָא מְטַגְנָא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא


If he offer it for a thanksgiving let him offer with the oblation of thanks unleavened cakes mingled with olive oil, and unleavened wafers anointed with olive oil, and flour fried with a mixture of olive oil.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:13)

Upon the cakes he shall offer his oblation of leavened bread with the hallowed sacrifice of thanksgiving.

And of it he shall present one as a seperation before the Lord; the priest who sprinkleth the blood of the hallowed sacrifice shall have it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:15)


וּבְשַר נִכְסַת תּוֹדַת קוּדְשׁוֹי בְּיוֹם קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ יִתְאָכֵיל לֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְאִצְטַנְעָא מִנֵיהּ עַד צַפְרָא


And the flesh of his hallowed sacrifice of thanksgiving shall be eaten on the day when it is offered; none of it may be laid up (or covered up) until the morning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:16)


וְאִם נִדְרָא אוֹ נְדַבְתָּא נִכְסַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ בְּיוֹמָא דְיַקְרֵיב יַת נִכְסְתֵיהּ יִתְאֲכֵיל וּבְיוֹם מָחֳרָן וּמַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר מִנֵּיהּ יִתְאֲכֵיל בְּפַנְיָא


But if his hallowed sacrifice be a vow or a free-will gift, the sacrifice may be (partly) eaten on the day when it is offered, and the remainder may be eaten on the day following at evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:17)


וּמַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר מִבְּשַר נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא בְּיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה בְּנוּרָא יִתּוֹקָד


And what remaineth of the flesh of the hallowed sacrifice on the third day shall be burned in fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:18)


וְאִין יִתְאָכְלָא יִתְאָכֵיל מִבְּשַר נִכְסַת קוּדְשׁוֹי בְּיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה לָא יִתְרְעֵי מַן דְמִקְרַב יָתֵיהּ לָא יִתְחַשֵׁב לֵיהּ לִזְכוּ פְסִיל יְהֵי וְאֵינַשׁ דְיֵכוֹל מִנֵיהּ חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּל


If, eating, he will eat of the flesh of his hallowed sacrifice on the third day, it shall not be accepted of him who offered it, nor reckoned to him for righteousness; it will be a profane thing, and the man who eateth of it shall bear his sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:19)


וּבְשַר קוּדְשַׁיָא דִי יִקְרַב בְּכָל מְסָאָב לֵית אֶפְשַׁר דְמִיתְאָכֵיל בְּנוּרָא יִתּוֹקָד וּבְשַר קוּדְשָׁא כָּל דְיִדְכֵּי לְקוּדְשָׁא יֵיכוּל בְּשַר קוּדְשָׁא


And if the flesh of things hallowed touch any uncleanness, it must not be eaten, but be burned in fire; but (as to) flesh that is consecrated, every one who is clean by sanctification may eat the hallowed flesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:20)


וּבַר נַשׁ דְיֵיכוֹל בִּסְרָא מִנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא דְמִתְקַרְבִין קֳדָם יְיָ וּסְאוֹבְתֵיהּ עֲלוֹי וְיִשְׁתְּצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵיהּ


But the man who eateth of the flesh of the hallowed sacrifice that is offered before the Lord with his uncleanness upon him, that man shall be destroyed from among his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:21)


וּבַר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יַקְרִיב בְּכָל מְסָאָב בְּסוֹאֲבוֹת אֵינָשָׁא אוֹ בִּבְעִירָא מְסָאֲבָא אוֹ בְּכָל שִׁיקוּץ מְסָאָב וְיֵיכוּל מִבְּשַר נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא דְמִתְקַרְבִין קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִשְׁתְּצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵיהּ


The man also who toucheth any unclean thing, whether the uncleanness of man, or of unclean beasts, or any unclean reptile, and eateth of the flesh of the hallowed sacrifices offered before the Lord, that man shall be cut off from his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:22)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:23)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר כָּל תְּרַב תּוֹר וְאִימַר וְעִיזָא לָא תֵיכְלוּן


Speak with the sons of Israel, saying: You may not eat any fat of oxen, or sheep, or goats;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:24)


וּתְרֵיב חֵיוָא דִמְקַלְקְלָא בְּשַׁעַת נִיכְסְתָא וּדְמִיתְנַבְלָא בְּמוֹתָנָא וּתְרֵיב חֵיוָא תְבִירָא אֶפְשַׁר דְיִתְעֲבֵיד לְכָל עִיבִידְתָּא בְּרַם תְּרֵיב חֵיוָא דִמְכַשְׁרָא יִתְסַק עַל מַדְבְּחָא וּמֵיכַל לָא תֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ


but the fat of an animal which corrupteth in the hour of sacrifice, or which dieth a dead thing by death, or the fat of a beast that is torn, may be used in any work; but the fat of an animal that is in a right (condition) shall be burned upon the altar, and shall in no wise be eaten.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:25)


אֲרוּם כָּל דְיֵיכוֹל תְּרֵיב מִן בְּעִירָא דְמִתְכַּשְׁרָא לְמִיקְרַב מִנֵיהּ קָרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִשְׁתְּצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא דְיֵיכוֹל יַת תַּרְבָּא מֵעַמֵיהּ


For he who eateth (the fat) of an animal that is fit to be offered as an oblation before the Lord, that man who eateth the fat shall be cut off from his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:26)


וְכָל אַדְמָא לָא תֵיכְלוּן בְּכָל מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן לְעוֹפָא וְלִבְעִירָא


In none of your dwellings shall you eat the blood whether of bird or of beast.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:27)


כָּל בַּר נַשׁ דְיֵיכוֹל כָּל אֲדָם מִן דְכָל חַי וְיִשְׁתְּצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵיהּ


Every man who eateth the blood of any living thing, that man shall be cut off from his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:28)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:29)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר כָּל מַאן דְמִקְרַב יַת נִכְסַת קוּדְשׁוֹי קֳדָם יְיָ יַיְתֵי בְּגַרְמֵיהּ יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ לִקְדָם יְיָ מִן נִכְסַת קוּדְשׁוֹי


Speak with the sons of Israel, saying: Whosoever presenteth his hallowed sacrifice before the Lord, shall himself bring the oblation of his hallowed sacrifice unto the presence of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:30)


יְדוֹי יַיְתִין יַת קוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ דִי יַפְרֵשׁ מִנִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיהּ יַת תַּרְבָּא שׁוּמְנוּנִיתָא דְעַל חַדְיָא וְיַת חַדְיָא כַּד מַחְתָּךְ בִּתְרֵין עִילְעִין מִיכָּא וּתְרֵין עִלְעִין מִיכָּא לִקֳבֵיל אַפְקוֹתָא יַיְתִינֵהּ לַאֲרָמָא יָתֵיהּ אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ


His hands shall bring the oblations of the Lord which he would set apart as his hallowed sacrifice, the fat, the fatness that is upon the breast, and the breast cut out with two ribs here and two ribs there at the top, shall be bring to be uplifted, an elevation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:31)


וְיִסַק כַּהֲנָא יַת תַּרְבָּא לְמַדְבְּחָא וִיהֵי חַדְיָא לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי


And the priest shall burn the fat upon the altar, and the breast shall be for Aharon and for his sons.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:32)


וְיַת שַׁקָא דְיַמִינָא מִן כַּתְפָא וְעַד דְרוֹעָא תִּתְּנוּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא לְכַהֲנָא מִנִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן


And the right shoulder of your hallowed sacrifice from the side unto the extremity (deroa, arm) you shall give as a separation unto the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:33)


מַאן דִמְקַרֵב יַת אֲדַם נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא וְיַת תְּרֵיב מִן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן דִילֵיהּ תְּהֵי שַׁקָא דְיַמִינָא לְחוּלַק


He of the sons of Aharon who offereth the blood and the fat of the hallowed sacrifice shall have the right shoulder as his portion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:34)


אֲרוּם יַת חַדְיָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא וְיַת שׁוֹקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא נְסֵיבִית מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן נִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן וִיהָבִית יַתְהוֹן לְאַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא וְלִבְנוֹי לִקְיַים עֲלָם מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


For the uplifted breast and the shoulder of separation have I taken of your hallowed sacrifice, and given them to Aharon the priest and to his sons by an everlasting statute, from the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:35)


דָא רְבוּתָא דְאַהֲרן וּרְבוּתָא דִבְנוֹי עַל כָּל אֲחוּהוֹן לֵיוָאֵי דְיֵיכְלוּן מִקוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ בְּיוֹמָא דִיְקָרְבוּן יַתְהוֹן לְשַׁמָשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ


This pertaineth to the consecration of Aharon and to the consecration of his sons over all the Levites their brethren, that they may eat of the Lord's oblations in the day that they present them to minister before the Lord;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:36)


דְפַקֵיד יְיָ לְמִנְתַּן לְהוֹם בְּיוֹמָא דְרַבֵּי יַתְהוֹן מֵעִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל קְיַם עֲלָם לְדָרֵיהוֹן


which the Lord commanded to be given them in the day of their consecration from among the sons of Israel, by an everlasting statute to your generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:37)


דָא אוֹרַיְיתָא לַעֲלָתָא דְאַתְיָא לְכַפָּרָא עַל הִרְהוּרֵי לִבָּא לְמִנְחָתָא וּלְחַטָאתָא וּלְאֲשָׁמָא וּלְאַשְׁלְמוּתָא וּלְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא


This is the law of the burnt offering which is brought to atone for the thoughts of the heart; of the mincha, of the sin offering, of the trespass offering, and of the peace offering, or the hallowed sacrifices


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 7:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/7:38)


דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה בְּטַוְורָא דְסִינַי בְּיוֹמָא דְפַקֵיד יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְקָרָבָא קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא דְעָבָדוּ לֵיהּ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי


which the Lord commanded Mosheh in Mount Sinai, in the day that he commanded the sons of Israel to offer their oblations before the Lord in the tabernacle that he made unto him in the wilderness of Sinai.



## Chapter 8

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:2)


קְרֵיב יַת אַהֲרן דְאִתְרַחֵק עַל עוֹבְדָא דְעֵיגְלָא וְטוֹל יַת לְבוּשַׁיָא דְפַקֵידְתָּךְ וְיַת מִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וְיַת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָאתָא וְיַת תְּרֵין דִכְרִין וְיַת סַלָא דְפַטִירַיָא


Bring near Aharon who is afar off on account of the work of the calf; and take the vestments that I commanded thee, and the oil of consecration, and the bullock, and the two rams, with the basket of unleavened cakes.

וּנְכַס משֶׁה יַת תּוֹרָא וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יַת אַדְמָא וִיהַב עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ וְרַבֵּי יַת מַדְבְּחָא מִן כָּל סְפַק אָנוֹס וְחָטוּפִין מְטוֹל דְחָשִׁיב בִּלְבָבֵיהּ דִלְמָא נְסִיבָא סַרְכַיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אַפְרָשׁוּתָא מִן אָחֵיהוֹן בְּאוֹנְסָא וּקְרִיבוּ לַעֲבִידַת מַשְׁכְּנָא אוֹ דִילְמָא הִישְׁתְּכַּח בִּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דְלָא הֲוָה בְּלִבֵּיהּ לְמֵיתַיָא לַעֲבִידְתָּא וְשָׁמַע קַל כְּרוּזָא וְאִיסְתְּפֵי וְאַיְיתִי בְּלָא צְבוֹ מְטוֹל כֵּן דְכֵי יָתֵיהּ בַּאֲדַם תּוֹרָא וְיַת מוֹתַר אַדְמָא אָרִיק לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא וְקַדְשֵׁיהּ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹי


And Mosheh killed the bullock: and Mosheh took the blood and put it upon the horns of the altar round about with his finger, and anointed the altar (to expiate it) from all double-mindedness, constraint, and force, from the thoughts of his heart, should any one of the princes of the sons of Israel have taken his separation from his brethren by violence, and brought it for the work of the tabernacle, or lest any one was found among the children of Israel who had it not in his heart to bring for the work, but heard the voice of the crier, and was constrained, and brought without willingness; therefore cleansed he it with the blood of the bullock, and poured the rest of the blood at the foot of the altar, and sanctified it to make atonement thereon.

וְסַדֵר יַת כּוֹלָא עַל יְדֵי אַהֲרן וְעַל יְדֵי בְנוֹי וַאֲרֵים יַתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ


and laid the whole in order upon Aharon's hands, and upon the hands of his sons, lifted them up, an elevation before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:28)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יַתְהוֹן מֵעַל יְדֵיהוֹן וְאַסֵיק עַל מַדְבְּחָא עַל עֲלָתָא קָרְבָּן אַשְׁלָמוּתָא הִינוּן דִשְׁלִימוּן בְּכוֹלָא מְטוּל לְאִתְקַבְּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And Mosheh took them from off their hands, and burned (them) upon the altar with the burnt sacrifice; a completing offering were they to complete all, to be received with acceptance before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:29)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יַת חַדְיָא וַאֲרִימֵיהּ אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִדְכַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא אִתְפְּרַשׁ לְמשֶׁה הֲוָה לְחוּלַק הֵי כְּמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And he took the breast, and uplifted it, an elevation before the Lord: of the oblation-ram that was the separated portion of Mosheh, as the Lord commanded Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:30)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה מִמִשְׁחָא דִרְבוּתָא וּמִן אַדְמָא דְעַל מַדְבְּחָא וְאַדִי עַל אַהֲרן וְעַל לְבוּשׁוֹי וְעַל בְּנוֹי וְעַל לְבוּשֵׁי בְנוֹי עִמֵיהּ וְקַדִישׁ יַת אַהֲרן וְיַת לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַת בְּנוֹי וְיַת לְבוּשֵׁי בְנוֹי עִמֵיהּ


And Mosheh took the consecrating oil, and of the blood which was upon the altar, and sprinkled upon Aharon, and upon his vestments, and on his sons, and on their vestments with him; and sanctified Aharon and his vestments, and his sons and their vestments with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:31)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי בְּשִׁילוּ יַת בְּשַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא בְּדוֹדְוָותַיָא בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וְתַמָן תֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ וְיַת לַחְמָא דִבְסַל קוּרְבָּנַיָא הֵי כְמָא דְפַקֵידִית לְמֵימָר אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹהִי יֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ


And Mosheh said to Aharon and to his sons, Boil the flesh of the oblations in pots at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, and there shall you eat it with the bread which is in the basket of oblation, according to the precept which was spoken; Aharon and his sons shall eat it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:32)


וּמַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר בְּבִישְרָא וּבְלַחְמָא בְּנוּרָא תּוֹקְדוּן


And what remaineth of the flesh, and of the bread, you shall burn with fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:33)


וּמִתְּרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא לָא תִפְקוּן שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין עַד יוֹם מִישְׁלַם יוֹמֵי אַשְׁלָמְנוּתְכוֹן אֲרוּם שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין יִתוֹקָם מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיִתְפְּרֵק וְיִתְקָרֵב קוּרְבַּנְכוֹן


And from the door of the tabernacle you shall not go forth seven days, until the day that your consecration be completed, (because in seven days is the tabernacle set up and taken in pieces,) and your oblation be offered.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:34)


הֵי כְמָא דְעָבַד וְסָדַר יַת סֵדֶר קוּרְבָּנַיָא בְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין הֵיכְדֵין פַּקֵיד יְיָ לְמֶעֱבַד אַתּוּן בָּתַר יוֹמֵי אַשְׁלָמוּתָא לִמְכַפְּרָא עֲלֵיכוֹן


(So did he, and ordained the order of the oblations on that day.) Likewise the Lord hath commanded to be done by you after the days of consecration, to make atonement for you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 8:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:35)


וּבִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא תֵּיתְבוּן יוֹמָם וְלֵילֵי שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין וְתִטְרוּן יַת מַטְרַת מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְלָא תְמוּתוּן אֲרוּם הֵיכְדֵין אִיתְפַּקֵדִית


And at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance you shall reside day and night seven days, and watch the vigils of the Word of the Lord, that you may not die, for thus it hath been commanded.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/8:36)


וַעֲבַד אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דְפַּקֵיד יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה


And Aharon and his sons did all the things which the Lord had commanded by the hand of Mosheh.



## Chapter 9

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:1)


ויהי וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה לִרְבוּת אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וְיוֹם תְּמִינָאָה לְאַשְׁלָמוּתָא הוּא יוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה לְיַרְחָא דְנִיסָן אָקִים משֶׁה יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְלָא פַרְקֵיהּ וְלָא שַׁמֵשׁ תּוּב עַל גַבֵּי מַדְבְּחָא בְּכֵן קָרָא משֶׁה לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי וּלְסָבֵי סַנְהֶדְרֵי יִשְרָאֵל


On the eighth day of the anointing of Aharon and his sons, and the eighth day of that consecration, being the first day of the month of Nisan, when Mosheh had erected the tabernacle, he took it not down, neither ministered any longer at the altar; but Mosheh called Aharon and his sons, and the elders of the sanhedrin of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:2)


וַאֲמַר לְאַהֲרן סַב לָךְ עֵיגַל בַּר תּוֹרֵי לְחַטָאתָא מְטוֹל דְלָא יִשְׁתְּעֵי עֲלָךְ שִטְנָא לִישָׁן תְּלִיתָאֵי עַל עֵיסַק עֵיגְלָא דְעָבַדְתָּ בְּחוֹרֵב וּבְרַם דְכַר לַעֲלָתָא תִּיסַב מִן בִּגְלַל דִי יִדְכַּר לָךְ זְכוּתָא דְיִצְחָק דְכַפְתֵיהּ אָבוֹי כְּדִיכְרָא בְּטַוָור פּוּלְחָנָא תְּרֵיהוֹן יְהוֹן שַׁלְמִין וּקְרֵיב קֳדָם יְיָ


And he said to Aharon, Take thou a calf, the young of a bullock, for a sin offering, that Satan may not accuse thee concerning the calf that thou madest at Horeb; and take a ram for the burnt sacrifice, that there may be a memorial for thee of the righteousness of Izhak whom his father bound as a ram on the mountain of worship, both of them shall be perfect, and bring them before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:3)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל תְּמַלֵיל לְמֵימָר סָבוּ בְּרַם אַתּוּן צְפִיר בַּר עִיזֵי מְטוֹל דְסַטָנָא מִימְתִיל בֵּיהּ מְטוֹל דְלָא יִשְׁתְּעֵי עֲלֵיכוֹן לִישָׁן תְּלִיתָאֵי עַל עֵיסַק צְפִיר בַּר עִיזֵי דִנְכִיסוּ שִׁבְטוֹי דְיַעֲקב וּרְמִיוּ יָתֵיהּ וְעִיבַד יָתֵיהּ לְחַטָאתָא וְעֵיגְלָא מְטוֹל דְאִשְׁתַּעְבַּדְתּוּן לְעֵיגְלָא וְאִימַר בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ מְטוֹל דְיִדְכַּר לְכוֹן זְכוּתָא דְיִצְחָק דְכַפְתֵיהּ אָבוֹי הֵי כְאִמְרָא תְּרֵיהוֹן שַׁלְמִין לְעָלָתָא


And to the children of Israel spoke he, saying: Take for yourselves a kid of the goats, because Satana resembles him, lest he recount against you the accusation concerning the kid of the goats, which the sons (tribes) of Jakob killed, (Gen. xxxvii. 31,) and offer him for a sin offering; and a calf, because ye worshipped the calf, (Exod. xxxii. 4,) and a lamb of the year, that there may be for you a memorial of the righteousness of Izhak, whom his father did bind as a lamb, both of them perfect, for a burnt offering;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:4)


וְתוֹר וְאִימְרָא לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁא לְדַבְּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִן בִּגְלַל דְיִתְרְעִי בְּכוֹן וּמִנְחָתָא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא אֲרוּם יוֹמָא דֵין אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ מִתְגְלֵי לְכוֹן


with a bullock and a lamb, for a hallowed oblation to sacrifice before the Lord, that He may be gracious to you; and a mincha mingled with oil of the olive. For this day will the glory of the Lord's Shekinah be revealed unto you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:5)


וְאִזְדַרְזוּן אַהֲרן וּבְנוֹי וְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּנְסִיבוּ יַת מַה דְפַּקֵיד משֶׁה וְהַיְיתִיוּ לִקֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וּקְרִיבוּ כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא וְקָמוּן בְּלֵב שְׁלִים קֳדָם יְיָ


And Aharon and his sons, and all the sons of Israel, hastened and took what Mosheh commanded, and presented them in front of the tabernacle of ordinance; and the whole congregation drew near, and lifted up their heart fully before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:6)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה דֵין פִּתְגָמָא דְתַעַבְדוּן אַעַבְּרוּ יַת יִצְרָא בִּישָׁא מִן לִבְּכוֹן וּמִן יַד יִתְגְלֵי לְכוֹן אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ


And Mosheh said, This is the thing which you must do. Put away the imagination of evil from your hearts, and there will speedily (at once) be revealed to you the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:7)


וַהֲוָה כֵּיוָן דְחָמָא אַהֲרן מַדְבְּחָא בְּקַרְנוֹי מִדַמֵי לְעִיגְלָא אִיסְתְּפֵי לְמִיקְרַב לְגַבֵּיהּ בְּכֵן אָמַר לֵיהּ משֶׁה אָנִיס מִנְדְעָךְ וּקְרֵיב לְמַדְבְּחָא וְלָא תִסְתְּפֵי וְעִיבַד יַת חַטָאתָךְ וְיַת עֲלָתָךְ וְכַפֵּר אַמְטוּלָתָךְ וְאַמְטוּל עַמָא וְעִיבַד יַת קָרְבַּן עַמָא וְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיהוֹן הֵי כְּמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ


But when Aharon saw at the corner of the altar the form of the calf, he was afraid to approach to its side. Mosheh, therefore, said to him, Take courage, and go near to the altar, fearing not, and offer thy sin offering, and make atonement for thyseIf and for the people, and perform the oblation of the people, and make atonement for them, as the Lord hath commanded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:8)


וּקְרֵיב אַהֲרן לְמַדְבְּחָא בִּזְרֵיזוּתָא וּנְכֵיס יַת עֶגְלָא דְחַטָאתָא דִידֵיהּ


And Aharon approached to the altar with resolution, and slew the calf for his own sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:9)


וּקְרִיבוּ בְּנֵי אַהֲרן יַת אַדְמָא לְוָתֵיהּ וְטָבַל אַדְבְּעֵיהּ בַּאֲדָם תּוֹרָא וִיהַב עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא וְיַת שְׁאַר אַדְמָא אָרִיק לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא וְקַדְשֵׁיהּ לִמְכַפְּרָא עֲלוֹי


And the sons of Aharon brought the blood to him, and he dipped his finger in the blood of the young bullock, and put it upon the horns of the altar, and the rest of the blood he poured out at the foundation of the altar, and sanctified it for the making of atonement upon it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:10)


וְיַת תְּרֵיב וְיַת כּוֹלְיַין וְיַת חַצְרָא מִן כַּבְדָא מִן חַטָאתָא אַסִיק לְמַדְבְּחָא הֵי כְּמָא דְפַקֵד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And the fat, and the kidneys, with the caul of the liver of the sin offering, he burned at the altar, as the Lord had commanded Mosheh.

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:23)


וְכֵיוָן דְאִתְעֲבִידוּ קוּרְבָּנַיָא וְלָא אִתְגְלֵית שְׁכִינְתָּא הֲוָה אַהֲרן מַבְהִית וַאֲמַר לְמשֶׁה דִילְמָא לָא אִתְרְעֵי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּעוֹבְדֵי יְדָי בְּכֵן עָלוּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וּצְלוּ עַל עַמָא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל וּנְפָקוּ וּבְרִיכוּ יַת עַמָא וַאֲמַר יְקַבֵּיל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּרַעֲוָא יַת קוּרְבַּנְכוֹן וְיִשְׁרֵי וְיִשְׁבּוֹק עַל חוֹבֵיכוֹן וּמִן יַד אִיתְגְלֵי אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ לְכָל עַמָא


But when, after the oblations had been performed, the Shekinah did not reveal itself, Aharon was ashamed, and said to Mosheh, It may be that the Word of the Lord hath no pleasure in the work of my hands. Then went Mosheh and Aharon into the tabernacle of ordinance, and prayed for the people of the house of Israel, and came forth and blessed the people, and said, May the Word of the Lord receive your oblations with favour, and remit and forgive your sins. Then, instant, instantly the Glory of the Lord's Shekinah revealed itself to all the people:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/9:24)


וּנְפָקַת אֵישָׁתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְאַכְלַת עַל מַדְבְּחָא יַת עָלָתָא יַת תַּרְבַּיָא וְחָמוּן כָּל עַמָא וְאוֹדוּן וְאִיתְרְכִינוּ בִּצְלוֹ עַל אַנְפֵּיהוֹן


and the Fire came forth from the Presence of the Lord and consumed upon the altar the sacrifice and the fat. And all the people saw, and gave praise, and bowed in prayer upon their faces.



## Chapter 10

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:1)


וּנְסִיבוּ בְנֵי אַהֲרן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא גְבַר מַחְתִּיתֵיהּ וִיהָבוּ בְהוֹן אֵישָׁתָא וְשַׁוִיאוּ עֲלָהּ קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין וּקְרִיבוּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֵישָׁתָא נוּכְרָתָא מִן תַּפְיַין מַה דְלָא פַּקֵיד יַתְהוֹן


But the sons of Aharon, Nadab and Abihu, took each man his censer, and put fire therein, and laid sweet incense upon it, and offered before the Lord strange fire taken from (under) the hearth-pots, which had not been commanded them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:2)


וּנְפָקַת שַׁלְהוֹבִית אֵישָׁתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ בִּרְגַז וְאִתְפַּלִיגַת לְאַרְבַּעְתֵּי חוּטִין וְאָעֲלַת בְּגוֹ אַפֵּיהוֹן וְאוֹקִידַת יַת נִשְׁמַתְהוֹן בְּרַם גוּפֵיהוֹן לָא אִיתְחַרְכוּ וּמִיתוּ קֳדָם יְיָ


And a flame of fire came out from before the Lord (as) with anger, and divided itself into four streams, (or lines,) and penetrated their nostrils, and burned their lives (souls) without destroying their bodies; and they died before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:3)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה הִיא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ עִמִי בְּסִינַי לְמֵימָר בִּדְקָרִיבִין קֳדָמַי אֲנָא מַקְדַשׁ מַשְׁכְּנָא דְאִין לָא מִזְדַהֲרִין בְּעִיבִידַת קוּרְבָּנַיָא אוֹקִידִינוּן בְּשַׁלְהוֹבִית אֵישָׁתָא מִן קֳדָמוֹי מְטוֹל דְעַל מֵיחְמֵי כָּל עַמָא אִיתְיְקַר וּשְׁמַע אַהֲרן וְשָׁתִיק וְקַבֵּיל אֲגַר טַב עַל מִשְׁתּוֹקֵיהּ


And Mosheh said, This is that which the Lord spake with me in Sinai, saying: In them who come near before Me I will have the tabernacle to be sacred, that, if they be not heedful in the service of the oblations, I will burn them with flaming fire from before Me, that in the sight of all the people I may be glorified. And Aharon heard, and was silent; and he received a good reward for his silence.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:4)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לְמִישָׁאֵל וּלְאֶלְצָפָן בְּנוֹי דְעוּזִיאֵל לֵיוָאֵי חֲבִיבָא דְאַהֲרן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן קְרִיבוּ טוּלוּ יַת אֲחוּכוֹן מִן קוּדְשָׁא וּתְסוֹבְרִינוּן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא


And Mosheh called unto Mishael and to Elzaphan, the sons of Uzziel the Levite, the relative of Aharon, and said to them, Take your brethren from the sanctuary, and carry them, without the camp.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:5)


וּקְרִיבוּ וְסוֹבְרִינוּן בְּאוּנְקְלָוָון דְפַרְזְלָא בְּכִיתּוֹנֵיהוֹן וּקְבָרוּנוּן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל משֶׁה


And they came nigh, and carried them with hooks of iron in their garments, and buried them without the camp, as Mosheh had directed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/10:6)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרן וּלְאֶלְעָזָר וּלְאִיתָמָר בְּנוֹי רֵישֵׁיכוֹן לָא תַרְבּוּן פָּרוּעַ וּלְבוּשֵׁיכוֹן לָא תְבַזְעוּן וְלָא תְמוּתוּן בִּיקִידַת אֵישָׁתָא וְעִילוֹי כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא יְהֵי רוּגְזָא בְּרַם שְׁתּוּקוּן וְתִזְכּוּן יַת דִינָא עֲלֵיכוֹן וַאֲחֵיכוֹן כָּל בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל יִבְכּוּן יַת יַקִידְתָּא דְאוֹקִיד יְיָ


And Mosheh said to Aharon, and to Elasar and to Ithamar, his sons, Unbare not your heads, neither rend your garments, lest you die by the burning fire, and there be wrath upon all the congregation; but be silent and justify the judgment upon you, and let all your brethren of the house of Israel bewail the burning which the Lord hath kindled.

And from the door of the tabernacle of ordinance go not forth lest you die; for the oil of the Lord's consecration is upon you. And they did according to the word of Mosheh.

And the Lord spake with Aharon, saying,

חֲמַר וְכָל מִדַעַם מַרְוֵי לָא תִשְׁתֵּי אַנְתְּ וּבְנָךְ עִמָךְ בִּזְמַן מֵיעַלְכוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא הֵיכְמָא דְעָבְדוּ בְּנָךְ דְמִיתוּ בִּיקִידַת אֵישָׁתָא קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן


Drink neither wine nor anything that maketh drunk, neither thou nor thy sons with thee at the time when ye are to enter into the tabernacle of ordinance, as thy sons did who have died by the burning of fire. It is an everlasting statute for your generations;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 10:10

And on this day three goats shall be offered; the goat for the beginning of the month, (or, new moon,) the goat of the people's sin offering, and the goat for the sin offering which Nachson bar Aminadab hath brought for the dedication of the altar. And Aharon and his sons went and burned those three. (But) Mosheh came and inquired for the goat of the people's sin offering; he sought it, but, behold, it had been burned, and he was angry with Elasar and Ithamar, the sons of Aharon who were left, and said,

וּמַלֵיל אַהֲרן עִם משֶׁה הָא יוֹמָא דֵין קְרִיבוּ בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת קָרְבַּן חַטַאתְהוֹן וְעָלְוַותְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲרַע יָתִי סִקוּל כְּאִילֵין בִּתְרֵין בְּנַי הֲלָא מַעֲשְרָא תִנְיָנָא אִתְפַּקֵיד דִי לָא לְמֵיכַל אָבִיל מִינֵיהּ כָּל דְכֵן קָרְבָּן חַטָאתָא וּמַה אִילוּ אִישְׁתְּלִיַית וַאֲכָלִית מִן קָרְבַּן חַטָאתָא יוֹמָא דֵין אוּף תְּרֵין בְּנַי דְאִשְׁתְּיָירוּ הֲוָה לְהוֹן מִן דִינָא דְיִתּוֹקְדוּן דְלֵית אֶפְשַׁר דִי שְׁפַר קֳדָם יְיָ


And Aharon said to Mosheh, Behold, this day the sons of Israel have brought the oblation of their sin offering and their burnt sacrifice before the Lord; but a stroke hath befallen me, in those my two sons. Of the second tythe is it not commanded, Thou shalt not eat of it while mourning? How much more, then of the sin offering? If I had eaten of the sin offering this day with my two sons who are left, would it not have been all error, so that they too might have been burned by a judgment, for doing that which was not pleasing before the Lord?
