And Mosheh heard, and it was approvable before him, and he sent out a crier through the camp, saying, I am he from whom the rite hath been hidden, and Aharon my brother hath brought its remembrance to me.

וְיַת אִלֵין מִינַיָיא תְּשַׁקְצוּן מִן עוֹפָא דְלֵית לְהוֹן צִיבְעָא יְתֵירָא וּדְלֵית לֵיהּ זְפַקְתָּא וּדְקוּרְקְבָנֵיהּ לֵיתוֹי מַקְלִיף לָא יִתְאַכְלוּן שִׁיקְצָא הִינוּן יַת נִישְׁרָא וְיַת עוּזָא וְיַת בַּר גַזָא


And these kinds of birds must be abominated: those not a finger long, or that have no vesicle (zephaq), or whose crop (kurkeban, ingluvies) peeleth not away, are not to be eaten they are abomination; the eagle, the black eagle, and the osprey,

and the white stork, and the black, after his kind and the woodcock, and the bat.

And every flying reptile that goeth upon four, the fly species, the wasp (or hornet) species, and the bee species shall be an abomination to you: nevertheless of honey of the bee you may eat.

יַת אִלֵין מִינַיָיא מִנְהוֹן תֵּיכְלוּן יַת גוּבָאֵי לִזְנֵיהּ וְיַת רְשׁוּנָא לִזְנֵיהּ וְיַת נִיפוֹלָא לִזְנֵיהּ וְיַת כַּרְזְבָא דְהוּא נִדוּנָא לִזְנֵיהּ


Of these kinds of them you may eat: the wingless locust after his kind, and the bald locust after his kind, the serpent-killer after his kind, and the karzeba, which is the palmerworm, after his kind.

וּלְאִלֵין תִּסְתַּאֲבוּן כָּל דְיִקְרַב בִּנְבֵילְתְּהוֹן יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


and by them you would be defiled: whosoever toucheth their carcase shall be unclean until evening.

And these also to you are such as defile; the blood, the skin, and the flesh of every reptile that creepeth upon the ground: the weasel, the mouse, black, red, and white, and the toad, after his kind;

וְכָל מִדַעַם דְיִפּוֹל עֲלוֹי מִנְהוֹן בְּמוֹתְהוֹן דְאֵיבָרַיָא דִידְהוֹן דְאִיתְפְּשַׁח מִנְהוֹן יְהֵי מְסָאָב כָּל מָאן דְקֵיסָא אוֹ לְבוּשׁ אוֹ צַלָא אוֹ שַק כָּל מָאן דְתִתְעֲבִיד בְּהוֹן עִיבִידְתָּא בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי יִתְעַל וִיהֵי מְסָאָב לְכָל צְרוֹךְ עַד רַמְשָׁא וְיִדְכֵּי


And whatever upon which any part of their dead body may fall, as their members when separated from them, shall be unclean; every vessel of wood, or garment, or leather, or sack, anything in which work is done, in four measures of water it shall be dipped, and be unclean for use until evening, when it shall be purified.

And any earthen vessel into which any of them may fall, any vessel in which they may be, shall be unclean and be broken;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:34)

all food for eating upon which (such) water cometh shall be unclean, and any fluid which is used for drinking in any such vessel shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:35)


וְכָל מִדַעַם דְיַפִּיל מִנְבֵילְתְּהוֹן עֲלוֹי יְהֵי מְסָאָב תַּנוּרִין וְתַפְיַין יִסְתְּרוּן מְסָאָבִין הִינוּן וּמְסָאָבִין יְהוֹן לְכוֹן


And any thing upon which a part of their carcass may fall shall be unclean, whether ovens or pans they shall be broken, they are defiled and shall be unclean to you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:36)


לְחוֹד עֵינַוָון וְגוֹבִין בֵּית כְּנִישׁוּת מַיָין נַבְעִין יְהֵי דְכֵי בְּרַם דִי יִקְרַב בִּנְבֵילְתְּהוֹן בְּגוֹ מַיָא הָאִילְיֵין יְהֵי מְסָאָב


But fountains and cisterns, the place of the collection of running waters, shall be clean: but he who toucheth the carcase of any of those things (that may have fallen) into the water shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:37)

And if any part of their carcase fall in the way upon seed that is to be sown, that which is sown dry shall be clean;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:38)

but if the carcase of any of them fall upon water that is put upon the seed when so wetted, the seed is unclean to you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:39)


וַאֲרוּם אִין יִתְפְּשַׁח אֵיבְרָא וִימוּת מִן בְּעִירָא דַכְיָא דְחַזְיָא לְכוֹן לְמֵיכוֹל דְיִקְרַב בִּנְבֵילְתָּהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:40)


וּדְיֵכוֹל מִנְבֵילְתָּהּ יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וּדְיוֹסִיט יַת נְבֵילְתָּהּ יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:41)


וְכָל רִיחֲשָׁא דִרְחֵישׁ עַל אַרְעָא שִׁיקְצָא הוּא לָא יִתְאֲכֵיל


And every reptile that creepeth on the ground is an abomination, it shall not be eaten.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:42)


כָּל דִמְהַלֵךְ עַל מָעוֹי וְכָל דִמְהַלֵךְ עַל אַרְבַּע מִן חִיוְיָא וְעַד נַדַל דְמַסְגֵי רִיגְלִין בְּכָל רִיחֲשָׁא דִרְחֵישׁ עַל אַרְעָא לָא תֵיכְלוּנוּן אֲרוּם שִׁיקְצָא הִינוּן


And whatever goeth upon its belly, and whatever animal crawleth upon four, from the serpent unto the caterpillar which hath many feet, of any reptile that creepeth upon the ground you may not eat, for they are an abomination.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:43)


לָא תְשַׁקְצוּן יַת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן בְּכָל רִיחֲשָׁא דִרְחֵישׁ וְלָא תִסְתַּאֲבוּן בְּהוֹן דִילְמָא תִּסְתַּאֲבוּן פּוֹן בְּהוֹן


You shall not contaminate your souls by any reptile that creepeth, nor defile yourselves with them, lest by them you make yourselves unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:44)


אֲרוּם אֲנָא יְיָ הוּא אֳלָהָכוֹן וְתִתְקַדְשׁוּן וְתֶהֱווֹן קַדִישִׁין אֲרוּם קַדִישׁ אֲנָא וְלָא תִסְתַּאֲבוּן יַת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן בְּכָל רִיחְשָׁא דִרְחֵישׁ עַל אַרְעָא


For I am the Lord your God; therefore sanctify yourselves, and be holy, for I am Holy, and defile not your souls by any reptile that creepeth upon the ground:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:45)


אֲרוּם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ דְאַסֵיקִית יַתְכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מִן אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מְטוּל לְמֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן לֵאלָהָא וְתֶהֱווּן קַדִישִׁין אֲרוּם קַדִישׁ אֲנָא


for I am the Lord who have brought you up free from the land of Mizraim, that I may be a God to you; and you may be holy, for I am Holy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:46)


דָא הוּא גְזֵירַת אוֹרַיְיתָא דִבְעִירָא וְעוֹפָא וְכָל נַפְשַׁת חַיְיתָא דְרִחֲשָׁא בְּמַיָא וּלְכָל נַפְשָׁא דִרְחִישָׁא עַל אַרְעָא


This is the decree of the law concerning beasts, and birds, and every living animal that creepeth upon the ground;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 11:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/11:47)


לְאַפְרָשָׁא בֵּינֵי מְסָאֲבָא וּבֵינֵי דַכְיָא בֵּינֵי חַיְיתָא דְמִיכַשְׁרָא לְאִיתְאַכְלָא וּבֵינֵי חַיְיתָא דְלָא מִיכַשְׁרָא לְאִיתְאַכְלָא


for making distinction between the unclean and the clean; between the animal whose flesh may be eaten, and the animal whose flesh may not be eaten.



## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר אִתְּתָא אֲרוּם תַעֲדֵי וְתֵילִיד בִּיר דְכַר וּתְהֵי מְסָאָבָא שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין הֵי כְיוֹמֵי רִיחוּק סְאוּבְתָּהּ הֵיכְדֵין תִּסְתָּאֵב


Speak with the sons of Israel, saying: When a woman hath conceived and borne a male child, she shall be unclean seven days, as the days of the removal of her uncleanness shall she be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:3)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְמִינָאֵי תִּשְׁתְּרֵי וּבָרָא יִתְגְזַר בְּשַר עָרְלָתֵיהּ


But on the eighth day she shall be loosed, and her child shall be circumcised in the flesh of his foreskin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:4)


וּתְלָתִין וּתְלָתָא יוֹמִין רְצִיפִין תְּהֵא כָּל דְמָהָא דַכְיָין בְּרַם בְּכָל קוּדְשַׁיָא לָא תִקְרַב וּלְבֵי מַקְדְשָׁא לָא תֵיעוֹל עַד זְמַן מִישְׁלַם יוֹמֵי דְכוּתָהּ


And thirty and three continuous days she shall have for the purification of the whole blood; but she must not touch things sacred, nor come into the sanctuary until the time when the days of her purification be completed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:5)


וְאִין בְּרַת נוּקְבָא תְלִיד וּתְהֵי מְסָאֲבָא אַרְבֵּסְרֵי יוֹמִין רְצִיפִין הֵי כְּרִיחוּקָהּ וּבַחֲמֵיסַר תִּישְׁתְּרֵי וְשִׁיתִּין וְשִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין רְצִיפִין תְהֵי עַל דְמָהָא דַכְיָין


And if she hath borne a daughter, she shall be unclean fourteen continuous days according to (the law of) her separation; and on the fifteenth she shall be released; but sixty and six continuous days shall she have for the (full) purification of the blood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:6)


וּבְמִשְׁלַם יוֹמֵי דְכִיתָא לִבְרָא אוֹ לִבְרַתָּא תַּיְיתֵי אִימַר בַּר שַׁתְיָא לַעֲלָתָא וְגוֹזָל בַּר יוֹנָן אוֹ שַׁפְנִינָא לְחַטָאתָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְוַת כַּהֲנָא


And when the days of her purification are completed for the son or the daughter, she shall bring a lamb of its year for a burnt offering, and a young pigeon or a turtle dove for a sin offering, unto the priest at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:7)


וִיקַרְבִינָהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלָהּ כַּהֲנָא וְתִידְכֵּי מַבּוּעַ תְּרֵין דְמָהָא דָא הוּא אוֹרַיְיתָא דִיְלִידְתָּא לִדְכַר אוֹ לְנוּקְבָא


and the priest shall offer it before the Lord and make atonement for her; then shall she be purified from either source of (her) blood. This is the law of the purification of her who hath borne a son or a daughter.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/12:8)


וְאִין לָא הַשְׁכַּח יְדָהּ הֵי כְמִיסַת לְמִיתוֹיָיא אִימְרָא וְתִיסַב תְּרֵין שַׁפְנִינִין אוֹ תְרֵין גוֹזְלִין בְּנֵי יוֹנָן חַד לַעֲלָתָא וְחַד לְחַטָאתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלָהּ כַּהֲנָא וְתִידְכֵּי


But if she find not her hand sufficient to bring a lamb, let her bring two turtle doves or two young pigeons; one for the burnt offering, and one for the sin offering, and the priest shall make atonement for her, and she shall be clean.



## Chapter 13

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:2)


בַּר נַשׁ אֲרוּם יְהֵי בִּמְשָׁךְ בִּסְרֵיהּ שׁוּמָא זְקִיפָא אוֹ קְלוֹפֵי אוֹ בַהַקֵי וִיהֵי בִּמְשַׁךְ בִּישְרֵיהּ לְמִיכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ וִיתֵיתֵי לְוַת אַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא אוֹ לְוַת חַד מִבְּנוֹי כַּהֲנַיָא


If a man have in the skin of his flesh a rising tumour or a white spot, and it be in the skin of his flesh (as) the plague of leprosy, let him be brought unto Aharon the priest, or to one of the priests his sons.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:3)


וְיֶחֱמֵי כַּהֲנָא יַת מַכְתְּשָׁא בִּמְשַׁךְ בִּישְרָא וְשַעֲרָא בְּמַכְתְּשָׁא אִיתְהַפִּיךְ לְחִיוָור וְחֵיזְיוּ דְמַכְתְּשָׁא עַמִיק לִמְחַוָור כְּתַלְגָא יַתִּיר מִמְשַׁךְ בִּישְרֵיהּ מַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּתָא הוּא וְיַחֲמִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא וִיסָאֵיב יָתֵיהּ


And the priest shall look at the plague in the skin of the flesh,--and if the hair of the stricken place be turned to whiteness, and the appearance of the plague be deeper (than the surface), and be whiter than the skin of his flesh, like snow, it is the plague of leprosy; and the priest having inspected him shall make him to be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:4)


וְאִם בַּהַקֵי חִיוַורְתָּא כְּסִידָא הוּא בִּמְשַׁךְ בִּישְרֵיהּ וְעָמִיק לֵית חֵיזְוֵיהּ לִמְחַוֵור כְּתַלְגָא יַתִּיר מִן מַשְׁכָא וּשְעָרָא לָא אִיתְהַפִּיךְ לְחִיוַור כְּסִידָא וְיַסְגֵר כַּהֲנָא יַת מַכְתְּשָׁא שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין


But if the bright spot be white like chalk in the skin of his flesh, and the appearance of it be not deep, with whiteness like snow rather than skin, the hair, too, not being turned to whiteness like chalk, the priest shall shut up him who is plagued seven days;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:5)


וְיֶחֱמִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה וְהָא מַכְתָּשָׁא קָם כַּד הֲוָה לָא הֲלִיךְ פִּיסְיוֹנָא דְמַכְתָּשָׁא בְּמַשְׁכָא וְיַסְגְרִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין תִּנְיָינוּת


and the priest shall inspect him on the seventh day, and, behold, if the plague stand as it was, and have not gone on wider in the skin, the priest shall shut him up a second seven days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:6)


וְיֶחֱמֵי כַּהֲנָא יָתֵיהּ בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה תִּנְיָנוּת וְהָא עַמְיָא מַכְתָּשָׁא לָא הֲלִיךְ פִּסְיוֹנָא דְמַכְתָּשָׁא בְּמַשְׁכָא וִידַכִּינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא קְלוּפֵי מִטַפְלָא הוּא וְיִצְבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיִדְכֵּי


And the priest shall inspect him the second seventh day; and, behold, if the plague hath become darker, and hath not gone wider in the skin, the priest shall make him to be clean; it is an obstinate sore, and he shall wash his clothes and be clean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:7)


וְאִם הֲלוּכֵי הֲלִיךְ פִּסְיוֹנָא דְקִילוּפֵי דְמִטַפְלָא בְּמַשְׁכָא בָּתַר דְאִיתְחַמֵי לְכַּהֲנָא לִדְכוּתֵיהּ וְיִתְחֲמֵי תִנְיָינוּת לְכַהֲנָא


But if the inveterate sore widen in the skin after he had been shown to the priest who had pronounced him clean, let him a second time be seen by the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:8)


וְיֶחֱמֵי כַּהֲנָא וְלָא הֲלִיכַת פִּיסְיוֹנָא דְקִילוּפֵי מִטַפְלָא בְּמַשְׁכָא וְיַסְאֲבִינֵיהּ כַהֲנָא מְטוֹל דִסְגִירוּתָא הוּא


And the priest shall look; and, behold, if the widening of the inveterate sore hath gone on in the skin, the priest shall make him unclean; for it is the leprosy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:9)


מַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ אֲרוּם יְהֵי בְּבַר נַשׁ וִיתֵיתֵיהּ לְוַת כַּהֲנָא


When the plague of leprosy is upon a man, let him be brought to the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:10)


וְיֶחֱמֵי כַּהֲנָא וְהָא שׁוּמָא זְקִיפָא חִיוְרָא בְּמוֹשְׁכָא כַּעֲמַר נָקִי וְהִיא הַפְכַת שַעֲרָא לְמֶחֱוַר כִּקְרוּם בֵּיעֲתָא וְרוֹשַׁם בִּישְרָא חַיָיא בְּשׁוּמָא


And the priest shall observe; and, behold, if there be a white tumour rising on the skin like pure wool, and the hair be turned to whiteness as the white of an egg, and the sign of quick flesh be in the tumour,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/13:11)


סְגִירוּתָא עֲתִּיקוּתָא הוּא בִּמְשַׁךְ בִּישְרֵיהּ וְיַסְאִיבִינֵיהּ וְיַחְלְטִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא לָא יַסְגְרִינֵיהּ אֲרוּם מְסָאָב הוּא


it is an inveterate leprosy in the skin of his flesh; and the priest shall adjudge and pronounce him unclean, but not shut him up, for he is (known to be) unclean.

וְאִין מִסְגַיָא תַּסְגֵי סְגִירוּתָא בְּמוֹשְׁכָא וְתַחְפֵי סְגִירוּתָא יַת כָּל מְשַׁךְ בִּישְרֵיהּ מֵרֵישֵׁיהּ וְעַד רִיגְלוֹי לְכָל חֵיזְיוּ דְחַמְיַין עֵינוֹי דְכַהֲנָא וּמִתְכַוְונָן בֵּין לִמְדַכְיָא וּבֵין לְסָאֳבָא


Yet if the leprosy increasing increaseth in the skin, and the leprosy covereth all the skin of his flesh, from his head even to his feet, in whatever part the eyes of the priest may look on, in deliberating between cleanness and uncleanness,

וְיֶחֱמֵי כַּהֲנָא וְהָא חְפַת סְגִירוּתָא יַת כָּל בִּישְרֵיהּ וְיִדְכֵּי יַת מַכְתָּשָׁא כּוּלֵיהּ אִתְהֲפִּיךְ לְחִיוַור דְכֵי הוּא


the priest shall consider; and, behold, if the leprosy covereth all his flesh, the plagued man shall be (pronounced) to be clean: all of him is turned to whiteness, he is clean.

And if a man have in his skin an ulcer, and it hath healed;

but in the place of the ulcer there hath come a white rising tumour, or a bright fixed spot, (in colour) white mixed with red; he shall be seen by the priest.

וְיֶחֱמֵי כַּהֲנָא וְהָא חֵיזְוָהָא מַכִּיךְ מִן מוֹשְׁכָא לְמֶחֱוַור וּשְעָרָהּ אִתְהַפִּיךְ לְמֶחֱוַור וְיַסְאִיבִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא מְטוּל דְמַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּתָא הוּא בְּשִׁיחֲנָא סַגִיאָת


And the priest shall look; and, behold, if the appearance of it be deeper than the skin, and it becometh white, and the hair is turned white, the priest shall make him to be unclean; for it is a plague of leprosy which increaseth in the ulcer.

Then shall the priest look upon the scurf on the seventh day; and, behold, if the scar hath not gone on in breadth in the skin, and its appearance is not deeper nor becoming whiter than the skin, the priest shall make him to be clean; and he shall wash his clothes and be clean.

But should the breadth of the scar go on in the skin after his purification,

This is the law for the plague of leprosy in a garment of woollen or of linen, or the warp or the woof, or anything of skin, to make it to be clean or to be unclean.



## Chapter 14

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying,

And let him sprinkle it upon the face of him who is to be cleansed of the leprosy seven times, and cleanse him; and send forth. the living bird over the face of the field. But the bird that had been killed the priest shall bury in the presence of the leper.

וְיִכּוֹס טַבְּחָא יַת אִימְרָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיִסַב כַּהֲנָא מִן אַדְמָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיִתֵּן עַל גְדֵירָא מִיצְעָא דְאוּדְנָא דְמִידְכֵּי יְמִינָא וְעַל פּוּרְקָא מִיצְעָא דְיָדֵיהּ יְמִינָא וְעַל פּוּרְקָא מִיצְעָא דְרִיגְלֵיהּ יְמִינָא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:51)


וְיִסַב יַת קֵיסָא דְאַרְזָא וְיַת אֵיזוֹבָא וְיַת צְבַע זְהוֹרֵי וְיַת צִפְּרָא חַיְיתָא וְיִטְמוֹשׁ יָתֵיהּ בְּאַדְמָא דְצִיפְּרָא נְכִיסְתָּא וּבְמֵי מַבּוּעַ וְיַדֵי לְבֵיתָא שְׁבַע זִמְנִין


and he shall take the cedarwood and the hyssop and the scarlet and the living bird, and dip them in the blood of the bird that had been killed and in the spring water, and sprinkle the house seven times.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:52)


וְיִדְכֵּי יַת בֵּיתָא בְּאַדְמָא דְצִפְּרָא חַיְיתָא וּבְקֵיסָא דְאַרְזָא וּבְאֵיזוֹבָא וּבִצְבַע זְהוֹרֵי


And he shall purify the house with the blood, with the living bird, and with the cedarwood, and with the hyssop, and with the scarlet.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:53)


וְיִפְטוֹר יַת צִיפּוֹרָא חַיְיתָא לְמִבָּרָא לְקַרְתָּא עַל אַנְפֵּי חַקְלָא וִיכַפֵּר עַל בֵּיתָא וְיִדְכֵּי בְּרַם אִין אִיטוּמוֹס בַּיְיתָא לְמִילְקָא תוּב בְּצוֹרְעָא תַיְיבָא צִפּוֹרָא תַּמָּן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וּמִתְכַּשְׁרָא לְמֵיכְלָא וְיַת צִיפְּרָא נְכִיסְתָּא הֲוָה מִקְבַּר כַּהֲנָא בְּמֵיחְמֵי מָרֵי בֵיתָא


And the living bird he shall send forth out of the town upon the face of the field, and shall atone for the house, and it shall be clean. But if it is to be that the house will be again struck with leprosy, the bird on that day will return, and may be fit for food. But the bird that was killed shall the priest bury in the presence of the owner of the house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:54)


דָא תֶהֱוֵי גְזֵירַת אַחְוַיַית אוֹרַיְיתָא לְכָל מַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּתָא וּלְנִיתְקָא


This is the decree of instruction in the law for every plague of leprosy and scorbutus,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:55)


וּלְצוּרְעָא דִלְבוּשָׁא וּלְבֵיתָא


and for leprosy in apparel, or in a house;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:56)


וּלְשׁוּמָא וְלִקְלוֹפֵי וּלְבַהֲקֵי


and for tumours, scars, and inflamed blotches.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 14:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/14:57)


לְמַלְפָא כַהֲנָא לְעַמָא בֵּין יוֹמָא קְבִילָא דְלָא לְמֵיחְמֵי בֵּיהּ מַכְתְּשָׁא לְבֵין יוֹמָא נְהִירָא וּבֵין בַּר נְשָׁא מְסָאָבָא לְבֵין בַּר נְשָׁא דַכְיָא דָא תֶהֱוֵי גְזֵירַת אַחְוַיַית מַכְתַּשׁ צוֹרְעָתָא


That the priest may teach the people to discern between the day of darkness in which they may not be able to see the plague, and the day of light; and between a man who is unclean and a man who is clean. This shall be the decree of instruction for the leprosy.



## Chapter 15

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרן לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh and with Aharon, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:2)


מַלִילוּ עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימְרוּן לְהוֹן גְבַר טְלֵי אוֹ גְבַר סִיב אַרוּם יְהֵי דָאִיב מִבִּשְרֵיהּ דְוָיֵיהּ חֲמָא תְּלַת זִימְנֵי מְסָאָב הוּא


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: A man, whether young or old, who hath a defluxion from his flesh, when he hath seen it three times, is unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:3)


וְדָא תְּהֵי סְאוֹבְתֵּיהּ גַווֹן חִיוַור בְּדָיֵיהּ חֲרִיר בִּישְרֵיהּ יַת דְיֵיהּ אוֹ דְאִיסְתַתֵּם בִּישְרֵיהּ מִדוֹוֵויהּ סְאוֹבְתֵּיהּ הוּא


And this shall be his uncleanness, the appearance of the colour of white in his defluxion inflaming, the defluxion of his flesh; or when his flesh hath stopped from his defluxion, it is his uncleanness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:4)


כָּל מַשְׁכְּבָא דִמְיַחֵד לִמִישְׁכּוֹב עֲלוֹי דוֹבָנָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב וְכָל מָנָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְמֵיתַב עֲלוֹי יְהֵי מְסָאָב


Every bed on which one who hath such defluxion lieth shall be unclean; and every thing on which such an one sitteth shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:5)


וּגְבַר דִי יִקְרַב בְּמַשְׁכְּבֵיהּ יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And the man who toucheth his bed shall wash his clothes, and wash himself in forty seahs of water, and shall be unclean until evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:6)


וְדִי יְתִיב עַל מָאנָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְמֵיתַב עֲלוֹי דוֹבָנָא יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And whoever may sit upon a thing whereon such an one who hath an issue hath sat, let him wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:7)


וּדְיִקְרַב בִּבְשַר דוֹבָנָא יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:8)


וַאֲרוּם אִין יָרִיק דוֹבָנָא בְּדַכְיָא וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And if he who hath an issue spit upon any one who is clean, let him wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:9)

And every girdle or saddle upon which he who hath an issue rideth shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:10)

And whoever toucheth any thing that hath been under him shall be unclean until evening; and he who carrieth them shall wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:11)


וְכָל מִדַעַם דְיִקְרַב בֵּיהּ דוֹבָנָא וִידוֹי לָא שְׁטַף בְּמַיָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב וְאִין גַבְרָא הוּא יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And whoever toucheth him who hath the issue, and washeth not his hands in water, shall be unclean; if he be a man, he shall wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until the evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:12)


וּמָאן דְפָחַר דְיִקְרַב בְּגַוֵיהּ דוֹבָנָא יִתְבַּר וְכָל מָאן דְקֵיסָא יִשְׁתְּטֵיף בְּמַיָא


And any vessel of earthenware whose inside may have been touched by him who hath the issue shall be broken; and any vessel of wood shall be washed in water.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:13)


וַאֲרוּם אִין פָּסַק מִדוֹבָנָא דוֹבֵיהּ וְיִמְנֵי לֵיהּ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין לִדְכוּתֵיהּ וְיִצְבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיִסְחֵי בִּישְרֵיהּ בְּמֵי מַבּוּעַ וְיִדְכֵּי


But if he who hath had the issue shall have ceased from it, he shall number to himself seven days for his purification, and wash his clothes, and bathe his flesh in spring water, to be clean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:14)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה יִסַב לֵיהּ תְּרֵין שַׁפְנִינִין רַבְרְבִין אוֹ תְּרֵין גוֹזְלִין בְּנֵי יָווֹן וְיַיְתֵי יַתְהוֹן לִקֳדָם יְיָ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא וְיִתְּנִינוּן לְכַהֲנָא


And on the eighth day let him take for himself two large turtle doves, or two young pigeons, and bring them before the Lord at the gate of the tabernacle of ordinance, and deliver them to the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:15)

And the priest shall make one a sin offering and one a burnt offering, and the priest shall atone for him before the Lord, and he shall be cleansed from his issue.

But if a man sin through ignorance and seed goeth from him, let him wash all his flesh in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.

וְאִתְּתָא תִּנְיָיתָא דִי יְשַׁמֵשׁ גְבַר עִמָהּ שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא וְיִסְחוֹן בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהוֹן מְסָאֲבִין עַד רַמְשָׁא


and secondly, a woman with whom a man lieth shall wash in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:19)


וְאִנְתְּתָא אֲרוּם תְּהֵי דַיְיבָא אֲדַם סִמוּק וְאוּכָם וּמוֹרִיק הֵי כְגַוָן זָעְפָרָנָא אוֹ הֵי כְמוֹ דְגַרְגִישְׁתָּא אוֹ הֵי כִמְזַג חֲמַר סִימוּק בִּתְרֵין חוּלְקִין דְמוֹי אֲדַם מְסָאָב הֲוֵי דוֹבָא בְּבִישְרָא שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תְּהֵי יָתְבָא בְּרִיחוּקָהּ כָּל דְיִקְרַב בָּהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And if a woman hath an issue of blood, red or dark, yellow as saffron, or water of clay, or as red wine mixed with two parts of water, she hath an uncleanness of blood in her flesh; she shall dwell apart seven days; any one who toucheth her shall be unclean until evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:20)


וְכָל מִדַעַם דִמְיַיחֵד לְמִשְׁכַּב עֲלוֹי בְּעִידוֹנֵי רִיחוּקָהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב וְכָל מִדַעַם דִמְיַיחֵד לְמֵיתַב עֲלוֹי בְּעִידוֹנֵי רִיחוּקָהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב


Whatever such an one shall lie upon during the time of her separation shall be unclean; and whatever such an one sitteth upon during the time of her separation shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:21)


וְכָל מַאן דְיִקְרַב בְּמִשְׁכָּבָהּ יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And whoever toucheth her bed shall wash his clothes, and bathe himself with forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:22)


וְכָל דְיִקְרַב בְּכָל מָאנָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְמֵיתַב עֲלוֹי יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And whoever toucheth any thing upon which such an one hath sat shall wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:23)


וְאִין עַל מִשְׁכָּבָא הוּא כְּדוֹבָא דְגוּפֵיהּ אוֹ עַל מָאנָא דְהִיא יַתְבָא עַל קְצָתֵיהּ בִּזְמַן מִיקְרְבֵיהּ בֵּיהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And if the effusion of her body be upon her bed, or on a thing upon any part of which she sitteth, what time any one toucheth it, he shall be unclean until evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:24)


וְאִין שַׁמָשָׁא יְשַׁמֵשׁ גְבַר עִמָהּ וְתֶהֱוֵי רִיחוּקָהּ עֲלוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין וְכָל מִשְׁכָּבָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְמִשְׁכְּבֵי עֲלוֹי יְהֵי מְסָאָב


If a man lie with her in the time of her separation, he shall be unclean seven days; and any bed upon which he lieth shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:25)


וְאִינְתְּתָא אֲרוּם יִדוֹב דוֹב אַדְמָא יוֹמִין תְּלָתָא בְּלָא אֲשׁוּנֵי רִיחוּקָהּ אוֹ אֲרוּם מִידוֹב בָּתַר יוֹמֵי רִיחוּקָהּ כָּל יוֹמֵי דוֹב סְאוֹבְתָּא תְּהֵי מְסָאָבָא לְדִמְשַׁמֵשׁ עִמָהּ מְטוֹל דִמְסָאָבָא הִיא


But a woman who hath a defluxion of blood three days beyond the time of her separation, or when it floweth after the days of her separation, all the days of the uncleanness of her defluxion shall she be unclean; he who lieth with her shall be unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:26)


וְכָל מִשְׁכְּבָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְמִשְׁכּוֹב עֲלוֹי כָּל יוֹמֵי דוֹבָא הֵי כְמַשְׁכְּבָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְרִיחוּקָהּ יִתְחַשֵׁב לָהּ וְכָל מָאנָא דִמְיַיחֵד לְמֵיתַב עֲלוֹי מְסָאָב יְהֵי הֵי כְרִיחוּק סְאוֹבְתָּהּ


And any bed upon which such any one lieth all the days of her defluxion shall be as the bed which was accounted hers during the time of her separation, and any thing upon which such an one sitteth shall be unclean as the uncleanness of her separation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:27)


וְכָל מַאן דְיִקְרַב בְּהוֹן יְהֵי מְסָאָב וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And whoever toucheth those (things) shall be unclean, and shall wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until the evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:28)


וְאִין אִידַכְיַית מִדוֹבָהּ וְתִמְנֵי לָהּ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין וּבָתַר כְּדֵין תִּטְבוֹל בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וְתִדְכֵי


But when she is cleansed from her issue, let her number to herself seven days, and afterwards wash in forty seahs of water, and be clean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:29)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְּמִינָאָה תִּסַב תְּרֵין שַׁפְנִינִין רַבְרְבִין אוֹ תְּרֵין גוֹזְלִין בְּנֵי יוֹוָן וְתֵיתֵי יַתְהוֹן לְוַת כַּהֲנָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And on the seventh day, let her take for herself two turtle doves, or two young pigeons eons, and bring them to the priest, at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:30)


וְיַעֲבֵיד כַּהֲנָא יַת חַד חַטָאתָא וְיַת חַד עֲלָתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלָהּ כַּהֲנָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִדוֹב סְאוֹבְתָּהּ


and the priest shall make one a sin offering, and the other a burnt offering and the priest shall make atonement before the Lord, on account of the defluxion of her uncleanness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:31)


וְתִפְרְשׁוּן יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן סְאוֹבְתֵּיהוֹן וְיִתְפַּרְשׁוּן מִן נְשֵׁיהוֹן סְמוֹךְ לְוֶוסְתְּהוֹן וְלָא יִסְתַּקֵף עֲלֵיהוֹן דִימוּתוּן מְטוּל סְאוֹבְתְּהוֹן בְּסָאֲבֵיהוֹן יַת מַשְׁכְּנִי דְתַמָן אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתִּי שַׁרְיָא בֵּינֵיהוֹן


So shall you separate the children of Israel from their uncleanness, and make them to be separate from their wives at the time of their seclusion, and to give not occasion that they die for their uncleanness in defiling My tabernacle, where the glory of My Shekinah dwelleth among them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:32)


דָא גְזֵירַת אַחֲוָיַית דוֹבָנָא וּמִן דְיִפּוֹק מִנֵיהּ שׁוּכְבַת זַרְעָא לְאִיסְתַּאֲבָא בָּהּ


This is the decree of instruction for him who hath a defluxion, and for him whose seed goeth forth and defileth him;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/15:33)


וְלִדְסִיאוֹבְתָּא בְּעִידוּנֵי רִיחוּקָהּ וּלְדִדַיֵיב יַת דִיבֵיהּ לִדְכַר אוֹ לְנוּקְבָא וְלִגְבַר דִי יְשַׁמֵשׁ עִם מְסָאָבְתָּא כָּל אִלֵין יֶהֱוָון זְהִירִין בְּסוֹאֲבַתְהוֹן וְכַד יִדְכּוּן יַיְתוּן יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן מְטוֹל לִמְכַפְּרָא עֲלֵיהוֹן


and for her who is unclean in the time of her separation, and for any one who hath an issue, whether male or female, and for a man who lieth with the unclean. All these shall be advised of their uncleanness, and, when purified, shall bring the oblations that make atonement for them.



## Chapter 16

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה מִן בָּתַר דְמִיתוּ תְּרֵין בְּנוֹי דְאַהֲרן כַּהֲנַיָא רַבְרְבַיָא בִּזְמַן קְרוֹבֵיהוֹן אֶשָׁא בַּרְיָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִיתוּ בְּאֵשָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, after that the two sons of Aharon the high priest had died (or, the priests the two elder sons of Aharon had died) at the time of their offering extraneous fire (aisha baria) before the Lord; died they by the flaming fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:2)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה מַלֵיל עִם אַהֲרן אָחוּךְ וְלָא יְהֵי עָלֵיל בְּכָל עִידַן לְקוּדְשָׁא מִן לְגֵיו לְפַרְגוֹדָא לִקְדָם כַּפּוּרְתָּא אֲרוּם בַּעֲנָנִי אִיקְרֵי שְׁכִינְתִּי מִתְגְלֵי עַל בֵּית כַּפּוֹרֵי


And the Lord said unto Mosheh: Speak with Aharon thy brother, that he enter not at any time into the holy place within the veil before the mercy-seat; for the cloud of the glory of My Shekinah is revealed over the place of the mercy-seat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:3)


בַּהֲדָא מִידָה יְהֵי עָלִיל אַהֲרן לְקוּדְשָׁא בְּתוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי דְלָא עֵירוּבִין לְקָרְבַּן חַטָאתָא וּדְכַר לַעֲלָתָא


This shall be the rite (mida) for the entering of Aharon into the holy place. With a young bullock, having no mixture, for the sin offering, and a ram for the burnt offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:4)


כִּיתוּנָא דְבוּץ מִילַת קוּדְשָׁא יִלְבַּשׁ וְאַווֹרְקְסִין דְבוּץ מִילַת יְהוֹן עַל בִּישְרֵיהּ וּבְקִמוּרָא דְבוּץ מִילַת יֵצֶר וּמַצְנִיפָא דְבוּץ מִילַת נְטִיכַס בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא הִינוּן בְּרַם בִּלְבוּשֵׁי דַהֲבָא לָא יְהֵי עָלֵיל מְטוֹל דְלָא יִדְכַּר חוֹבַת עֵיגְלָא דְדַהֲבָא וּבִזְמַן מֵעֲלֵיהּ יַסְחֵי יַת בִּישְרֵיהּ בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי וְיַלְבֵּישִׁינוּן


With the vestments of fine linen, the holy robe, shall he be dressed, and linen drawers shall be upon his flesh, and with the girdle of fine linen shall he be bound, and the mitre of fine linen shall be ordained for his head. These are the holy garments; but with the golden robes he shall not enter, that there be not brought to memory the sin of the golden calf; and at the time when he is to enter he shall wash his flesh in forty seahs of water, and attire himself with them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:5)


וּמִן כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יִסַב תְּרֵין צְפִירֵי בְּנֵי עִזֵי דְלָא עֵירוּבִין לְקָרְבַּן חַטָאתָא וּדְכַר חַד לַעֲלָתָא


And from the congregation of the sons of Israel let him take two kids of the goats, without mixture, for a sin offering, and one ram for a burnt offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:6)


וְיַקְרֵיב אַהֲרן יַת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָאתָא דְהוּא מִן מָמוֹנֵיהּ וִיכַפֵּר בְּאִשְׁתְּעוּת מִילַיָא עֲלוֹי וְעַל אֵינַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ


And Aharon shall offer the bullock of the sin offering which (hath been purchased) with his own money, and make an atonement with words of confession for himself and for the men of his house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:7)


וְיִסַב יַת תְּרֵין צְפִירִין וִיקֵם יַתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And he shall take the two goats, and cause them to stand before the Lord, at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:29)


וּתְהֵי דָא לְכוֹן לִקְיַים עֲלָם בְּיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה הוּא יֶרַח תִּשְׁרִי בְּעַשְרָא יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא תְּעַנוּן יַת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן מִן מֵיכְלָא וּמִן מִשְׁתַּיָא וּמִן הַוְיַית בָנֵי וְתַמְרוּקָא וּמְסָנָא וְתַשְׁמִישׁ עַרְסָא וְכָל עִיבִידְתָּא לָא תַעַבְדוּן יַצִיבָא וְגִיוֹרָא דְיִתְגַיֵיר בֵּינֵיכוֹן


And this shall be to you for an everlasting statute: in the seventh month, it is the month Tishri, on the tenth day of the month, you shall humble your souls, (abstaining) from food, and from drinks, and from the use of the bath, and from rubbing, and from sandals, and from the practice of the bed: nor shall you do any work, neither the native-born nor the stranger who dwelleth among you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:30)


אֲרוּם בְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין יְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיכוֹן לְדַכָּאָה יַתְכוֹן מִכָּל חוֹבֵיכוֹן וְאַתּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ תּוֹדוּן סוּרְחָנוּתְכוֹן וְתִידְכּוּן


For on this day be shall make ATONEMENT for you to cleanse you from all your sins; and you shall confess your transgressions before the Lord, and shall be clean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:31)


שַׁבָּת שַׁבַּתָּא הוּא לְכוֹן כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן וּתְעַנוּן יַת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן קְיַים עֲלָם


It is a Sabbath of rest to you: no work of business shall you do, but shall humiliate your souls. It is an everlasting statute.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:32)


וִיכַפֵּר כַּהֲנָא דִי רַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ וְדִי קְרַב קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ לְשַׁמָשָׁא תְּחוֹת אָבוֹי וְיִלְבַּשׁ יַת לְבוּשֵׁי בוּץ מִילַת לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא


And the priest who is anointed, and who hath offered his oblation to minister instead of his father, shall be clothed in the robes of fine linen, even the consecrated robes.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/16:33)

And he shall make atonement for the Holy of Holies, and for the tabernacle of ordinance, and for the altar; and for the priests, and for all the people of the congregation, shall he atone, with confession of words.

And this shall be to you for an everlasting statute, to expiate the children of Israel from all their sins, once in the year. And Aharon did as the Lord commanded Mosheh.

וְלִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָא אַיְיתֵיהּ לְקָרָבָא קָרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ קֳדָם מַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ אֲדַם קְטוֹל יִתְחַשֵׁב לְגַבְרָא הַהוּא וּתְהֵי לֵיהּ כְּאִילוּ אֲדַם זַכְּאַי אָשַׁד וְיִשְׁתֵּצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵיהּ


and bringeth it not to the door of the tabernacle of ordinance to offer it an oblation before the Lord, before the tabernacle of the Lord, the blood of slaughter shall be reckoned to that man, and it shall be to him as if he had shed innocent blood, and that man shall be destroyed from his people.

In order that the sons of Israel may bring their sacrifices which they have heretofore killed on the face of the field, they may henceforth bring them before the Lord, at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, unto the priest, and sacrifice their consecrated victims before the Lord.

And thou shalt tell them: A man, whether young or old, of the house of Israel, or of the strangers who sojourn among you, who shall sacrifice a burnt offering, or consecrated oblation,

וְלִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָא יַיְתִינֵיהּ לְמֶעֱבַד יָתֵיהּ קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵיהּ


and bring it not to the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, to be made an oblation before the Lord, that man shall be destroyed from his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:10)


וּגְבַר טְלֵי וּגְבַר סִיב מִבֵּית גְנִיסַת יִשְרָאֵל וּמִן גִיוֹרָא דְמִתְגַיְירוּן לְמֵיתַב בֵּינֵיהוֹן דִי יֵיכוּל כָּל אַדְמָא וְאֶתֵּן יַת פְּנוּיָיתָא לְמֶעֱסוֹק בְּבַר נְשָׁא הַהוּא דִי יֵיכוּל כָּל אַדְמָא וְאֵישֵׁיצֵי יָתֵיהּ מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


A man also, whether young or old, of the house of the family of Israel, or of the strangers who sojourn, in dwelling among them, who shall eat any blood, I will cause employment to turn away (or cease) from that man who eateth any blood, and will destroy him from among his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:11)


אֲרוּם קִיוּם נְפָשׁ כָּל בִּישְרָא בְּאַדְמָא הוּא וַאֲנָא יְהַבְתִּיהָ לְכוֹן לִגְזֵירָתָא דְתִיתְּנוּן אֲדַם נִיכְסָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא מְטוֹל לִמְכַפְּרָא אֲדַם עַל נַפְשַׁתְכוֹן אֲרוּם אֲדַם נִיכְסָא הוּא עַל חוֹבֵי נַפְשָׁא מְכַפֵּר


Because the subsistence of the life of all flesh is in the blood, and I have given it to you for a decree, that you shall bring the blood of the victim unto the altar to make atonement for the blood of your lives, because the blood of the victim is to atone for the guilt of the soul.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:12)


בְּגִין כֵּן אֲמָרִית לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אִיזְדַהֲרוֹן דְכָל בַּר נַשׁ מִנְכוֹן לָא יֵיכוֹל אַדְמָא וְגִיוֹרַיָא דְמִתְגַיְירוּן לְמֵיתַב בֵּינֵיכוֹן לָא יֵיכְלוּן אַדְמָא


Therefore have I said to the sons of Israel, Beware lest any man among you eat the blood. Neither shall the strangers who sojourn by dwelling among you eat the blood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:13)


וּגְבַר טְלֵי אוֹ גְבַר סִיב מִבֵּית גְנִיסַת יִשְרָאֵל וּמִן גִיוֹרַיָא דְיִתְגַיְירוּן לְמֵיתַב בֵּינֵיהוֹן דִי יְצַד צֵידָא חַיְיתָא אוֹ עוֹפָא דְמִיכַשְׁרִין לְמֵיכַל וְיֵישׁוֹד יַת אַדְמֵיהּ בְּנִיכְסָתָא וְאִין לָא מִתְקַלְקְלָא נִיכְסַתֵּיהּ יְכַסִינֵיהּ בְּעַפְרָא


And any man, whether young or old, of the house of the stock of Israel, or of the sojourners who sojourn by dwelling among you, who hunteth venison of beast or fowl proper to be eaten, shall pour out its blood when it is killed; and if what he hath killed be not destroyed (or strangled?), let the blood be covered with dust.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 17:14

אֲרוּם קִיוּם נְפַשׁ כָּל בִּישְרָא אַדְמֵיהּ בְּנַפְשֵׁיהּ הוּא וַאֲמָרִית לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲדַם כָּל בִּישְרָא לָא תֵיכְלוּן אֲרוּם קִיוּם נְפַשׁ כָּל בִּישְרָא אַדְמֵיהּ הוּא כָּל מַן דְיֵכְלִינֵיהּ יִשְׁתֵּיצֵי


Because the subsistence of the life of all flesh is its blood; it is its life; and I have told the sons of Israel, You shall not eat the blood of any flesh; for the subsistence of the life of all flesh is its blood: whosoever among you eateth it shall be destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:15)


וְכָל בַּר נַשׁ דְיֵיכוֹל בִּיסְרָא דִמְטַלֵק בְּקִילְקוּל נִיכְסֵתָא וּבְשַר תְּבִירָא בְּיַצִיבָא וּבְגִיוֹרָא וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָאוִוין דְמוֹי סָאוִוין וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וְיִדְכֵּי


And any man who shall eat flesh which hath been thrown away on account of having been strangled (or corrupted), or the flesh of that which hath been torn, (any man,) whether native or sojourner, shall wash his clothes, and bathe in forty seahs of water, and be unclean until evening when he shall be clean;
