[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/17:16)


וְאִין אַרְשַׁע וְלָא יְצַבַּע וּבִשְרֵיהּ לָא יַסְחֵי וִיקַבֵּל חוֹבֵיהּ


but if he be perverse and will not wash, nor bathe his flesh, he shall bear his transgression.



## Chapter 18

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them, I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:3)


כְּעוֹבְדִין בִּישִׁין דְעַמָא אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם דִי תֵיתְבוּן בָּהּ לָא תַעַבְדוּן וְהֵי כְּעוֹבְדִין בִּישִׁין דְעַמָא דְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן דַאֲנָא מָעִיל יַתְכוֹן לְתַמָן לָא תַעַבְדוּן וּבְנִימוּסֵיהוֹן לָא תְהָכוּן


After the evil work of the people of the land of Mizraim, among whom you have dwelt, you shall not do; so likewise, after the evil work of the people of the land of Kenaan, whither I am bringing you, ye shall not do, neither shall you walk according to their laws;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:4)


יַת סִדְרֵי דִינַי תַעַבְדוּן וְיַת קְיָימַי תִיטְרוּן לְהַלָכָא בְּהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


but you shall perform the orders of My judgments, and observe My statutes to walk in them: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:5)


וְתִטְרוּן יַת קְיָמַי וְיַת סִדְרֵי דִינַי דְאִין יַעֲבֵיד יַתְהוֹן אֵינָשָׁא וְיֵיחֵי בְּהוֹן בְּחַיֵי עַלְמָא וְחוּלְקֵיהּ עִם צַדִיקַיָא אֲנָא יְיָ


And you shall keep My statutes, and the order of My judgments, which if a man do he shall live in them, in the life of eternity, and his portion shall be with the just: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:6)


גְבַר טְלֵי וּגְבַר סִיב לְכָל קְרִיבַת בִּישְרֵיהּ לָא תְקַרְבוּן לְבַזָאָה עִרְיָיתָא בְּתַשְׁמִישְׁתָּא וּבְפִרְסוּמֵי עִרְיָיא אֲנָא יְיָ


No man, either young or old, shall come nigh to any of the kindred of his flesh to dishonour (their) nakedness by carnality, or by the knowledge of their nakedness. I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:7)


עֶרְיַית אָבוּךְ וְעֶרְיַית אִמָךְ לָא תְבַזֵי אִיתָא לָא תְשַׁמֵשׁ עִם אַבְהָא וּגְבַר לָא יְשַׁמֵשׁ עִם אִמֵיהּ אִמָךְ הִיא לָא תְגַלֵי עִרְיָיתָהּ


The nakedness of thy father, or the nakedness of thy mother, thou shall not dishonour. A woman shall not lie with her father, nor a man with his mother; she is thy mother: thou shalt not discover her nakedness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:8)


עֶרְיַית אִתַּת אָבוּךְ לָא תִבְזֵי מְטוּל דְעִרְיְיתָא דְאָבוּךְ הִיא


The nakedness of thy father's wife thou shalt not dishonour, for it is the nakedness of thy father.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:9)


עֶרְיַית אֲחָתָךְ בַּת אָבוּךְ אוֹ בַּת אִמָךְ מַה דְיוֹלִיד אַבָּךְ מִן אִיתָא אוֹחְרִי אוֹ מִן אִמָךְ אוֹ מַה דִילֵידַת אִמָךְ מִן אַבָּךְ אוֹ מִן גְבַר חוֹרַן לָא תִבְזֵי עֶרְיַתְהֶן


The nakedness of thy sister, the daughter of thy father, or the daughter of thy mother, (or of her) whom, thy father begat by another wife, or of thy mother, whom thy mother bare by thy father or by another husband, thou shalt not dishonour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:10)


עֶרְיַית בְּרַת בְּנָךְ אוֹ בְּרַת בְּרַתָּךְ לָא תִבְזֵי עֶרְיַיתְהֶן אֲרוּם הֵי כְעִרְיָיתָךְ הִינוּן


The nakedness of thy son's daughter, or the daughter of thy daughter, thou shalt not dishonour, because they are as thy own nakedness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:11)


עֶרְיַית בַּת אִתַּת אָבוּךְ דִילֵידַת מִן אַבָּךְ אַחְתָךְ הִיא לָא תִבְזֵי עִירְיְיתָהּ


The nakedness of thy father's wife's daughter, who hath been begotten of thy father, she is thy sister, thou shalt not dishonour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:12)


עֶרְיַית אֲחַת אַבָּךְ לָא תִבְזֵי קְרֵיבַת בְּשַר אַבָּךְ הִיא


The nakedness of thy father's sister thou shalt not dishonour; she is of kin to thy father's flesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:13)


עֶרְיַית אֲחַת אִמָךְ לָא תִבְזֵי אֲרוּם קְרֵיבַת בְּשַר אִמָךְ הִיא


The nakedness of thy mother's sister thou shall not dishonour; for she is of kin to thy mother's flesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:14)


עֶרְיַית אַחְבוּךְ לָא תִבְזֵי וּלְוַת אִנְתְּתֵיהּ לָא תִקְרֵב בְּתַשְׁמִישׁ עַרְסָא אִיתַּת אַחְבוּךְ הִיא


The nakedness of thy father's brother thou shalt not dishonour, nor come nigh to his wife carnally; she is the wife of thy father's brother.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:15)


עֶרְיַית כַּלָתָךְ לָא תִבְזֵי אִתַּת בְּרָךְ הִיא לָא תִבְזֵי עִירְיְיתָהּ


The nakedness of thy daughter-in-law thou shalt not dishonour; she is the wife of thy son, thou shalt not dishonour her nakedness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:16)


עֶרְיַית אִתַּת אָחוּךְ לָא תִבְזֵי בְּחַיֵי אָחוּךְ וּבָתַר מוֹתֵיהּ אִין אִית לֵיהּ בְּנִין עִירְיְיתָא דְאָחוּךְ הוּא


The nakedness of thy brother's wife thou shalt not dishonour in the life-time of thy brother, or after his death, if he have children; for it is the nakedness of thy brother.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:17)


עֶרְיַית אִתָּא וּבְרַתָּהּ לָא תִבְזֵי יַת בְּרַת בְּרָהּ וְיַת בְּרַת בְּרַתָּהּ לָא תִיסַב לְבַזָאָה עִרְיְיתָהּ קְרִיבַת בִּישְרָא הִינוּן זְנוּ הוּא


The nakedness of a woman and of her daughter thou shalt not dishonour, neither shalt thou take her son's daughter or the daughter of her daughter, to dishonour their nakedness; for they are of kin to her flesh; it is corruption.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:18)


וְאִתְּתָא בְּחַיֵי אָחָתָא לָא תִיסַב לְאַעֲקָא לָהּ לְבַזָאָה עֶרְיְיתָהּ עֲלָהּ כָּל יוֹמֵי חַיָיהָא


Neither shalt thou take a wife in the lifetime of her sister, to aggrieve her by dishonouring her nakedness, over her, all the days of her life.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:19)


וּלְצֵיד אִתְּתָא בִּזְמַן רִיחוּק סְאוּבְתָּהּ לָא תִקְרַב לְבַזָּאָה עֶרְיְיתָהּ


And unto the side of a woman in the time of the separation of her uncleanness thou shalt not draw nigh to dishonour her nakedness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:20)


וּלְצֵיד אִתַּת חַבְרָךְ לָא תִתֵּן תַּשְׁמִישָׁךְ לְזַרְעָא לְאִסְתָּאֲבָא בָהּ


Nor unto the side of thy neighbour's wife shalt thou come to defile her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:21)


וּמִן זַרְעָךְ לָא תִתֵּן בְּתַשְׁמִישְׁתָּה לְצֵיד בַּת עַמְמִין לְמָעְבְרָא לְפוּלְחָנָא אוֹחְרָא וְלָא תָפִיס יַת שְׁמָא דֶאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ


And of thy offspring thou shalt not give up any to lie carnally with the daughters of the Gentiles, to perform strange worship; nor shalt thou profane the Name of thy God: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:22)


וְעִם דְכוּרֵי לָא תִשְׁכּוּב בְּתַשְׁמִישַׁיָא דְאִתְּתָא מְרַחְקָא הִיא


Nor with a male person shalt thou lie as with a woman; it is an abhorrent thing.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:23)


וּבְכָל בְּעִירָא לָא תִתֵּן תַּשְׁמִישָׁךְ לְאִסְתָּאֲבָא בָּהּ וְאִתְּתָא לָא תִקְרַב קֳדָם בְּעִירָא לְאִתְהַנְיָא מִינֵיהּ תִּבְלָא הוּא


Neither shalt thou lie with any beast to corrupt thyseIf therewith nor shall any woman approach before a beast for evil pleasure; it is confusion.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:24)


לָא תִסְתַּאֲבוּן בַּחֲדָא מִכָּל אִילֵין אֲרוּם בְּכָל אִילֵין אִיסְתַּאֲבוּ עַמְמַיָא דַאֲנָא מַגְלֵי מִן קָדָמֵיכוֹן


Defile not yourselves by any one of all these; for by all these have the peoples defiled themselves whom I am about to drive away from before you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:25)


וְאִסְתַּאֲבָא אַרְעָא וְאַסְעָרִית חוֹבָאָה עֲלָהּ וּפְלָטַת אַרְעָא יַת דַיְירָהָא


And the land hath been defiled, and I have visited the guilt upon it, and the land delivereth itself of its inhabitants.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:26)


וְתִיטְרוּן אַתּוּן כְנִישְׁתָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל יַת קְיָימַי וְיַת סִדְרֵי דִינַי וְלָא תַעַבְדוּן חֲדָא מִכָּל תּוֹעֶבְיָא הָאִילֵין יַצִיבַיָא וְגִיוֹרַיָא דִי יִתְגַיְירוּן בֵּינֵיכוֹן


But you, O congregation of Israel, observe My statutes, and the order of My judgments, and commit not one of these abominations, neither (you who are) native born, or the strangers who sojourn among you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:27)


אֲרוּם יַת תּוֹעֵיבְתָּא הָאִילֵין עָבָדוּ אִינְשֵׁי אַרְעָא דִקְדָמֵיכוֹן וְאִסְתְּאָבַת אַרְעָא


For these abominable things have been done by the men of the land who have been before you, so that the land hath been polluted:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:28)


וְלָא תִפְלוֹט אַרְעָא יַתְכוֹן בְּסָאוֹבְכוּן יָתָהּ הֵיכְמָא דִפְלָטַת יַת עַמָא דִקְדָמֵיכוֹן


lest, when you pollute the land, it cast you forth, as it will have delivered itself of the people that were before you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:29)


אֲרוּם כָּל מַאן דְיֶעֱבַד חֲדָא מִכָּל תּוֹעֵיבְתָּא הָאִילֵין וְיִשְׁתֵּיצְיַין נַפְשָׁתָא דִיעַבְדוּן הֵכְדֵין מִגוֹ עַמְמֵיהֶן


For whoever committeth any one of these abominations, the souls who do so shall be destroyed from among their people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/18:30)


וְתִטְרוּן יַת מַטְרַת מֵימְרִי לְאִזְדַהֲרָא מְטוֹל לְמֶעֱבַד מִנִימוּסֵי תּוֹעֵבְתָּא דְאִיתְעַבִידוּ בְּאַרְעָא קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְלָא תִסְתַּאֲבוּן בְּהוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


Observe you (then) the keeping of My Word, in being careful to avoid the practice of these abominable rites, which have been practised in the land before you, and the defilement of yourselves by them: I am the Lord.



## Chapter 19

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:2)


מַלֵיל עִם כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן קַדִישִׁין תְּהוֹן אֲרוּם קַדִישׁ אֲנָא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


Speak with the whole congregation of the sons of Israel, and say to them: Ye, shall be holy, for I the Lord your God am Holy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:3)


גְבַר מִן אִימֵיהּ וּמִן אָבוֹי תֶּהֱווֹן דַחֲלִין וְיַת יוֹמֵי שַׁבַּיָא דִילִי תִנְטְרוּן אֲנָא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


Let every man revere his mother and his father, and keep the days of My Sabbaths: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:4)


לָא תִסְטוּן לְפוּלְחַן טַעֲוָון וְדַחֲלָן דְמַתְּכָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן לְכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


Go not astray after the worship of idols, nor make gods for yourselves that are molten: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:5)


וַאֲרוּם תִּיכְסוּן נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְרַעֲוָא לְכוֹן תֵּיכְסוּנֵיהּ


And when you sacrifice the consecrated victims before the Lord, you shall make the sacrifice acceptable.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:6)


בְּיוֹמָא דְיִתְנְכֵס יִתְאֲכֵיל וּבְיוֹמָא חוֹרַן וּמַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר עַד יוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה בְּנוּרָא יִתּוֹקָד


On the day that it is sacrificed you may eat of it, and on the day following; but what remaineth on the third day shall be burned with fire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:7)


וְאִין אִיתְאַכְלָא יִתְאֲכֵיל בְּיוֹמָא תְּלִיתָאָה פְּסִיל הוּא לָא יֶהֱוֵי לְרַעֲוָא


But if it be indeed eaten on the third day, it is profaned, and shall not be accepted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:8)


וּדְיֵיכְלִינֵיהּ חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּיל אֲרוּם יַת קוּדְשָׁא דַיְיָ אָפִיס וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


And he who eateth it shall receive (the penalty of) his sin; for he hath profaned the holy of the Lord, and that man shall be destroyed from among his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:9)


וּבִזְמַן מַחֲצַדְכוֹן יַת חֲצָדָא דְאַרְעֲכוֹן לָא תְסַיְיפוּן אוּמָנָא חֲדָא דְאִית בְּחַקְלָךְ (ונ"ל שצ"ל למחצוד) לְמָחְזוֹר וְלִקְטָא דְחַצְדָךְ לָא תְלַקֵט


And in the time that you reap the harvest of your land you shall not finish one corner that is in the circuit of thy field, and the (full) ingathering of thy harvest thou shalt not collect.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:10)


וּכְרָמֵיכוֹן לָא תְבַעֲרוּן טוֹטְלְוַתְהוֹן וְנִתְרָא דְכַרְמָךְ לָא תְלַקֵט לַעֲנִיֵי וּלְגִיוֹרֵי תִּשְׁבּוֹק יַתְהוֹן בְּחִבּוּרֵיהוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


Neither mayest thou shake out thy vines; (the whole of) their bunches, and the remnant of thy vines thou shalt not gather: thou shalt leave them for the poor and for the strangers at the time of their collection: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:11)


עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא תֶהֱווֹן גַנָבִין וְלָא תְכַפְּרוּן וְלָא תְשַׁקְרוּן אֵינַשׁ בְּחַבְרֵיהּ


Sons of Israel, My people, you shall not steal, nor prevaricate, nor do fraudulently one man with his neighbour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:12)


עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא יִשְׁתְּבַּע חַד מִנְכוֹן בִּשְׁמִי לְשִׁיקְרָא לְאַפְסָא יַת שְׁמָא דֶאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ


Sons of Israel, My people, let no one of you swear by My Name in vain, to profane the Name of thy God: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:13)


לָא תִטְלוּם יַת חַבְרָךְ וְלָא תָנִיס וְלָא תְבִית סוּטְרָא דְאַגִירָא לִמְעַכְּבָא גַבָּךְ עַד צַפְרָא


Thou shalt not be oppressive (hard) upon thy neighbour, nor take away by force, nor let the hire of the hireling be remaining all night at thy side until the morning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:14)


לָא תְלוּטוּן מַן דְלָא שְׁמַע וּקֳדָם סוּמַיָא לָא תְשַׁווּן תּוֹקְלָא וְתִדְחַל מֵאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ


Thou shalt not curse one who heareth not, nor set a stumbling-block before the blind, but shalt fear thy God: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:15)


לָא תַעַבְדוּן שְׁקַר בְּסֵדֶר דִינָא לָא תִסְבּוּן אַפִּין לְמַסְכְּנָא וְלָא תְיַיקְרוּן אַפֵּי רַבָּא בְּקוּשְׁטָא תְדוּנוּן חַבְרֵיכוֹן


Thou shalt not act falsely in the order of judgment ment neither accept the face of the poor, nor honour the face of the great but in truthfulness shalt thou judge thy neighbour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:16)


לָא תֶהֱווֹן אָזְלִין בָּתַר לִישַׁן תְּלִיתָאֵי דְהוּא קְשֵׁי הֵי כְּחַרְבָּא דִקְטַל מִן תְּרֵין חוּרְפוֹי לְמֵיכוּל קוּרְצִין לִמְעִיקָא לְבַר עַמָךְ לָא תִמְנַע זְכוּ דְחַבְרָךְ לְמִסְהֲדָא עֲלוֹי בְּדִינָא אֲנָא יְיָ


Thou shalt not go after the slanderous tongue, which is cruel as a sword that killeth with its two edges in uttering false accusations to afflict thy people. Thou shalt not hinder the acquittal of thy neighbour in witnessing against him in the judgment: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:17)


לָא תְמַלְלוּן שְׁעִיעַיָא בְּפוּמְכוֹן לְמִיסְנֵי יַת אֲחוּכוֹן בְּלִיבְּכוֹן אוֹכָחָא תּוֹכָחוֹן יַת חַבְרֵיכוֹן בְּרַם אִין מִיבְהִית לָא תְקַבְּלוּן מְטוּלְתֵּיהּ חוֹבָא


Speak not bland words with your lips, having hatred to your brother in your hearts; but reproving you shall reprove your neighbour; and though it make you ashamed, you shall not contract sin in account of him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:18)


לָא תֶהֱווֹן נַקְמִין וְלָא נַטְרִין דְבָבוּ לִבְנֵי עַמָךְ וּתְרַחֲמֵי לְחַבְרָךְ דְמַן אַנְתְּ סַנֵי לָךְ לָא תַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ אֲנָא יְיָ


Be not revengeful, nor cherish animosity against the children of thy people; but thou shalt love thy neighbour himself, as that though there be (cause of) hatred with thee thou mayest not do (evil) to him: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:19)


יַת קְיָמַי תִּטְרוּן בְּעִירָךְ לָא תַרְבְּעִינֵיהּ עִירְבוּבִין חַקְלָךְ לָא תִזְרַע עִירְבוּבִין לְבוּשׁ עִירְבוּבִין כִּלְאֵי עֲמַר וְכִיתַּן לָא יִסוֹק עֲלָךְ


You shall keep My statutes. Thy cattle shall not be made to gender with various kinds, neither sow thy field with mixed seeds, nor put upon thee a garment of divers materials, (as) wool and linen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:20)


וּגְבַר אֲרוּם יִשְׁכּוֹב עִם אִיתָא תַּשְׁמִישׁ זַרְעָא וְהִיא אַמְתָא וְחָרָתָא מִתְאַרְסָא לִגְבַר חָרֵי וּמִתְפַּרְקָא כוֹלָא עַד כְּדוֹן לָא אִיתְפְּרִיקָאַת בְּכַסְפָּא אוֹ שְׁטַר שִׁיחְרוּרָהּ לָא אִיתְיְהֵיב לָהּ פִּשְׁפּוּשׁ יֶהֱוֵי בְּדִינָהּ לְמִילְקֵי הוּא מְחַיְיבָא וְלָא [הִיא] (הֲוָה) בְּרַם סֵדֶר קְטוֹלִין לֵית הִינוּן חֲשִׁיבִין אֲרוּם לָא אִיתְחָרָת כּוֹלָא


And if a man lie carnally with a woman, and she be an (Israelitish) handmaid (about to be) made free, and betrothed to a free man, but her redemption not altogether completed by (the payment of) the money, or the written instrument of liberation not having been given to her, let inquisition be made for judgment: she is liable to be chastised, but he is not. But it shall not be considered a matter of putting to death, because she was not altogether free. (Deut. xxii. 22-24.)


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:21)


וְיַיְתִי גְבַר דְשַׁמֵשׁ עִמָהּ וְלָא הִיא יַת קָרְבַּן אֲשָׁמֵיהּ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא דִכְרָא לַאֲשָׁמָא


And the man who lay with her must bring his trespass offering to the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, a ram for a trespass offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:22)


וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי כַּהֲנָא בְּדִכְרָא דַאֲשָׁמָא קֳדָם יְיָ מְטוֹל חוֹבָתֵיהּ דְחַב וְיִשְׁתְּבֵיק לֵיהּ מֵחוֹבְתֵיהּ דְחָב


And the priest shall make atonement with the ram of his trespass offering before the Lord, for his sin that he hath sinned; and the sin that he hath sinned shall be forgiven.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:23)


וַאֲרוּם תֵּיעָלוּן לְאַרְעָא וְתִינְצְבוּן כָּל אִילַן דְמֵיכְלָא וְתִגְזְרוּן גִזְרָא יַת אִינְבֵּיהּ תְּלַת שְׁנִין יְהֵי לְכוֹן מְרָחַק לְאַבָּדָא לָא יִתְאָכֵל


And when you have come into the land, and have planted any tree that may be eaten of, you shall verily circumcise the fruit of it; three years shall it be to you for rejection, to be destroyed; it shall not be eaten.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:24)


וּבְשַׁתָּא רְבִיעָתָא יְהֵי כָּל אִינְבֵּיהּ קוּדְשֵׁי תוּשְׁבְּחָן קֳדָם יְיָ מִתְפְּרַק מִן כַּהֲנָא


And in the fourth year all the fruit of it shall be consecrated, (a token of) praise before the Lord delivered for the priest (or, to be redeemed from the priest).


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:25)


וּבְשַׁתָּא חֲמִישָׁתָא תֵּיכְלוּן יַת אִנְבֵּיהּ מְטוּל דְיוֹסְפוּן לְכוֹן מִן שְׁמַיָא עֲלַלְתָּא אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


But in the fifth year thou mayst eat the fruit of it; for produce will be increased to you from the heavens: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:26)


לָא תֵיכְלוּן מִבְּשַר כָּל נִכְסְתָא עַד דְאַדְמָא קְיַם בְּמִזְרְקַיָא לָא תֶהֱווֹן נַטְרֵי נַחְשִׁין וְלָא (ס"א אֲחוֹדֵי עַיְינִין עיין דברים י"ח פסוק ט"ז בת"י) אֲחוֹרֵי סַנְהֶדְרִין עַיְינִין


You shall not eat the flesh of any sacrifice while the blood remaineth in the veins. You shall not be observers of auguries, after the sanhedrin of the speculators.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:27)


לָא תַקְפֵי צִדְעֵי רֵישֵׁיכוֹן וְלָא תִגְלְבוּן יַת שׁוּמַת דִיקְנֵיכוֹן


You shall not round off the (hair on) the sides of your heads, nor shave the corners of your beards.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:28)


וְשׁוֹרְטַת חִיבוּל עַל נְפַשׁ דְמִית לָא תִתְּנוּן בִּבְשַרְכוֹן וּכְתַב חָקִיק לִרְשַׁם חָרִית צִיוּרָא לָא תִתְּנוּן בְּכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ


And a corrupting incision for the soul of the dead thou shalt not make in thy flesh, neither set upon yourselves an inscription by the incutting of any figurated sign: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:29)


לָא תִפְסוּן יַת בְּנָתֵיכוֹן לְמִסְבָא יַתְהוֹן לִזְנוּ וְלָא תִשְׁהוּן לְמִסְבָא בְּנָתֵיכוֹן לְגוּבְרִין סְמוּךְ לְפִירְקֵיהוֹן דְלָא יְטַעֲוַון בִּזְנוּ בָּתַר עַמְמֵי אַרְעָא וְתִיתְמְלֵי אַרְעָא זְנוּ


You shall not profane your daughters to give them up to fornication: neither delay to give your daughters unto a husband in their proper ages, lest they go astray by fornication after the people of the land, and the land be filled with whoredom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:30)


יַת יוֹמֵי שַׁבַּיָא דִילִי תִטְרוּן וּלְבֵית מוּקְדָשִׁי תְּהוֹן אָזְלִין בְּדַחַלְתָּא אֲנָא יְיָ


The days of My Sabbaths You shall keep, and go unto My sanctuary with reverence: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:31)


לָא תִסְטוּן בָּתַר שַׁאֲלֵי בִּידִין וּמַסִיק זְכוּרוּ וְתַבְעֵי גְרַם יָדוֹעַ וְלָא תִיתְבְּעוּן לְאִיסְתַּאֲבָא בְהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


Go not astray after those who inquire of impostors, or bring up the dead, or interrogate the bone of Jeddua: neither be ye inquirers with them, to pollute yourselves thereby I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:32)


מִן קֳדָם סָבִין דִסְבִירִין בְּאוֹרַיְתָא תְּקוּמוּן וּתְיַיקֵר אַפֵּי חַכִּימָא וְתִדְחַל מֵאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ


You shall rise up before the aged who instruct in the law and honour the presence of the wise, and fear thy God: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:33)


וַאֲרוּם אִין אִיתְגַיֵיר וְאִיתְחַזֵק עִמְכוֹן גִיוֹרָא בְּאַרְעֲכוֹן לָא תוֹנוּ יָתֵיהּ בְּמִילִין קָשִׁין


And if a stranger becometh a sojourner, and settleth among you in your land, you shall not molest him with hard words:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:34)


כְּיַצִיבָא מִנְכוֹן יְהֵי לְכוֹן גִיוֹרָא דְמִתְגַיֵיר עִמְכוֹן וְתִרְחַם לֵיהּ כְּוָותָךְ דְמָא אַתְּ סָנֵי לָךְ לָא תַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ אֲרוּם דַיְירוּן הֲוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


but the stranger who sojourneth among you shall be (treated) as the native born, and thou shalt love him as thyseIf: that which is hateful unto thee, thou shalt not do unto him; for ye were sojourners in the land of Mizraim: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:35)


לָא תַעַבְדוּן שִׁיקְרָא בְּסִידְרָא דִינָא בְּמִישְׁחֲתָא דְקַיְטָא וְסִיתְוָוא בְּמַתְקְלָא וּבִמְכִילְתָּא בִּגְדִישׁוּתָא וּבִמְחִיקָא


You shall not deal falsely in the ordering of judgment; in the admeasurement of summer and winter; in weight and measure, in heaping up, or in sweeping off:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:36)


מוֹדְנָווּן דִקְשׁוֹט מַתְקְלִין דִקְשׁוֹט מְכִילוּ דִקְשׁוֹט וְקִיסְטִין דִקְשׁוֹט יְהֵי לְכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דְאַנְפָקִית יַתְכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


but balances of truth, weights of truth, measures of truth, and tankards of truth, shall yours be. I am the Lord our God who brought you redeemed from the land of Mizraim:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 19:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/19:37)


וְתִיטְרוּן יַת כָּל קְיָימַי וְיַת כָּל סִדְרֵי דִינַי וְתַעַבְדוּן יַתְהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ


and you shall observe all My statutes, and all the ordinations of My judgments, and do them: I am the Lord.



## Chapter 20

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:2)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל תְּמַלֵיל לְמֵימָר גְבַר טְלֵי אוֹ סִיב מִן גְנִיסַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דְיַעֲבֵיר מִזַרְעֵיהּ לְמוֹלֶךְ לְמִיתוֹקְדָא בְּנוּרָא אִיתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל עַמָא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל יְחַיְיבוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּמַטְלוּת אַבְנִין


And with the sons of Israel speak thou, to say: A man of the family of the sons of Israel, whether young or old, who shall make (an offering) of his offspring unto Molek to be burned in the fire, shall be verily put to death: the people of the house of Israel shall punish his guilt by the infliction of stones;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:3)


וַאֲנָא אֶתֵּן פְּנוּיָיתָא לְמֶעֱסוֹק בְּגַבְרָא הַהוּא וְאֵישֵׁיצֵי יָתֵיהּ מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ אֲרוּם מִזַרְעֵיהּ יְהַב לְפּוּלְחָנָא אוֹחֲרָא מִן בִּגְלַל לְסַאֲבָא יַת מַקְדָשִׁי וּלְאַפְסָא יַת שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשִׁי


and I will cause a reverse, to make prosperity to cease with that man, and will cut him off from among his people; because he gave his offspring in strange worship to pollute My sanctuary and to profane My holy Name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:4)


וְאִין מִכְבַּשׁ יִכְבְשׁוּן עַמָא בֵית יִשְרָאֵל יַת עֵינֵיהוֹן מִן גַבְרָא הַהוּא בִּדְיִתֵּן מִזַרְעֵיהּ לְפוּלְחָנָא אוֹחֲרָא מְטוֹל דְלָא לְמִקְטוֹל יָתֵיהּ


And if the people of the house of Israel hiding hide their eyes from that man, when he giveth his offspring unto strange worship, that they might not kill him,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:5)


וְאֶשַׁוֵי אֲנָא פְנוּיַיְתָא לְמֶעֱסוֹק בְּגַבְרָא הַהוּא וּבִגְנִיסָתֵיהּ דִמְחַפְיַין עֲלוֹי לְאִתְרְדָאָה בְּיִסוּרֵי וְיָתֵיהּ אֵישֵׁיצֵי וְיַת כָּל דְטָעַן בַּתְרוֹי לְמִטְעֵי בָּתַר פּוּלְחָנָא אוֹחֲרָא מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


then will I appoint a reverse, to make a controversy against that man, and against his family who protect (cover) him, to chastise (them) with afflictions; and him will I destroy, and all who follow him to wander after strange worship, from among their people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:6)


וּבַר נַשׁ דְיִסְטֵי בָּתַר שַׁאֲלֵי בִּידִין וּמַסְקֵי זְכוּרוּ וְתַבְעֵי גְרַם יְדוֹעַ לְמִטְעֵי בַּתְרֵיהוֹן וְאֶתֵּן פְּנִיוּתָא לְמֶעֱסוֹק בְּבַר נְשָׁא הַהוּא וְאֵישֵׁיצֵי יָתֵיהּ בְּמוֹתָנָא מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


And the man who turns aside to inquire of the impostors, or to seek to bring up the dead, or to inquire by the bone of Jeddua, to go astray after them, I will appoint a reverse to punish that man, and will destroy him by a plague from among his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:7)


וְתִתְקַדְשׁוּן וּתְהוֹן קַדִישִׁין בְּגוּפֵיכוֹן מְטוֹל דַאֲקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא צְלוֹתְכוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ מְקַדִשְׁכוֹן


But sanctify yourselves, and be holy in your bodies, that your prayers may be received with acceptance: I am the Lord who sanctifies you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:8)


וְתִטְרוּן יַת קְיָמַי וְתַעַבְדוּן יַתְהוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ מְקַדִשְׁכוֹן


And observe My statutes and perform them: I am the Lord who sanctifies you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:9)


אֲרוּם גְבַר טְלֵי וּגְבַר סִיב דְיֵילוּט יַת אָבוֹי וְיַת אִמֵיהּ בִּשְׁמָא מְפָרֵשׁ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל בְּאַטְלוּת אַבְנִין מְטוֹל דְאָבוֹי וְאִמֵיהּ לָט קְטָלָא חַיָיב


For the young man or the old man who curseth his father or his mother by the revealed Name, shall be verily put to death by the casting of stones; because he hath cursed his father or his mother, he is guilty of death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:10)


וּגְבַר דִי יִגוֹר יַת אִתַּת גְבַר מֵאַרְסָא אוֹ דִי יִגוֹר יַת אִתַּת חַבְרֵיהּ מִבַּעְלָא אִיתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל עַל מִיבְעֲלָא בְּשִׁינוּק סוּדְרָא אַקוֹשָׁא בְּגוֹ רְכִיכָא וְעַל מֵאַרְסָא בְּאַטְלוּת אַבְנִין גִיוֹרָא וְגִיוֹרְתָּא


And the man who by adultery defileth the wife of (another) man, or who committeth adultery with the wife of his married neighbour, shall be verily put to death, by strangulation, with the hard towel in the tender part (?): and on account of a betrothed person, by the casting of stones: both the adulterer and the adulteress (shall die).


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:11)


וּגְבַר דִי יְשַׁמֵשׁ עִם אִתַּת אָבוֹי בֵּין דְהִיא אִמֵיהּ בֵּין דְהִיא חוֹרַנְיָיתָא עֲרְיָיתָא דְאָבוֹי בָּזֵי אִיתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְלוּן תְּרֵיהוֹן קְטָלָא חַיָיבִין בְּאַטְלוּת אַבְנִין


And a man who lieth with his father's wife, whether his own mother or another wife, and who hath dishonoured the nakedness of his father shall be verily put to death: both of them are guilty of death by the casting of stones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/20:12)


וּגְבַר דִי יְשַׁמֵשׁ עִם כַּלָתֵיהּ אִיתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְלוּן תְּרֵיהוֹן תִּבְלָא עָבָדוּ קְטָלָא חַיָיבִין בְּאַטְלוּת אַבְנִין


And if a man lieth with his daughter-in-law, both of them shall be put to death; they have wrought confusion, they are guilty of death by the casting of stones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 20:13

וּגְבַר דִי יְשַׁמֵשׁ עִם אַחֲתֵיהּ בְּרַת אָבוֹי אוֹ בְּרַת אִימֵיהּ וְיִבְזֵי יַת עֶרְיְיתָהּ וְהִיא תִבְזֵי יַת עֶרְיְיתֵיהּ גְנַיי הוּא אֲרוּם חִסְדָא עֲבָדִית עִם קַדְמָאֵי מִן בִּגְלַל דְיִתְמְלֵי עַלְמָא מִנְהוֹן עַד לָא אִיתְיְהִיב קְיָימָא בְּעַלְמָא וּמִן דְאִתְיְהִיב קְיָימָא בְּעַלְמָא כָּל דְיֶעֱבַד כְּדֵין וְיִשְׁתֵּיצוּן בְּמוֹתָנָא וְחָמְיַין בְּבִישְׁתְּהוֹן בְּנֵי עַמְהוֹן מְטוֹל דְעֶרְיַית אַחְתֵיהּ בְּזֵי חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּל


And if a man lie with his sister, his father's daughter, or the daughter of his mother, and he dishonour her nakedness, it is depravity: for I showed mercy with the first ones, on behalf of the peopling of the world by them, while as yet I had not promulged the law in the world: but after the law hath been declared in the world, every one who committeth these things shall be destroyed by mortality, and the children of their people shall witness their punishment; for he who hath dishonoured the nakedness of his sister shall be guilty of death.

And you shall make distinction between the animal which is fit to be eaten, and that which it is improper to eat; and between the fowl which it is improper to eat, and that which is fit to be eaten. Defile not your souls by (eating of) the animal that is torn by a wild beast, or the bird torn by the falcon, or anything that creepeth upon the which I have from you for their uncleanness.

And the man or the woman who hath in them (the spirit of) divination or necromancy shall die by the casting of stones; for they are guilty of death.



## Chapter 21

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:1)


ויאמר וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֵימַר לְכַהֲנַיָא בְּנֵי אַהֲרן דְכוּרַיָא דְיִתְפַּרְשׁוּן מִן סְאוֹב וְכִדְנָא תֵימַר לְהוֹן עַל בַּר נַשׁ דְמִית לָא יִסְתָּאֵב בְּעַמֵיהּ


AND the Lord spake to Mosheh, saying: Speak unto the priests, the men of the children of Aharon, that they keep themselves apart from defilement and thus shalt thou say to them: For a man who is dead, (the priest) shall not defile himself among his people;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:2)

but for a woman who is of kin to his flesh, for his daughter, and for his father, and for his son, and for his daughter, and for his brother,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:3)

and for his sister, a virgin who is nigh to him, and who hath neither been betrothed, nor married to a husband, for her he may defile himself.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:4)


לָא יִסְתָּאֵב בַּעֲלָא לְאִיתְּתֵיהּ אֱלָהֵן כַּד כַּשְׁרָא לֵיהּ בְּרַם לִקְרֵיבָא דְהִינוּן עַבְדִין עוֹבְדֵי עַמֵיהּ יִתְחַל עֲלֵיהוֹן


The husband shall not defile himself on account of his wife, except so far as it is right for him; but for a relative of those who do the work of his people he may defile himself.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:5)


לָא יְרַשְׁמוּן בֵּין עֵינֵיהוֹן וְלָא רוֹשַׁם בְּרֵישֵׁיהוֹן וְאוּמָנָא דִיקָנֵיהוֹן לָא יִסְפְּרוּן וּבִבְשָרֵיהוֹן לָא יְחַבְּלוּן חִבּוּל


They shall not mark themselves between their eyes, nor set a mark upon their heads, nor cut away the corners of their beards, nor make any incision in their flesh:

קַדִישִׁין יְהוֹן קֳדָם אֱלָהָהוֹן וְלָא יִפְסוּן יַת שְׁמָא דֶאֱלָהָהוֹן אֲרוּם יַת קוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ תִּקְרוּבְתָּא דֶאֱלָהָהוֹן הִינוּן מַקְרִיבִין וִיהוֹן קַדִישִׁין בְּגוּפֵיהוֹן


but they shall be holy before their God, and shall not profane the name of their God;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:7)


אִתְּתָא מַטְעַיָא בִּזְנוּ וּדְאִתְיְלִידָא מִן פְּסוּלַיָא לָא יִסְבּוּן וְאִתְּתָא דְמִפְטְרָא בֵּין מִגַבְרָא בֵּין מִן יְבָמָהּ לָא יִסְבּוּן אֲרוּם קַדִישׁ הוּא קֳדָם אֱלָהֵיהּ


They shall not take to wife a woman who hath gone astray by fornication, or who was born illegitimate, nor a woman who hath been put away, whether from her husband or the husband's brother, may they take; for he is to be holy before his God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:8)

Thou shalt sanctify him unto the priesthood; for the oblation itself of thy God he is to offer: he shall be holy to thee, and thou shalt not make him profane: I, the Lord who sanctify you, am holy.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:9)

And if the betrothed daughter of a man of the priesthood profane herself, by going astray in fornication; if, while she is yet in her father's house, she is guilty of fornication, she shall be burned with fire.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:10)

And the high priest who hath been anointed over his brethren, and upon whose head the anointing oil was poured, and who offered his oblation to be arrayed in the (holy) robes, shall not make his head bare, nor either rend or tear his garment in the hour of grief.

Nor unto any person who is dead shall he go in, nor for his father or his mother make himself unclean.

but a widow, or a divorced person, or one who was born of depraved parents, or who hath gone astray by fornication, such as these be shall not take; but a virgin proper shall he take to wife from the daughters of his people.

for no man who hath a blemish in him shall offer. A man who is blind or lame, or stricken in his nostrils, or mutilated in his thigh,

or a man who hath a broken foot, or a broken hand

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/21:20)

or whose eyelids droop so as to cover his eyes, who hath no hair on his eyelids; or who hath a suffusion of whiteness with darkness in his eyes; or who hath the dry scurvy, or who is full of the blotches of Egypt, or whose testicles are swollen or shrunk,

no man, a priest of the race of Aharon the priest who hath in him any such blemish, shall be qualified to offer the oblations of the Lord. He hath a blemish, and it is not meet for him to offer the oblation of his God.

Nevertheless he may support himself with the residue of the oblations of his God which remaineth of the most holy and of the holy (offerings);

only he must not enter within the veil, nor approach the altar; for a blemish is in him, and he shall not profane My sanctuary; for I the Lord do sanctify them.

## Chapter 22

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:2)


מַלֵיל עִם אַהֲרן וְעִם בְּנוֹי וְיִתְפַּרְשׁוּן מִקוּדְשַׁיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְלָא יִפְסוּן יַת שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשִׁי דְהִינוּן מְקַדְשִׁין קֳדָמַי אֲנָא יְיָ


Speak with Aharon and with his sons, that they keep apart from the consecrated things of the children of Israel, and profane not the Name of My Holiness (in whatever) they hallow before Me: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:3)


אֵימַר לְהוֹן אִזְדַהֲרִין לְדָרֵיכוֹן כָּל גְבַר דְיִקְרַב מִכָּל בְּנֵיכוֹן לְקוּדְשַׁיָא דִי יְקַדְשׁוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ וְסוּבְתֵּיהּ עֲלוֹי וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא בְּמוֹתָנָא מִן קֳדָמַי אֲנָא יְיָ


Say to them, Take heed in your generations: whatever man of all your sons who shall offer things hallowed, which the children of Israel have consecrated before the Lord, having his uncleanness upon him, that man shall be destroyed with a stroke of death before Me: I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:4)


גְבַר טְלֵי אוֹ גְבַר סִיב מִזַרְעָא דְאַהֲרן וְהוּא מְצָרְעָא אוֹ דַיֵיב בְּקוּדְשַׁיָא לָא יֵיכוֹל עַד זְמַן דְמִידְכֵּי וּדְיִקְרַב בְּכָל סוֹאֲבַת בַּר נַשׁ אוֹ גְבַר דְתִפּוֹק מִינֵיהּ שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא


Any man, young or old of the offspring of Aharon, who is a leper, or hath a running issue, shall not eat of things consecrated till he be clean: and whoever toucheth any uncleanness of man, or one from whom uncleanness hath proceeded,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:5)


אוֹ גְבַר דִי יִקְרַב בְּכָל רִחְשָׁא דְיִסְתְּאַב לֵיהּ אוֹ בְאֵינָשָׁא דְמִית דְיִסְתְּאַב לֵיהּ וּלְכָל סְאוּבְתֵּיהּ בְּחַיוֹי


or who toucheth any reptile that maketh unclean, or (the corpse of) a dead man which maketh unclean, or any of the uncleanness of his life,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:6)


בַּר נַשׁ כּהֵן דְיִקְרַב בֵּיהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וְלָא יֵיכוּל מִן קוּדְשַׁיָא אֱלָהֵן אִין אַסְחֵי בִּישְרֵיהּ בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָוִוין דְמוֹי


the man being a priest who toucheth such shall be unclean until the evening, and may not eat of the holy things, except that he wash his flesh in forty seahs of water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:7)


וְיִטְמוֹעַ שִׁמְשָׁא וְיִתְכַּשֵׁר וּבָתַר כְּדֵין יֵיכוּל מִן קוּדְשַׁיָא אֲרוּם מְזוֹנֵיהּ הוּא


And when the sun hath set and he be fit, he may afterward eat of the holy things; for they are his food.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:8)


נְבֵילָא וּקְטוֹלָא לָא יֵיכוּל לְאִסְתָּאֲבָא בָהּ אֲנָא יְיָ


But of a dead carcase, or (that which hath been) killed (by violence), he may not eat to defile himself therewith. I am the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:9)


וְיִטְרוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת מַטְרַת מֵימְרִי וְלָא יְקַבְּלוּן עֲלוֹי חוֹבָא וְלָא יְמוּתוּן בֵּיהּ בְּאֵישָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא אֲרוּם יִפְסוּנֵיהּ אֲנָא יְיָ מְקַדִשְׁהוֹן


But the sons of Israel shall observe the keeping of My Word, that they may not bring sin upon themselves, nor die for it by the flaming fire; because they have profaned it: I am the Lord who sanctify them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:10)


וְכָל חִילוֹנֵי לָא יֵיכוּל קוּדְשָׁא בַּר יִשְרָאֵל דְהוּא תּוֹתָבָא דְכַהֲנָא וְאַגִירָא לָא יֵיכוּל קוּדְשָׁא


No stranger or profane person shall eat of a consecrated thing, (neither) a son of Israel who is an inmate of the priest, nor any hireling, may eat of the hallowed thing.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:11)


וְכַהֲנָא אֲרוּם יִקְנֵי בַּר נַשׁ נוּכְרָאָה קִנְיַן כַּסְפֵּיהּ הוּא יֵיכוֹל בֵּיהּ וּמַרְבְּיָינֵי בַיְיתֵיהּ הִינוּן יֵיכְלוּן בְּלַחֲמֵיהּ


But if the priest buy a man a stranger with the price of his money, he may eat of it, and such as have grown up in his house may eat of his bread.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:12)


וּבְרַת כּהֵן אֲרוּם תְּהֵי מִתְנַסְבָא לִגְבַר חִילוֹנֵי הִיא בְּאַפְרָשׁוּת קוּדְשַׁיָא לָא תֵיכוּל


And the daughter of a priest, if she be married to a man a stranger, may not eat of things set apart by consecration.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:13)


וּבְרַת כּהֵן אֲרוּם תְּהֵי אַרְמְלָא אוֹ מִיתַרְכָא וּוְלַד לֵית לָהּ מִנֵיהּ וְתָבָת לְבֵית אִיבָהָא דְלָא מִינְטְרָא לְיָבָם הֵי כְּיוֹמֵי טַלְיוּתָהּ וְלָא מְעַבְּרָא מִן מְזוֹנֵיהּ דְאִיבָהָא תֵּיכוּל וְכָל חִילוֹנֵי לָא יֵיכוֹל בֵּיהּ


But if the daughter of a priest be a widow, or be divorced and having no child by him hath returned to her father's house, and hath not been wedded to a brother-in-law, (Deut. xxv. 5,) she, being as in the days of her youth, and not being with child, may eat of her father's meat; but no stranger shall eat thereof.

And if a man of Israel eat that which is consecrated unknowingly, let him add a fifth part of its value to it, and give the (price of the) holy thing unto the priest.

Speak with Aharon and with his sons and with all the children of Israel: -A man, whether young or old, of the house of the family of Israel, or of the strangers who are in Israel, who shall offer his oblation of any of their vows, or their free will offerings which they present before the Lord for a burnt sacrifice,

לְרַעֲוָא לְכוֹן שְׁלִים דְכַר בְּתוֹרֵי בְּאִימְרַיָא וּבִבְנֵי עִזַיָא


to be acceptable for you, it shall be perfect, a male of the bullocks, of the lamb, or of the young goats.

But anything that hath a blemish you shall not offer; for that will not be acceptable from you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:21)


וּגְבַר אֲרוּם יִקְרַב נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְאַפְרָשָׁא נִדְרָא אוֹ נְסֵיבְתָּא בְּתוֹרֵי אוֹ בְעָנָא שְׁלִים יְהֵי לְרַעֲוָא כָּל מוּמָא לָא יְהֵי בֵיהּ


And if a man will offer a consecrated victim before the Lord to fulfil a vow, or as a free will offering, from the herd, or from the flock, it must be perfect to be acceptable; no blemish shall be in it.

דְסָמֵי אוֹ דִתְבִיר גַרְמָא אוֹ דְרִיסוֹי לַקְיַין אוֹ דְעֵינוֹי לַקְיַין דִמְעָרַב חִיוְרָא בְּאוּכְמָא אוֹ דִמְלֵי חַרְסִין יַבְשִׁין אוֹ חֲזָזִיתָא מִצְרֵיתָא לָא תְקַרְבוּן אִלֵין קֳדָם יְיָ וְקוּרְבָּנָא לָא תְקַרְבוּן מִנְהוֹן עַל מַדְבְּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ


Whatever is blind, or broken-boned, or stricken in the eyelids, or whose eyes are stricken with a mixture of white and dark, or one filled with scurvy or the blotches murrain, you shall not offer before the Lord, nor present an oblation of them on the altar before the Lord.

A bullock or a ram that hath superfluity or deficiency of the testicles, you may make a free will offering, but for a vow it will not be acceptable.

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:27)


עִידַן דְתִידְכַּר לָן סִדְרֵי קוּרְבָּנֵינָן מַה דְהַוֵינָן מְקַרְבִין בְּכָל שְׁנָא וּשְׁנָא הֲווֹ קוּרְבָּנֵינָן מְכַפְּרִין עַל חוֹבֵינָן וּכְדוּ דִי גָרְמוּ חוֹבַיָא וְלֵית לָן מַה נַקְרְבָא מִן עֶדְרֵי עֲנֵינָן תּוֹרָא אִיתְבְּחַר קֳדָמוֹי בְּגִין מַדְכַּר זְכוּת סָבָא דְאָתָא מִמַדִינְחָא פַּטִיר כּוּלֵיהּ קָרֵיב לִשְׁמָךְ בַּר תּוֹרִין רַכִּיךְ שָׁמִין אִימְרָא אִיתְבְּחַר תִּנְיָין בְּגִין מַדְכַּר זַכְוָותֵיהּ דִשְיֵיהּ דְאִתְעֲקַד עַל גַבֵּי מַדְבְּחָא וּפְשַׁט צַוְארֵיהּ בְּגִין שְׁמָךְ שְׁמַיָא מָכוּ וּנְחָתוּ וַחֲמָא יִצְחָק יַת שַׁכְלוּלֵיהוֹן וְכַהֲיָין עֵינוֹי מִמְרוֹמַיָא מִן בְּגִין כְּדֵין זָכָה וְאִזְדַמַן לֵיהּ אִימְרָא תְּחוֹתוֹי לְעָלָתָא גְדִי בַּר עִזֵי אִתְבְּחַר תְּחוֹתָא בְּגִין מַדְכַּר זְכוּת שְׁלֵימָא דְעָבַד גַדְיֵי בַּר עִזֵי תַּבְשִׁילִין וְאוֹבִיל לְאָבוֹי וְזָכָה לִמְקַבְּלָא סְדַר בִּרְכָתָא בְּגִין כֵּן פָּרֵישׁ משֶׁה נְבִיָא וַאֲמַר עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל תּוֹר אוֹ אִימַר אוֹ גַדְיָא אֲרוּם יִתְיְלִיד כְּאוֹרַח עַלְמָא וִיהֵי שַׁבְעָתָא יוֹמִין בָּתַר אִמֵיהּ מְטוּל דְיִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע דְלָא נָפִיל וּמִיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה וּלְהָלְאָה יִתְרְעֵי לְקָרָבָא קוּרְבָּנָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ


(to the effect that): What time thou callest to our mind the order of our oblations, as they shall be offered year by year, being our expiatory offering for our sins, when on account of our sins (such sacrifices are required), and we have none to bring from our flocks of sheep, then shall a bullock be chosen before him, in memorial of the righteousness of the elder who came from the cast, the sincere one who brought the calf, fat and tender, to Thy Name. A sheep is to be chosen, secondly, in memory of the righteousness of him who was bound as a lamb on the altar, and who stretched forth his neck for Thy Name's sake, while the heavens stooped down and condescended, and Izhak beheld their foundations, and his eyes were blinded by the high things; on which account he was reckoned to be worthy that a lamb should be provided for him as a burnt offering. A kid of the goats is to be chosen likewise, in memorial of the righteousness of that perfect one who made the savoury meat of the kid, and brought it to his father, and was made worthy to receive the order of the blessing: wherefore Mosheh the prophet explaineth, saying: Sons of Israel, my people, When a bullock, or a lamb, or a kid is brought forth according, to the manner of the world, it shall be seven days after its dam, that there may be evidence that it is not imperfect; and on the eighth day and thenceforth, it is acceptable to be offered an oblation to the Name of the Lord.

עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל הֵיכְמָא דְאָבוּנָן רַחֲמָן בִּשְׁמַיָא כֵּן תֶּהֱוֹן רַחֲמָנִין בְּאַרְעָא תּוֹרָתָא אוֹ רְחֵילָא יָתָהּ וְיַת בְּרָהּ לָא תִיכְסוּן בְּיוֹמָא חָד


Sons of Israel, my people, as our Father in heaven is merciful, so shall you be merciful on earth: neither cow, nor ewe, shall you sacrifice along with her young on the same day.

And when you offer a sacrifice of thanksgiving to the Name of the Lord, you shall offer so as to be accepted.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:30)


בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא יִתְאֲכֵיל לָא תְשַׁיְירוּן מִנֵיהּ עַד צַפְרָא אֲנָא יְיָ


It shall be eaten on that day, none shall remain till the morning: I am the Lord.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:31)


וְתִטְרוּן יַת פִּיקוּדַי וְתַעַבְדוּן יַתְהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ דִיהַב אֲגַר טַב לְנַטְרֵי פִּקוּדַי וְאוֹרַיְיתָי


And you shall observe My commandments to do them I am the Lord who give a good reward, to them who keep My commandments and My laws.

וְלָא תִפְסוּן יַת שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשִׁי וְאֶתְקַדֵשׁ בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אֲנָא יְיָ מְקַדִשְׁכוֹן


Nor shall you profane My Holy Name, that I may be hallowed among the children of Israel. I am the Lord who sanctify you,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 22:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/22:33)


דְהַנְפִּיק יַתְכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מְטוּל לְמֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן לֶאֱלָהָא אֲנָא יְיָ


having brought you forth redeemed from the land of Mizraim, that I may be to you Eloah: I am the Lord.



## Chapter 23

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן זְמַן סִדוּרֵי מוֹעֲדַיָא דַיְיָ דִתְאַרְעוּן יַתְהוֹן מְאַרְעֵי קַדִישׁ אִלֵין הִינוּן זְמַן סִדוּרֵי מוֹעֲדָי


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them, The orders of the time of the Festivals of the Lord, which you shall proclaim as holy convocations, these are the orders of the time of My festivals.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:3)


שִׁיתָּא יוֹמִין תִּתְעֲבֵיד עִיבִידָא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה שַׁבָּא וְנַיְיחָא מְאַרַע קַדִישׁ כָּל עִיבִידָא לָא תַעַבְּדוּן שַׁבְּתָא הוּא לַיְיָ בְּכָל אֲתַר מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן


Six days shalt thou do work, and the seventh day (shall be) a Sabbath and a rest, a holy convocation. No manner of work may you do; it is a Sabbath to the Lord in every place of your habitations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:4)


אִלֵין זְמַנֵי סִדוּרֵי מוֹעֲדַיָא דַיְיָ מְאַרַע קַדִישׁ דִי יִתְרְעוּן יַתְהוֹן בִּזְמַנֵיהוֹן


These are the times of the Festivals of the Lord, holy convocations which you shall proclaim in their times:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:5)


בְּיַרְחָא דְנִיסָן בְּאַרְבֵּיסַר לְיַרְחָא בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשְׁתָא זְמַן נִיכְסַת פִּיסְחָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ


In the month of Nisan, on the fourteenth day of the month, between the suns (shall be) the time for the sacrifice of the Pascha to the Name of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:6)


וּבַחֲמֵיסַר יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא הָדֵין חַגָא דְפַטִירַיָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ שַׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין פַּטִירֵי תֵיכְלוּן


And on the fifteenth day of this month the feast of unleavened cakes to the Name of the Lord. Seven days you shall eat unleavened bread.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:7)


בְּיוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה דְחַגָא מְאַרַע קַדִישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן


On the first day of the feast a holy convocation shall be to you; ye shall do no work of labour,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:8)


וּתְקַרְבוּן קָרְבָּנָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה דְחַגָא מְאַרַע קַדִישׁ כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן


but offer the oblation to the Name of the Lord seven days; in the seventh day of the feast shall be a holy convocation; you shall do no work of labour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:9)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:10)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם תְעָלוּן לְאַרְעָא דַאֲנָא יָהֵיב לְכוֹן וְתַחְצְדוּן יַת חַצְדָא וְתַיְיתוּן יַת עוּמְרָא שֵׁירוּי חֲצַדְכוֹן לְוַת כַּהֲנָא


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: When you have entered into the land which I give you, and you reap the harvest, you shall bring the sheaf of the first fruits of your harvest unto the priest;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:11)


וִירֵים יַת עוּמְרָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְרַעֲוָא לְכוֹן מִבָּתַר יוֹמָא טָבָא קַמָאָה דְפִסְחָא יְרִימְנֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא


and he shall uplift the sheaf before the Lord to be accepted for you. After the first festal day of Pascha (or, the day after the feast-day of Pascha)


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:12)


וְתַעַבְדוּן בְּיוֹם אֲרָמוּתְכוֹן יַת עוּמְרָא אִימַר שְׁלִים בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ לְעָלָתָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ


on the day on which you elevate the sheaf, you shall make (the sacrifice of a lamb of the year, unblemished a burnt offering unto the Name of the Lord:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:13)


וּמִנְחָתֵיהּ תְּרֵין עֶשְרוֹנִין סְמִידָא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא קוּרְבָּנָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ מְטוֹל לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא וְנִיסוּכֵיהּ חֲמַר עִינְבֵי רַבְעוּת הִינָא


and its mincha, two tenths of flour, mingled with olive oil, for an oblation to the Name of the Lord, to be received with acceptance; and its libation, wine of grapes, the fourth of a hin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:14)


וּלְחֵם וּקְלִי וּפֵירוּכִין חַדְתִּין לָא תֵיכְלוּן עַד בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין עַד זְמַן אִיתוֹיֵיכוֹן יַת קָרְבַּן אֱלָהָכוֹן קְיַם עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן בְּכָל מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן


But neither bread nor parched corn (of the ripe harvest) nor new ears may you eat until this day, until the time of your bringing the oblation of your God: an everlasting statute unto your generations in all your dwellings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:15)


וְתִימְנוּן לְכוֹן מִבָּתַר יוֹמָא טָבָא קַמָאָה דְפִסְחָא מִן יוֹם אִיתוֹיֵיכוֹן יַת עוּמְרָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא שְׁבַע שְׁבוּעִין שַׁלְמִין יֶהֶוְויַן


And number to you after the first feast day of Pascha, from the day when you brought the sheaf for the elevation, seven weeks; complete they shall be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:16)


עַד מִבָּתַר שְׁבוּעָתָא שְׁבִיעָאָה תִּימְנוּן חַמְשִׁין יוֹמִין וּתְקַרְבוּן מִנְחָתָא דִלְחֵים חֲדַת לִשְׁמֵיהּ דַיְיָ


Until the day after the seventh week you shall number fifty days, and shall offer a mincha of the new bread unto the Name of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:17)


מֵאֲתַר מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן תַּיְיתוּן לְחֵם אֲרָמוּתָא תַּרְתֵּין גְרִיצִין תְּרֵין עֶשְרוֹנִין סְמִידָא יְהַוְויָין חָמִיעַ יִתְאַפְיָן בִּיכּוּרִין לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ


From the place of your dwellings you are to bring the bread for the elevation; two cakes of two-tenths of flour, which must be baked with leaven, as first fruits unto the Name of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:18)


וּתְקַרְבוּן עַל לַחְמָא הַהוּא שַׁבְעָא אִימְרִין שַׁלְמִין בְּנֵי שָׁנָה וְתוֹר בַּר תּוֹרִין דְלָא עֵירוּבִין חַד לְחַטָאתָא וּתְרֵין אִמְרִין בְּנֵי שָׁנָה לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא


And with that bread you are to offer seven lambs of the year, unblemished, and a young bullock without mixture (of colour), the one for a sin offering, and two lambs of the year for a sanctified oblation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:19)


וְתַעַבְּדוּן צְפִיר בַּר עִיזֵי דְלָא עֵירוּבִין חַד לְחַטָאתָא וּתְרֵין אִמְרִין בְּנֵי שָׁנָה לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא


And you shall make (a sacrifice) of a young goat without mixture, the one for a sin offering and two lambs of the year for a sanctified oblation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:20)


וִירֵים כַּהֲנָא יַתְהוֹן עַל לַחְמָא דְבִכּוּרַיָא אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ עַל תְּרֵין אִמְרִין קוּדְשָׁא יְהוֹן לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ לְכַהֲנָא


And the priest shall uplift them with the bread of the first fruits, an elevation before the Lord, with the two lambs; they shall be holy to the Name of the Lord, and shall be for the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:21)


וּתְעַרְעוּן חַיִין וְקַיָימִין חַי בִּזְמַן בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין מְעָרַע קַדִישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְּדוּן קְיַם עֲלָם בְּכָל מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן לְדָרֵיכוֹן


And you shall proclaim with life and strength that self-same day, that at the time of that day there shall be to you a holy convocation: you shall do no work of labour: it is an everlasting statute in all your dwelling for your generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:22)


וּבִזְמַן מֵחַצְדְכוֹן יַת חֲצַד אַרְעֲכוֹן לָא תְסַיְיפוּן אוּמָנָא חֲדָא דְאִית בְּחַקְלָךְ בְּחַצְדָךְ וּלְקָטָא דְחַצְדָךְ לָא תְלַקֵט לַעֲנִיֵי וּלְגִיוֹרֵי תִּשְׁבּוֹק יַתְהוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


And when you reap the harvest of the ground, you shall not finish one corner that is in thy field at thy reaping nor shalt thou gather the gleanings of thy harvest, but leave them for the poor and the strangers: I am the Lord thy God.

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:24)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר בְּתִשְׁרֵי דְהוּא יַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יְהֵי לְכוֹן יוֹמָא טָבָא דוּכְרַן יַבָּבָא מְאָרַע קַדִישׁ


Speak with the children of Israel, saying: In Tishri, which is the seven month, shall be to you a festival of seven days, a memorial of trumpets, a holy convocation.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:25)

וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:27)


בְּרַם בְּעַשְרָא יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה הָדֵין יוֹמָא דְכִפּוּרַיָא הוּא מְאָרַע קַדִישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּתְעַנוּן יַת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן מִמֵיכְלָא וּמִשְׁתַּיָא וְהַנְיַית בֵּי בָּנַי וְתַמְרוּקִין וְתַשְׁמִישׁ עַרְסָא וְסַנְדְלָא וּתְקַרְבוּן קָרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ


But on the tenth day of this seventh month is the Day of Atonement; a holy convocation shall it be to you, and you shall humble your souls, (abstaining) from food, and from drink, and from the use of the bath, and from anointing, and the use of the bed, and from sandals; and you shall offer an oblation before the Lord,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:28)


וְכָל עִיבִידָא לָא תַעַבְּדוּן בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין אֲרוּם יוֹמָא דְכִפּוּרַיָא הוּא לְכַפָּרָא עֲלֵיכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


and do no work on this same day; for it is the Day of Atonement, to make atonement for you before the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:29)


אֲרוּם כָּל בַּר נַשׁ דְיֵיכוֹל לְצַיְימָא וְלָא יְצוּם בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בְּמוֹתָנָא מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


For every man who eateth in the fast, and will not fast that same day, shall be cut off by death from among his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:30)


וְכָל בַּר נַשׁ דְיַעֲבֵיד כָּל עִיבִידָא בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין וְאוֹבִיד יַת בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא בְּמוֹתָנָא מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


And every man who doeth any work on that same day, that man will I destroy with death from among his people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:31)


כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן בְּכָל אֲתַר מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן


No work of labour may you do an everlasting statute for your generations, in all your dwellings.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:32)


שַׁבָּא וְנַיְחָא הוּא לְכוֹן וּתְעַנוּן יַת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן וְתִשְׁרוּן לְצַיְימָא בְּתִשְׁעָה יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא בְּעִידוֹנֵי רַמְשָׁא מִן רַמְשָׁא הַהוּא עַד רַמְשָׁא חוֹרַן תֶּהֱווּן צַיְימִין צוּמְכוֹן וְשַׁבְּתוּן שׁוֹבֵיכוֹן וְעָבְדִין זִמְנִין מוֹעֲדֵיכוֹן בְּחֶדְוָא


It is a Sabbath and time of leisure for you to humble your souls. And you shall begin to fast at the ninth day of the month at even time; from that evening, until the next evening, shall you fast your fast, and repose in your quietude, that you may employ the time of your festivals with joy.

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:34)
