Speak with the sons of Israel: In the fifteenth day of this seventh month shall be the Feast of Tabernacles, seven days unto the Name of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:35)


בְּיוֹמָא קַדְמָאָה דְחַגָא מְאָרַע קַדִישׁ כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן


On the first day of the feast is a holy convocation; no work of labour may you do.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:36)


שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין תְּקַרְבוּן קוּרְבָּנָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ כְּנִישִׁין תֶּהֱווֹן לְצַלָאָה קֳדָם יְיָ עַל מִטְרָא כָּל עִיבִידַת פּוּלְחָנָא לָא תַעַבְדוּן בְּיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה מְאַרְעֵי קַדִישׁ יְהֵי לְכוֹן וְתִקְרְבוּן קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ


Seven days you shall offer an oblation to the Name of the Lord, you shall gather together to pray before the Lord for rain; no work of labour may you do.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:37)


אִילֵין זִמְנֵי סִדְרֵי מוֹעֲדַיָא דַיְיָ דִתְעַרְעוּן יַתְהוֹן מְאַרְעֵי קַדִישׁ לְקָרָבָא קוּרְבָּנָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ עֲלָתָא וּמִנְחָתָא נִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין וְנִיסוּכִין פִּתְגַם יוֹם בְּיוֹמֵיהּ


These are the times of the order of the Lord's festivals which you are to convoke for holy convocations, to offer an oblation to the name of the Lord, a burnt sacrifice and a mincha, sanctified offerings and libations, the rite of a day in its day;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:38)


בַּר מִן יוֹמֵי שַׁבַּיָא דַיְיָ בַּר מִמַתְּנוֹתֵיכוֹן וּבַר מִנִדְרֵיכוֹן וּבַר מִכָּל נְסִיבְתֵּיכוֹן דְתִתְּנוּן קֳדָם יְיָ


beside the days of the Lord's Sabbaths, beside your gifts, and beside your vows, and beside your free-will offering which you bring before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:39)


בְּרַם בַּחֲמֵיסַר יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה בִּזְמַן מִכְנַשְׁכוֹן יַת עֲלַלְתָּא דְאַרְעָא תְּחַגוּן יַת חַגָא דַיְיָ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין בְּיוֹמָא קַמָאָה נַיְיחָא וּבְיוֹם תְּמִינָאָה נַיְיחָא


But on the fifteenth of the seventh month, at the time when you collect the produce of the ground, you shall solemnize a festival of the Lord seven days. On the first day, rest; and on the eighth day, rest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:40)


וְתִסְבּוּן מִן דִלְכוֹן בְּיוֹמָא קַמָאָה דְחַגָא פֵּירֵי אִילַן מְשֻׁבַּח תְּרוּגִין וְלוּלַבִין וְהַדַסִין וְעַרְבִין דְמַרְבְּיַין עַל נַחֲלִין וְתֵיחְדוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין


And of your own shall you take on the first day of the feast, the fruits of praiseworthy trees, citrons, and lulabin, and myrtles, and willows that grow by the brooks; and you shall rejoice before the Lord your God seven days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:41)


וּתְחַגוּן יָתֵיהּ חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין בְּשַׁתָּא קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן בְּיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה תְּחַגוּן יָתֵיהּ


And you shall solemnize it before the Lord seven days in the year, by an everlasting statute in your generations shall you observe it in the seventh month.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:42)


בִּמְטַלַלְתָּ דִתְרֵי דוֹפְנַיָיהָא כְּהִלְכַתְהוֹן וּתְלִיתַיָיא עַד פּוּשְׁכָא וְטוּלָהּ סַגִי מִשִׁימְשָׁהּ מִתְעַבְדָא לְטוּלָא לְשׁוּם חַגָא מִן זַיינִין דְמַרְבְּיַין מִן אַרְעָא וּתְלִישִׁין מְשַׁחֲתָהָא עַד שַׁבְעָא פּוּשְׁכֵי וַחֲלָלָא דְרוֹמָא עַשְרָא פּוּשְׁכֵי תֵּיתְבוּן בָּהּ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין כָּל דְכוּרַיָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל וַאֲפִילוּ זְעִירֵי דְלָא צְרִיכִין לְאִימְהוֹן יֵתְבוּן בִּמְטָלַיָא מְבָרְכִין לְבַרְיֵיהוֹן כָּל אֵימַת דְעַיְילִין תַּמָן


In tabernacles of two sides according to their rule, and the third a handbreadth (higher), that its shaded part may be greater than that into which cometh the sunshine; to be made for a bower (or shade) for the feast, from different kinds (of materials) which spring from the earth and are uprooted: in measure seven palms, but the height within ten palms. In it you shall sit seven days; the males in Israel, and children who need not their mothers, shall sit in the tabernacles, blessing their Creator whenever they enter thereinto.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 23:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/23:43)


מִן בִּגְלַל דְיֵדְעוּן דָרֵיכוֹן אֲרוּם בְּמַטְלַת עֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא אוֹתֵיבִית יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בִּזְמַן דְהַנְפֵּיקִית יַתְהוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֳלָהָכוֹן


That your generations may know how, under the shadow of the cloud of glory, I made the sons of Israel to dwell at the time that I brought them out redeemed from the land of Mizraim.

And Mosheh declared the time of the orders of the Lord's festivals, and taught them to the sons of Israel.

Command the children of Israel that they bring of their own, pure beaten olive oil for the light, that the lamps may burn continually, on the day of Sabbath, and on the day of work;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:3)


מִבָּרָא לְפַרְגוֹדָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא לְעַלְמָא דִשְׁכִינְתָּא שַׁרְיָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא יְסַדֵר יָתֵיהּ אַהֲרן מֵרַמְשָׁא וְעַד צַפְרָא קֳדָם יְיָ תְּדִירָא קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן


outside of the veil of the testimony for evermore, because the Shekinah dwelleth in Israel: in the tabernacle of ordinance shall Aharon order it from evening till morning before the Lord continually, by an everlasting statute unto your generations.

וּתְסַדֵר יַתְהוֹן תַּרְתֵּין סִדוּרִין שִׁית בְּסִדְרָא חֲדָא וְשִׁית בְּסִדְרָא חֲדָא עַל פְּתוֹרָא בִּטְהֲרֵיהּ דִמְסַדֵר קֳדָם יְיָ


And thou shalt set them in two orders (rows), six in one order, and six in the other upon the table in its purity, as it is ordained before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:7)


וְתִתֵּן עַל סִידוּרַיָא צְרִיד לְבוֹנְתָּא בְּרִירְתָּא וּתְהֵי לְלַחֲמָא לְאַדְכָּרָא קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And thou shalt put upon the orders pure frankincense, that it may be an oblation of memorial bread before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:8)


בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָּא בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָּא יְסַדְרִינֵיהּ חֲדַת קֳדָם יְיָ תְּדִירָא מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל קְיַים עֲלָם


From Sabbath day to Sabbath day he shall order it anew before the Lord continually from the children of Israel. This shall be an everlasting statute.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:9)


וּתְהֵי לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי וְיֵכְלוּנֵיהּ מִן בָּתַר אִסְתַּלְקוּתֵיהּ מֵעַל פָּתוֹרָא בַּאֲתַר קַדִישׁ אֲרוּם קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא לֵיהּ מִקוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ קְיַים עֲלָם


And it shall be for Aharon and for his sons, and they shall eat it after they have taken it from off the table in the holy place; for it is most sacred to him of the oblations of the Lord by an everlasting statute.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:10)


גַבְרָא חַיָיבָא מְרוֹד בֶּאֱלָהָא שְׁמַיָא נְפַק מִמִצְרַיִם בַּר גַבְרָא מִצְרָאָה דְקָטַל גַבְרָא בַּר יִשְרָאֵל בְּמִצְרַיִם וְעָאַל עַל אִנְתְּתֵיהּ וְאִתְעַבָּרַת וִילֵידַת בַּר בְּגוֹ בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְכַד הֲווֹן יִשְרָאֵל שַׁרְיָין בְּמַדְבְּרָא בָּעָא לְמִפְרוֹס מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ בְּגוֹ שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי דָן וְלָא שַׁבְקוּהָ מִן בִּגְלַל דְטִיכְסִין דְיִשְרָאֵל גְבַר עַל טִיכְסֵיהּ בְּאַתְוָון לְיֵיחוּס אַבָהַתְהוֹן שַׁרְיָין וְאִתְקוֹטְטוּ כַּחֲדָא בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא וַאֲזָלוּ לְבֵי דִינָא בַּר אִתְּתָא בַּת יִשְרָאֵל וְגַבְרָא בַּר יִשְרָאֵל דְמִן שִׁבְטָא דְדָן


But a wicked man, a rebel against the God of heaven, had come out of Mizraim, the son of the Mizraite man who had killed the man of Israel in Mizraim, and had gone in unto his wife, who conceived and bare a son among the children of Israel. And while the Israelites were dwelling in the wilderness, he had sought to spread his tent in the midst of the tribe of the children of Dan; but they would not permit him, because in the arrangements of Israel every man dwelt with his family by the ensigns of the house of their fathers. And they contended together in the camp, and the son of the Israelitess with a man of Israel, who was of the tribe of Dan, went to the house of judgment;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:11)


וְכַד נָפַק מִבֵּי דִינָא כַּד מְחַיֵיב פָּרֵישׁ וְחָרִיף בַּר אִתְּתָא בַּת יִשְרָאֵל יַת שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְיַקִירָא דְמִתְפָּרֵשׁ דְשָׁמַע בְּסִינַי וְאָזִיד וְאַרְגִיז וְשׁוּם אִמֵיהּ שְׁלוֹמִית בַּת דִבְרִי לְשִׁבְטָא דְדָן


and when they had come out from the house of judgment, where he had been condemned, the son of the daughter of Israel expressed and reviled the great and glorious Name of Manifestation which he had heard at Sinai, and defiled and execrated; and the name of his mother was Shelomith, the daughter of Dibree, of the tribe of Dan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/24:12)


דֵין הוּא חַד מִן אַרְבַּעְתֵּי דִינִין דִי עֲלוּ קֳדָם משֶׁה נְבִיָיא וְדָן יַתְהוֹן עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דִלְעֵיל מִנְהוֹן דִינָא מָמוֹנָא וּמִנְהוֹן דִינָא נַפְשָׁתָא בְּדִינֵי מָמוֹנָא הֲוָה משֶׁה זָרִיז וּבְדִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא הֲוָה מָתוּן וּבְאִילֵין אָמַר משֶׁה לָא שְׁמָעִית מִן בִּגְלַל לְמֵילְפָא רֵישֵׁי סַנְהֶדְרַיְיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיקוּם בַּתְרֵיהּ דֶיֶהֱווֹן זְרִיזִין בְּדִינֵי מָמוֹנָא וּמְתוּנִין בְּדִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא וְלָא יְבַהֲתוּן לְמִישְׁאֲלָא דִינָא דְמִתְקַשֵׁי לְהוֹן אֲרוּם משֶׁה רַבְּהוֹן דְיִשְרָאֵל צָרִיךְ דְיֵימַר לָא שְׁמָעִית בְּגִין כֵּן אַצְנָעוּ יָתֵיהּ בְּבֵית מַטְרָא עַד זְמַן דְיִתְפָּרֵשׁ לְהוֹן עַל גְזֵירַת מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ


This is one of four judgments which were brought in before Mosheh the prophet, who decided them by the dictate of the Word, who is above. They were judgments about money and about life. In judgments on money Mosheh was prompt; but in the judgment on life he was deliberate (or slow by delay) each (party) Mosheh said, I have not heard: that he might teach the chiefs of the Sanhedrin of Israel, who were to arise after him, to be prompt in judgments respecting money, but slow in judgments that affected life; and not to be ashamed to inquire for counsel in cases that should be too hard for them, forasmuch as Mosheh, Rabban of Israel, had need to say, I have not heard. Therefore they shut him up in the house of confinement till the time that it should be explained to them by the decree of the Word of the Lord.

Bring forth the blasphemer without the camp, and let the witnesses who heard his blasphemy, and the judges, lay their hands upon his head, and let the whole congregation stone him with stones.

בְּרַם מַאן דִמְפָרֵשׁ וּמְחַרֵף שְׁמָא דַיְיָ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל אַטְלָא יְאַטְלוּן יָתֵיהּ אַבְנִין כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא כְּגִיוֹרָא כְּיַצִיבָא בִּזְמַן דְיֶחֱרַף שְׁמָא דִמְיַיחֵד יִתְקְטֵיל


Whosoever expresseth and revileth the Name of the Lord shall verily be put to death; all the congregation shall cast stones upon him, whether he be a sojourner or native-born, when he hath blasphemed the Name that is Alone, he shall die.

And a man who inflicteth a blemish on his neighbour, whatsoever he hath done it shall be done unto him:

the value of a fracture for a fracture; the value of an eye for an eye; the value of a tooth for a tooth; whatsoever blemish he inflicteth upon the man, the same shall be rendered unto him.

And Mosheh spake with the sons of Israel, and they brought forth the blasphemer without the camp, and stoned him with stones; and the sons of Israel did it, by laying their hands upon, leading him away hanging, and burying him, as the Lord had commanded Mosheh.

AND the Lord spake with Mosheh in the mountain of Sinai, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם תֵּיעָלוּן לְאַרְעָא דַאֲנָא יָהֵיב לְכוֹן וְתַשְׁמֵט אַרְעָא שְׁמִיטְתָא קֳדָם יְיָ


Speak with the children of Israel, and say to them: When you have entered into the land that I will give to you, then shall the ground rest for a rest before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:3)


שִׁית שְׁנִין תִּזְרְעוּן חַקְלֵיכוֹן וְשִׁית שְׁנִין תִּנְזְרוּן כַּרְמֵיכוֹן וְתִכְנְשׁוּן יַת עֲלַלְתָּהּ


Six years you shall sow your fields, and six years prune your vineyards and gather in the fruit;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:4)


וּבְשַׁתָּא שְׁבִיעֲתָא נְיַיח דִשְׁמִיטְתָא יְהֵי לְאַרְעָא דִי תַשְׁמֵט קֳדָם יְיָ חַקְלֵיכוֹן לָא תִזְרְעוּן וְכַרְמֵיכוֹן לָא תִנְזְרוּן


but in the seventh year there shall be a rest of remission to the earth, that she may rest before the Lord; you shall not sow your fields, nor prune your vineyards.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:31)


וּבָתֵּי כּוּפְרָנַיָא דְלֵית לְהוֹן שׁוּר מַקַף חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר הֵי כְטִינְדִיסִין דִפְרִיסִין עַל חֲקַל אַרְעָא יִתְחַשְׁבוּן פּוּרְקָנָא תְּהֵי לְהוֹן וּבְיוֹבְלָא יִפְקוּן


But houses in villages which have no walls round about them, are to be accounted as tents which are spread upon the fields of the earth; they may be redeemed, and they shall go out at the Jubela.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:32)


וְקִרְוֵי לֵיוָאֵי בָּתֵּי קִירְוֵי אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן פּוּרְקַן עֲלַם יְהֵי לְלֵיוָאֵי


But the cities of the Levites, the houses of the cities of their possession may be always redeemable by the Levites.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:33)


וּמַאן דְיִפְרוֹק מִן לֵיוָאֵי וְיִפּוֹק זְבִינֵי בֵּיתָא וְקִירְוֵי אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן בְּיוֹבְלָא אֲרוּם בָּתֵּי קִירְוֵי לֵיוָאֵי הִיא אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And when one hath purchased of the Levites the house that was sold in the cities of their possession, it shall go out at the Jubela; for the houses of the Levites are their inheritance among the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:34)


וּבְרַם חֲקִיל פַּרְוִילִי קִירְוֵיהוֹן לָא יְזַבְּנוּן אֲרוּם אַחְסָנַת עֲלַם הוּא לְהוֹן


But a field in the suburbs of their cities shall not be sold for it is an everlasting possession for them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:35)


וַאֲרוּם יִתְמַסְכַּן אָחוּךְ וּתְמוּט יְדֵיהּ עִמָךְ וְתַתְקִיף בֵּיהּ וּתְהַנְיֵיהּ יִדוֹר וְתוֹתָב וְיִתְפַּרְנֵס עִמָךְ


And if thy brother hath become poor, and his hand wavereth with thee, then thou shalt strengthen and do him good, as a guest and a sojourner he shall be nourished with thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:36)


עַמִי בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל לָא תִיסְבוּן מִנֵיהּ לָא שַׁעֲרִין וְלָא רִיבְיַין וְתִדְחַל מֵאֱלָהָךְ וְיִתְפַּרְנֵס אָחוּךְ עִמָךְ


My people of the house of Israel you shall not take usuries or remunerations (in his case) but thou shalt fear thy God, and let thy brother have nourishment with thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:37)


עַמִי בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת כַּסְפֵּיכוֹן לָא תִתְּנוּן לֵיהּ בְּשַׁעַרְיָין וּבְרִיבְיָין לָא תִתְּנוּן עִבוּרֵיכוֹן


My people of the house of Israel, you shall not lend him for usury, nor give him your provisions for increase.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:38)


אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִי פְרִיקִית וַאֲפֵיקִית יַתְכוֹן פְרִיקִין מִן אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מְטוֹל לְמִתַּן לְכוֹן יַת אַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן לְמֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן לֶאֱלָהָא


I am the Lord your God, who redeemed and brought you out redeemed from the land of Mizraim, to give unto you the land of Kenaan, and to be unto you Eloha.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:39)


וַאֲרוּם יִתְמַסְכֵּן אָחוּךְ עִמָךְ וְיִזְדַבֵּן לָךְ לָא תִפְלַח בֵּיהּ הֵי כְּנִימוּסֵי פוּלְחָנוּת עַבְדַיָא


If thy brother with thee shall have become poor, and have sold himself unto thee, thou shalt not make him serve according to the laws of the service of bondmen


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:40)


הֵי כְּאַגִירָא הֵי כְּתוֹתָבָא יְהֵי עִמָךְ עַד שַׁתָּא דְיוֹבְלָא יִפְלַח עִמָךְ


but as a hired man and as a sojourner shall he serve with thee, until the year of Jubela shall he serve thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:41)


וְיִפּוֹק לְבַר חוֹרִין מֵעִמָךְ הוּא וּבְנוֹי עִמֵיהּ וִיתֵיב לִגְנִיסָתֵיהּ וּלְאַחֲסָנַת אַבְהָתוֹי יְתוּב


Then shall he go out from thee at liberty, he and his children with him, and return to his family, and to the heritage of his fathers shall he return.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:42)


אֲרוּם עֲבָדַי הִינוּן דְהַנְפֵּיקִית יַתְהוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לָא יִזְדַבְּנוּן הֵי כְנִימוּסֵי זְבִין עַבְדַיָא


For they are My servants whom I brought forth redeemed from the land of Mizraim; they shall not be sold according to the laws of the sale of bondmen.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:43)

Neither may you make him serve with rigour, but thou shalt fear the Lord thy God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:44)


בְּרַם עַבְדֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהָתֵיכוֹן דִיהוֹן לְכוֹן מִן אַמְהַתְכוֹן דְמִן עַמְמַיָא דִי בְּחַזְרָנוּתֵיכוֹן מִנְהוֹן תְּזַבְּנוּן עַבְדִין וְאַמְהָן


But your bondmen and your handmaids which you may have, of the handmaids of the Gentiles, of them you may purchase bondmen and handmaids.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:45)


וְאוּף מִבְּנֵי תּוּתְבַיָא עֲרֵלַיָא דְדַיְירִין עִמְכוֹן מִנְהוֹן תְּזַבְּנוּן וּמִן יִחוּסֵיהוֹן דְעִמְכוֹן דְאִתְיְלִידוּ בְּאַרְעֲכוֹן וְלָא מִן כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וִיהוֹן לְכוֹן לְאַחֲסָנָא


Moreover, of the children of the uncircumcised strangers who sojourn among you, of them you may buy, and of their families that are with you, which they have begotten in your land; but not from the Kenaanaee; and they shall be yours for possession.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:46)


וְתַחְסְנוּן יַתְהוֹן לִבְנֵיכוֹן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן לִירוּתַת אַחֲסָנָא לַעֲלַם בְּהוֹן תִּפְלְחוּן וּבְאֲחוּכוֹן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל גְבַר בְּחַבְרֵיהּ לָא תְשַׁעֲבְּדוּן בְּהוֹן בְּקַשְׁיוּ


And you may leave them to your children after you, to inherit as a perpetual possession; them you shall make to serve: but of your brethren of the sons of Israel no man (may enslave) his fellow; them shall you not make to serve with rigour.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:47)


וַאֲרוּם תְּאַרַע יַד עָרֵל וְתּוֹתַב דְעִמָךְ וְיִתְמַסְכֵּן אָחוּךְ עִמֵיהּ וְיִזְדַבֵּן לְעָרֵל תּוֹתָב דְעִמָךְ אוֹ לִשְׁרִישׁ פּוּלְחָנָא נוּכְרָאָה לִמְשַׁמְשָׁא לָהּ וּלְפַלְחָהָא דְמִזַרְעִית גִיוֹרָא


And if the hand of the uncircumcised sojourner with you wax strong, and thy brother with him become poor, and sell himself to the uncircumcised stranger who is with thee, or to the stock of a strange religion, to serve him or to worship with him who is of the generation of strangers;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:48)


בָּתַר דְיִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע לְכוֹן דְיִזְדַבֵּן מִן יַד פּוּרְקָנָא יְהֵי לֵיהּ חַד מִן אָחוֹי יִפְרְקִינֵיהּ


when it is known to you that he hath been sold, forthwith redemption shall, be his; one of his brethren shall redeem him;


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:49)


אוֹ אַחְבוֹי אוֹ בַּר אַחְבוֹי יִפְרְקִינֵיהּ אוֹ מִקָרִיב בִּישְרֵיהּ מִזַרְעִיתֵיהּ יִפְרְקִינֵיהּ אוֹ תְּאַרַע יְדֵיהּ אוֹ יְדָא דְצִבּוּרָא וְיִתְפָּרִיק


either the brother of his father or the son of his father's brother may redeem him; or a kinsman of the flesh of his family may redeem him; or by his own hand being made strong, or by the hand of the congregation, he may be redeemed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:50)


וִידַיֵיק עִם עַרְלָאָה דְזַבְּנֵיהּ מִשַׁתָּא דְיִזְדַבֵּן לֵיהּ עַד שַׁתָּא דְיוֹבְלָא וִיהֵי כְסַף זַבִּינוֹ כְּמִנְיַן שְׁנַיָא הֵי כְיוֹמֵי אֲגִירָא יְהֵי עִמֵיהּ


And he shall account with the uncircumcised person who had bought him, from the year that he was sold until the year of Jubela, and the price of his repurchase shall be according to the number of the years; according to the days of an hireling shall it be with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:51)


אִין עַד כְּדוּן אִית סַגִי בִּשְׁנַיָא לְפוּם סְמוּכְהוֹן יָתִיב פּוּרְקָנֵיהּ מִכְּסַף זְבִינוֹי


If yet there be many years, according to their number he shall give for his redemption of the money that he was bought for.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:52)


וְאִם קָלִיל יִשְׁתַּיְירוּן בִּשְׁנַיָא עַד שַׁתָּא דְיוֹבְלָא וִיחַשֵׁב לֵיהּ כְּפוּם סְכוּם שְׁנוֹי יָתֵיב יַת פּוּרְקָנֵיהּ


But if few years remain till the year of Jubela, he shall compute with him, and according to the amount of the years give for his redemption.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:53)


הֵי כְּאָגִיר שְׁנָא בִּשְׁנָא יְהֵי עִמֵיהּ לָא יְשַׁעְבֵּיד בֵּיהּ בְּקַשְׁיוּ וְאַנְתְּ חָמֵי לֵיהּ


As a hireling by the year shall he be with him, and his master shall not make him to serve with rigour while thou see it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:54)


וְאִין לָא יִתְפְּרַק בְּאִילֵין שְׁנַיָא וְיִפּוֹק בַר חוֹרִין בְּשַׁתָּא דְיוֹבְלָא הוּא וּבְנוֹי עִמֵיהּ


But if he be not redeemed within those years, he shall go out free at the year of Jubela, he and his children with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 25:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/25:55)


אֲרוּם דִילִי הִינוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדוּן לְאוֹרַיְיתָא עַבְדַי הִינוּן דְאַפֵּיקִית יַתְהוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


For the sons of Israel are Mine, to obey My laws; servants are they whom I brought out redeemed from the land of Mizraim. I am the Lord your God.



## Chapter 26

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:1)


לָא תַעַבְדוּן לְכוֹן טַעֲוָן וְצִילְמִין וְקַמְתִּין מְטוֹל סְגוֹדָא לָא תְקוּמוּן לְכוֹן וְאֶבֶן מְצַיָיר לָא תִתְּנוּן בְּאַרְעֲכוֹן לְמִגְחַן עֲלָהּ בְּרַם סַטְיוּ חָקִיק בְּצִיוּרִין וּדְיוֹקְנִין תְשַׁווּן בְּאַרְעִית מַקְדָשֵׁיכוֹן וְלָא לְמִסְגוֹד לָהּ אֲרוּם אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


You shall not make to you idols or images, nor erect for you statues to worship, neither a figured stone shall ye place in your land to bow yourselves toward it. Nevertheless a pavement sculptured with imagery you may set on the spot of your sanctuary, but not to worship it: I am the Lord your God.

וְאַיְיתֵי עֲלֵיכוֹן עָם שְׁלוּפֵי חֶרֶב לְמִתְפְּרַע מִנְכוֹן עַל דִבְטַלְתּוּן יַת קֳיָימַי וְתִתְכַּנְשׁוּן מִן צְיָירָא לְקוּרְיֵיכוֹן וְאִיגְרֵי מוֹתָנָא בֵּינֵיכוֹן וְתִתְמַסְרוּן כַּד מִיתִין בְּיַד סַנְאֵיכוֹן


And I will bring against you a people unsheathing the sword to take vengenance upon you, for that you will have abolished My covenant; and when you are gathered together from the wilderness into your cities, I will send the pestilence among you, or deliver you to die by the hand of your adversaries.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:26)


כַּד אֶתְבּוֹר לְכוֹן חוֹטָר כָּל סַעֲדֵי מְזוֹנָא וְיֵיאַפְיַין עֲשַר נְשִׁין לַחְמֵיכוֹן בְּתַנוּרָא חַד מִדְהוּא זְעֵיר וּמְדַדְיָן וּמְפַלְגָן לְכוֹן כַּד מִינְפַל בְּמַתְקָלָא וְתֵיכְלוּן וְלָא תִשְבְּעוּן


And when I shall have broken for you the staff of all the subsistence of food, then ten women may bake your bread in one oven on account of its scarcity, and measure and divide it to you diminished in weight, and you will eat and not be satisfied.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:27)


וְאִין בַּהֲדָא תּוֹכַחְתָּא לָא תִשְׁמְעוּן לְאוּלְפַן אוֹרַייְתִי וּתְהַלְכוּן קֳדָמַי בְּעַרְאַי


But if by no one correction ye will hearken to the instruction of My law, but will walk perversely before Me,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:28)


וְאִידַבֵּר אוּף אֲנָא יַתְכוֹן בְּעַרְאַי בְּעַלְמָא וְאִירְדֵי יַתְכוֹן לְחוֹד אֲנָא שְׁבַע מְחָן עַל שְׁבַע עֲבֵירָן דִי חֲטֵיתוּן קֳדָמָי


I will also remember you adversely in the world, and will chastise you, even I, with seven plagues, for the seven transgressions with which ye have sinned before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:29)


וְתֵיכְלוּן בְּשַר בְּנֵיכוֹן וּבְשַר בְּנָתֵיכוֹן אָמַר משֶׁה נְבִיָא כַּמָה קַשְׁיָין הִינוּן חוֹבַיָא וּמָה מְרִירִין אִינוּן חַטְיָיאֵי דִי גָרְמוּ לְאַבְהָתָא לְמֵיכַל בְּשַר בְּנֵיהוֹן וּבְנָתֵיהוֹן עַל דְלָא נָטְרוּ מִצְוָתָא דְאוֹרַייְתָא


And ye shall eat the flesh of your sons, and the flesh of your daughters. Mosheh the prophet hath said, How heavy will have been the guilt, and how bitter those sins, that caused our fathers to eat the flesh of their sons, and the flesh of their daughters, because they kept not the commandments of the law!


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:30)


וְאֵישֵׁיצֵי יַת בָּמוֹתֵיכוֹן וְאִפְכַר יַת מְנַחֲשֵׁיכוֹן וּקְסָמֵיכוֹן וְאֶשַׁוֵי יַת פִּגְרֵיכוֹן רְמָאִין עַל פִּגְרֵי טַעֲוָותֵיכוֹן וְתִירְחֵק מֵימְרִי יַתְכוֹן


And I will destroy your high places, and overthrow your diviners and your enchanters, and your carcases will I cast away with the carcases of your idols, and My Word shall abhor you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:31)


וְאֶתֵּן יַת קוּרְיֵיכוֹן צַדְיָין וַאֲשַׁעְמֵם יַת מוּקְדָשֵׁיכוֹן וְלָא אֲקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא רֵיחַ קוּרְבָּנֵיכוֹן


And I will make your cities desert places, and desolate your sanctuary; nor will I receive with acceptance the odour of your oblations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:32)


וְאַצְדֵי לְחוֹד אֲנָא יַת אַרְעָא דְלָא יְהֵי עֲלָהּ נְיַיח רוּחָא וְיִצְדוּן הֵיכְדֵין עֲלָהּ סַנְאֵיכוֹן דְשַׁרְיָין בָּהּ


And I, even I, will lay your country waste, that the spirit of quietness may not be upon it; so that your enemies who will dwell in it shall be confounded.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:33)


וְיַתְכוֹן אַדְרֵי בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָא וְאַגְרֵי בַּתְרֵיכוֹן עַם שְׁלוּפֵי חֶרֶב וּתְהֵי אַרְעֲכוֹן צַדְיָיא וְקוּרְיֵיכוֹן יֶהֶוְויָין צַדִיאָן


And you will I disperse among the nations; for I will stir up against you a people who draw the sword, and your country shall be devastated, and your cities be solitary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:34)


הָא בְּכֵן תַּרְעֵי אַרְעָא יַת שְׁנֵי שְׁמִיטָהָא כָּל יוֹמִין דְהִיא צַדְיָא מִנְכוֹן וְאַתּוּן תֶּהֱווֹן מִיטַלְטְלִין בַּאֲרַע בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן בְּכֵן תִּתְנַיַיח אַרְעָא וְתִרְעֵי יַת שִׁזְבֵי שְׁמִיטָהָא


Behold, then shall the land enjoy the years of its Sabbaths all the days that it is forsaken of you, and you are wanderers in the land of your enemies.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:35)


כָּל יוֹמִין דְהִיא צַדְיָא מִנְכוֹן תִּתְנַיַיח הֵי כְּמָא דְלָא אִתְנַיְיחַת יַת שְׁנֵי שְׁמִיטֵיכוֹן כַּד הֲוֵיתוּן שַׁרְיָין עֲלָהּ


All the days that it is forsaken by you it shall rest, because it was not at rest in the years of the times for repose when you were dwellers upon it.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:36)


וּדְיִשְׁתַּיְירוּן בְכוֹן וְאָעֵיל תַּבְרָא בְּלִבְּהוֹן בְּאַרְעֲתָא דְסַנְאֵיהוֹן וִיהֵי רָדִיף יַתְהוֹן קַל טַרְפָא דְנָתִיר מִן אִילַן וִיעַרְקוּן הֵי כְּמַעֲרוּקֵי חַרְבָּא וְיִפְּלוּן וְלֵית דְרָדִיף


And unto those of you who remain will I bring brokenness of their hearts in the land of their enemies; and the sound of a leaf falling from the tree shall put them to flight; and they shall flee as those who flee from the sword, and fall, while no man pursueth.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:37)


וִיתַקְלוּן גְבַר בְּאָחוֹי הֵי כְּמִן קֳדָם שְׁלוּפֵי חַרְבָּא וְרָדִיף לָא אִית וְלָא תְהֵי לְכוֹן תְּיָקָא לְמֵקוּם קֳדָם בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן


And they shall thrust each man his brother, as before them who draw the sword, though none pursue; and you shall have no power of resistance to stand before your adversaries.

וְתֵיבְדוּן בֵּין עַמְמַיָא וּתְגַמַר יַתְכוֹן בְּמוֹתָנָא אֲרַע בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן


And you shall perish among the nations, and be consumed with pestilence in the land of your enemies,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:39)


וּדְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר מִנְכוֹן יִתְיַמְסוּן בְּחוֹבֵיהוֹן בְּאַרְעֲתָא דְסַנְאֵיכוֹן וְאוּף בְּחוֹבֵי אַבְהַתְהוֹן בִּישַׁיָא דַאֲחִידִין בִּידֵיהוֹן עִמְהוֹן יִתְיַמְסוּן


And those who remain of you shall fail (melt away) for their sins in the land of your enemies, and also for the evil sin of your fathers which they held fast in their hands: like them shall they melt away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:40)


וִיוַדוּן בְּשַׁעַת אַנִיקְהוֹן יַת חוֹבֵיהוֹן וְיַת חוֹבֵי אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּשִׁקְרֵיהוֹן דְשַׁקְרוּ בְּמֵימְרִי וְאוּף דְהַלִיכוּ עִמִי בְּעַרְאַי


But (when) in the hour of their need they shall confess their sins, and the sins of their fathers, with their falseness with which they have acted falsely against My Word; and that they have acted frowardly also with Me,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:41)


בְּרַם אֲנָא אִידַבֵּר יַתְהוֹן בְּעַרַאי בְּעַלְמָא וְאָעֵיל יַתְהוֹן בְּגָלוּתָא בְּאַרַע בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיהוֹן הָא בְּכֵן יִתְבַּר לִבְּהוֹן זְדָנָא וּבְכֵן יַרְעוּן יַת חוֹבֵיהוֹן


so that I have remembered them adversely in the world, and brought them into captivity in the land of their enemies; behold, then will their proud heart have been broken, and they will make confession of their sins,


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:42)


וְאִדְכַּר בְּרַחֲמִין יַת קְיָימָא דְקַיֵימִית עִם יַעֲקב בְּבֵית אֵל וְאוּף יַת קְיָימָא דְקַיֵימִית עִם יִצְחָק בְּטַוָור מוֹרִיָה וְאוּף יַת קְיָימָא דְקַיֵימִית עִם אַבְרָהָם בֵּינֵי פִּיסוּגַיָא אֶדְכּוֹר וְאַרְעָא דְיִשְרָאֵל אֶדְכּוֹר בְּרַחֲמִין


and I will remember in mercy the covenant which I confirmed with Jakob at Bethel, and the covenant which I covenanted with Izhak at Mount Moriah, and the covenant which I covenanted with Abraham, between the divided portions, I will remember, and the land of Israel will I remember in mercy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:43)


וְאַרְעָא תִּתְרְטֵישׁ וְתִשְׁתְּבֵיק מִנְהוֹן וְתַרְעֵי יַת שׁוֹבֵי שְׁמִיטָהָא כָּל יוֹמִין דְהִיא צַדְיָא מִנְכוֹן וְהִינוּן יַרְעוּן יַת חוֹבֵיהוֹן לְוָוטִין חֲלַף בִּירְכָן יִמְטוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן מֵיכְלָא כָּל קֳבֵיל מֵיכְלָא הוּא מְטוּל דְהִינוּן בְּסִידְרֵי דִינַי קָצוּ וְיַת קְיָימֵי אוֹרַיְיתִי רְחִיקַת נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן


But the land shall (first) be relinquished and forsaken you; and enjoy the repose of her remissions all the days that it shall be deserted by you. And they will receive retribution for their sins: curses instead of blessings will come upon them, measure for measure: because they shunned the orders of judgments, and their souls revolted from the covenant of My law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:44)


וְאוּף עַל כָּל דָא אֲרַחִים יַתְהוֹן בְּמֵימְרִי כַּד יֶהֶוְויָין גַלְיָין בְּאַרַע בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיהוֹן לָא אֶמְאָסִינוּן בְּמַלְכוּתָא דְבָבֶל וְלָא יִרְחַק מֵימְרִי יַתְהוֹן בְּמַלְכוּתָא דְמָדַי לִמְשֵׁיצַיָא יַתְהוֹן בְּמַלְכוּתָא דְיָוָן לְמִפְסוֹק קְיָימִי עִמְהוֹן בְּמַלְכוּתָא דֶאֱדוֹם אֲרוּם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּיוֹמוֹי דְגוֹג


Yet for all this I will have mercy upon them by My Word, when they are captives in the land of their enemies, I will not spurn them away in the kingdom of Babel; nor shall My Word abhor them. in the kingdom of Madai, to destroy them in the kingdom of Javan, or to abolish My covenant with them in the kingdom of Edom; for I am the Lord in the days of Gog.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:45)


וּדְכִירְנָא לְהוֹן קְיַים דְקַיֵימִית עִם אֲבָהַתְהוֹן קֳדָמַי בִּזְמַן דִפְרִיקִית וְאַפֵּיקִית יַתְהוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְחַמְיַין כָּל עַמְמַיָא יַת כָּל גְּבוּרְתָּא דַעֲבָדִית לְהוֹן מְטוֹל לְמֶהֱוֵי לְהוֹן לֶאֱלָהָא אֲנָא יְיָ


And I will remember with them the covenant which I confirmed before Me with their fathers in the time that I brought them out redeemed from the land of Mizraim; when all the nations beheld all the mighty acts which I wrought for them, that I might be their God; I, the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 26:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/26:46)


אִלֵין קְיָימַיָא וְסִדְרֵי דִינַיָיא וּגְזֵירַת אוֹרַיְיתָא דִיהַב יְיָ בֵּין מֵימְרֵיהּ וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּטַוְורָא דְסִינַי עַל יְדָא דְמשֶׁה


These are the statutes and the orders of the judgments and decrees of the law, which the Lord appointed between His Word and the sons of Israel, in the mountain of Sinai, by the hand of Mosheh.

Speak with the children of Israel, and say to them: When a man willeth to set apart the separation of a vow, in the valuing of the life unto the name of the Lord,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:3)


וִיהֵי עִלוֹיֵיהּ דְבִיר דְכַר מִן בַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וְעַד בַּר שִׁתִּין שְׁנִין וִיהֵי עֲלוֹיֵיהּ חַמְשִׁין סִילְעִין דִכְסַף בְּסִלְעֵי קוּדְשָׁא


then his valuation for a male from twenty years old unto sixty years, fifty shekels, in the shekel of the sanctuary, shall be his valuation:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:4)

but if for a female, his valuation shall be thirty shekels.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:5)

And if it be a child from five years until twenty years, his estimation for a male shall be twenty shekels; and for a female, ten shekels.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:6)

But for a child of a month old until five years, his estimation for a male shall be five silver shekels; and for a female, three silver shekels.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:7)

Moreover, for a man of sixty years and upwards, his estimation shall be fifteen shekels; and for a female, ten shekels.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:8)


וְאִין מִסְכֵּן הוּא מִמֵסַת עֲלוֹיֵיהּ וְיוֹקִמִינֵיהּ קֳדָם כַּהֲנָא וְיַעֲלִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא כְּמֵיסַת דְתַדְבֵּיק יַד נוֹדִירָא הֵיכְדֵין יַעֲלִינֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא


But if he be too poor for (such) a rate of his estimation, he shall stand before the priest; and the priest shall make an estimation for him, according to the ability of his hand, so shall the priest estimate for him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:9)

And if it be an animal, of such as are offered as an oblation before the Lord, whatever he giveth of it before the Lord shall be sacred.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:10)

He shall not alter it nor change it, that which is perfect for that which hath blemish, or that in which there is blemish for the perfect: but if by changing he will exchange animal for animal, both that and the one that is changed shall be consecrate.

But if he would redeem it, let him add a fifth of its price upon that of its valuation.

But if he will dedicate his field after the year of Jubela, the priest shall compute with him the sum of the money according to the proportion of years that remain unto the next Jubela year, and shall abate it from the valuation.

###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:28)


בְּרַם כָּל אַפְרָשָׁא דִי יַפְרֵישׁ גְבַר קֳדָם יְיָ מִן כָּל דְאִית לֵיהּ מֵאֱנָשָׁא וּבְעִירָא וּמֵחֲקַל אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ לָא יִזְדַבֵּן וְלָא יִתְפְּרֵק כָּל אַפְרָשָׁא קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ


Nevertheless, no devoted thing which a man shall separate before the Lord of anything that is his, of man, or beast, or of his inheritance, shall be sold or redeemed; every (devoted) separation is most sacred before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:29)


כָּל אַפְרָשָׁא דִי יִתְפְּרַשׁ מִן אֵינָשָׁא לָא יִתְפְּרֵק בְּכַסְפָּא אֱלָהִין בְּעִלְוַון וּבְנִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין וּבְמִבְעֵי רַחֲמִין קֳדָם יְיָ מְטוּל דְדֵין קְטוֹל מִתְחַיֵיב


Every separation which shall be separated of man shall not be redeemed with money, but with burnt offerings, and with sanctified victims, and with supplication for mercy before the Lord, because such are to be put to death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:30)


וְכָל מַעַשְרָא דְאַרְעָא מִזַרְעָא דְאַרְעָא וּמִפֵּירֵי אִילָנָא דַיְיָ הוּא קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ


And all the tythe of the land, of the seed of the ground, or the fruits of the tree, is the Lord's, and is most sacred before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:31)


וְאִין מִפְרוֹק יִפְרוֹק גְבַר מִמַעַשְרֵיהּ חוֹמֶשׁ דְמוֹי יוֹסֵף עֲלוֹי


But if a man will redeem any (part) of his tythes, he shall add a fifth part of its value thereunto.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:32)


וְכָל מַעַשְרָא דְתוֹרֵי וְעָנֵי כָּל דְחַלְפוּן תְּחוֹת שַׁרְבִיטָא עֲשִירָאָה יְהֵי קוּדְשָׁא קֳדָם יְיָ


And every tythe of oxen and sheep, whatever passeth under the (tything) rod, the tenth shall be consecrated before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:33)


לָא יְפַשְׁפֵשׁ בֵּין טַב לְבִישׁ וְלָא יְפַרְגִינֵיהּ וְאִין מִפְרוֹג יְפַרְגִינֵיהּ וִיהֵי הוּא וּפֵירוּגֵיהּ יְהֵי קוּדְשָׁא לָא יִתְפְּרוֹק


He shall not scrutinize between the good and the bad, nor exchange it; but if changing he will exchange it, both it and that for which it is changed shall be sacred, and not be redeemed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Leviticus 27:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Leviticus/r/27:34)


אִילִין פִּקוּדַיָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה וְלֵית אֶפְשַׁר לְחַדְתָּא בְּהוֹן מִדַעַם וּפַקֵדִינוּן מְטוֹל לְאַחְוָואוּתְהוֹן לְוַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּטוּרָא דְסִינַי


These are the precepts which the Lord prescribed unto Mosheh, and of which not one must be trifled with (or, innovated upon); and He prescribed them to be shown Unto the children of Israel at Mount Sinai.
